            final_df = pd.concat([final_df[final_df.index < new_data.index.min()], new_data])

            final_df = final_df[~final_df.index.duplicated(keep='last')]

        else: final_df = new_data.copy()

        final_df.sort_index(inplace=True)

    if can_use_cache and final_df is not None and not final_df.empty and (needs_api_fetch or force_reload):

        _save_to_cache(final_df, cache_key, cache_dir_local)



    # --- 返回最終數據 (同 v1.1) ---

    if final_df is not None and not final_df.empty:

        # print(f"  [{function_name}] DEBUG: Final combined DataFrame has shape {final_df.shape} before date slicing.")

        final_df_cropped = final_df.loc[start_date_cfg:final_end_date_str]

        if not final_df_cropped.empty:

             print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}][{function_name}] Fetch for '{ticker}' completed. Returning {len(final_df_cropped)} rows.")

             return final_df_cropped

        else:

             print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}][{function_name}] Warning: Data became empty after slicing to requested date range ({start_date_cfg} to {final_end_date_str}).")

             return None

    else:

         print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}][{function_name}] Fetch failed or resulted in no data for {ticker}.")

         return None



print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] Cell 9: Fetcher function 'fetch_yf_hourly' [MultiIndex Fix v1.2] defined.")



"""### 10. 數據抓取器定義 - FRED (Fetcher Def - FRED)"""



# 程式碼儲存格 10: 數據抓取器定義 - FRED (Fetcher Def - FRED) [日誌中文化 v1.1]

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-



import pandas as pd

import numpy as np

from typing import Optional, Dict, Any, List

from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone # 確保導入 timezone

import traceback # 引入 traceback



# --- 假設依賴的函數和變數已在之前的儲存格定義 ---

# 需要 Cell 0 的: LIB_STATUS, taiwan_tz

# 需要 Cell 1 的: FRED_API_KEY (全局變數)

# 需要 Cell 2 的: _make_cache_key, _load_from_cache, _save_to_cache, CACHE_DIR_CELL2, PYARROW_AVAILABLE_CELL2



# 嘗試導入 Fred API 客戶端

try:

    if 'LIB_STATUS' in locals() and LIB_STATUS.get('fredapi', False):

        from fredapi import Fred

        FRED_CLIENT_AVAILABLE = True

    else:

        Fred = None

        FRED_CLIENT_AVAILABLE = False

except ImportError:

    Fred = None

    FRED_CLIENT_AVAILABLE = False

    # --- 修改：中文日誌 ---

    print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] 警告: 在 Cell 10 中導入 Fred (from fredapi) 失敗。")

except NameError:

     Fred = None

     FRED_CLIENT_AVAILABLE = False

     # --- 修改：中文日誌 ---

     print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] 警告: 未找到 LIB_STATUS。假設 fredapi 不可用。")





print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] Cell 10: Fetcher Definition - FRED [日誌中文化 v1.1]...")



# --- 內部輔助函數：抓取單個 FRED Series (包含快取邏輯) ---

def _fetch_single_fred_series_with_cache(

        series_id: str,

        api_key: Optional[str],

        start_date_req: Optional[str],

        end_date_req: str,

        force_reload: bool = False

    ) -> Optional[pd.Series]:

    """(內部輔助) 抓取單個 FRED 序列，帶快取和增量更新。"""



    # --- 日誌：庫/Key 狀態 ---

    if not FRED_CLIENT_AVAILABLE or Fred is None:

        if not api_key:

             # --- 修改：中文日誌 ---

             print(f"  -> 信息 (FRED Fetcher): fredapi 庫不可用且缺少 API Key for {series_id}。將嘗試讀取快取。")

        # else:

             # print(f"  -> 信息 (FRED Fetcher): fredapi 庫不可用 for {series_id}。將嘗試讀取快取。")

    # elif not api_key:

        # print(f"  -> 信息 (FRED Fetcher): 缺少 API Key for {series_id}。將嘗試讀取快取。")



    # 快取功能檢查

    can_use_cache = False

    cache_dir_local = None

    if all(f in globals() for f in ['_make_cache_key', '_load_from_cache', '_save_to_cache', 'CACHE_DIR_CELL2']) \

       and globals().get('PYARROW_AVAILABLE_CELL2', False) and globals().get('CACHE_DIR_CELL2'):

        can_use_cache = True

        cache_dir_local = globals().get('CACHE_DIR_CELL2')



    cache_key = "CACHE_DISABLED"

    if can_use_cache:

        cache_key = _make_cache_key("fred_series", series_id, start_date_req or "None", end_date_req)



    cached_series: Optional[pd.Series] = None

    fetch_start_date_for_api: Optional[str] = start_date_req



    # --- 讀取快取與增量更新判斷 ---

    if not force_reload and can_use_cache:

        cached_df_raw = _load_from_cache(cache_key, cache_dir_local)

        if cached_df_raw is not None and not cached_df_raw.empty:

            # ... (省略快取數據處理邏輯 - 保持與 v8 文件一致) ...

            # 假設處理成功，cached_series 被賦值，fetch_start_date_for_api 可能被更新

            target_col_name = series_id; temp_series = None

            try:

                df_to_process = cached_df_raw

                index_col_name = 'Date' if 'Date' in df_to_process.columns else ('index' if 'index' in df_to_process.columns else None)

                if index_col_name and index_col_name in df_to_process.columns and not isinstance(df_to_process.index, pd.DatetimeIndex):

                     df_to_process[index_col_name] = pd.to_datetime(df_to_process[index_col_name], errors='coerce')

                     df_to_process = df_to_process.dropna(subset=[index_col_name]).set_index(index_col_name)

                if isinstance(df_to_process.index, pd.DatetimeIndex):

                    if df_to_process.index.tz is not None: df_to_process.index = df_to_process.index.tz_localize(None)

                    df_to_process.index = df_to_process.index.normalize()

                    if target_col_name in df_to_process.columns:

                        temp_series = df_to_process[target_col_name].sort_index()

                        temp_series = temp_series[~temp_series.index.duplicated(keep='last')]

                if temp_series is not None and not temp_series.empty:

                    cached_series = temp_series

                    last_cached_dt = cached_series.index.max().date()

                    try: tz_local = globals().get('taiwan_tz', timezone.utc)

                    except NameError: tz_local = timezone.utc

                    today_dt = datetime.now(tz_local).date()

                    if last_cached_dt < today_dt:

                        fetch_start_date_obj = last_cached_dt + timedelta(days=1)

                        req_start_dt = pd.to_datetime(start_date_req).date() if start_date_req else None

                        if req_start_dt and fetch_start_date_obj < req_start_dt: fetch_start_date_obj = req_start_dt

                        fetch_start_date_for_api = fetch_start_date_obj.strftime('%Y-%m-%d')

                    else: fetch_start_date_for_api = None

                else: fetch_start_date_for_api = start_date_req

            except Exception as e_cache_proc:

                 # --- 修改：中文日誌 ---

                 print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] FRED 抓取器警告: 處理序列 {series_id} 的快取時出錯: {e_cache_proc}。將重新加載。")

                 cached_series = None; fetch_start_date_for_api = start_date_req

        # else: pass # 快取讀取失敗或為空



    # --- 判斷是否需要調用 API ---

    needs_api_fetch = True

    if cached_series is not None and not force_reload:

        if fetch_start_date_for_api is None: needs_api_fetch = False

        else:

            try:

                if pd.to_datetime(fetch_start_date_for_api).date() > pd.to_datetime(end_date_req).date(): needs_api_fetch = False

            except: pass

    elif force_reload: fetch_start_date_for_api = start_date_req; cached_series = None; needs_api_fetch = True



    should_call_api = needs_api_fetch and FRED_CLIENT_AVAILABLE and Fred is not None and api_key is not None



    # --- 調用 FRED API ---

    new_series: Optional[pd.Series] = None

    if should_call_api:

        # print(f"  -> DEBUG: Calling FRED API for {series_id} from {fetch_start_date_for_api or 'earliest'}...")

        try:

            fred_client = Fred(api_key=api_key)

            api_result = fred_client.get_series(series_id, observation_start=fetch_start_date_for_api)

            if api_result is not None and not api_result.empty:

                temp_api_series = api_result

                if isinstance(temp_api_series, pd.DataFrame): temp_api_series = temp_api_series.squeeze()

                if isinstance(temp_api_series, pd.Series):

                    if not isinstance(temp_api_series.index, pd.DatetimeIndex):

                        temp_api_series.index = pd.to_datetime(temp_api_series.index, errors='coerce')

                        temp_api_series = temp_api_series[temp_api_series.index.notna()]

                    if not temp_api_series.empty:

                         if temp_api_series.index.tz is not None: temp_api_series.index = temp_api_series.index.tz_localize(None)

                         temp_api_series.index = temp_api_series.index.normalize()

                         new_series = temp_api_series[~temp_api_series.index.duplicated(keep='last')].sort_index()

        except ValueError as ve:

             # --- 修改：中文日誌 ---

             print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] FRED 抓取器錯誤: API key 可能對 {series_id} 無效. {ve}")

        except Exception as e_fred_api:

             # --- 修改：中文日誌 ---

            print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] FRED 抓取器錯誤: 抓取 {series_id} 失敗: {e_fred_api}")



    # --- 合併快取與新數據 ---

    final_series: Optional[pd.Series] = None

    if cached_series is not None: final_series = cached_series.copy()

    if new_series is not None:

        if final_series is not None:

            final_series = pd.concat([final_series[final_series.index < new_series.index.min()], new_series])

            final_series = final_series[~final_series.index.duplicated(keep='last')].sort_index()

        else: final_series = new_series.copy()



    # --- 保存到快取 ---

    should_save_cache = can_use_cache and final_series is not None and not final_series.empty and \

                        (force_reload or (should_call_api and new_series is not None))

    if should_save_cache:

         _save_to_cache(final_series.to_frame(name=series_id), cache_key, cache_dir_local)



    return final_series





# --- 主抓取函數 ---

def fetch_fred_data(config: Dict[str, Any]) -> Optional[pd.DataFrame]:

    """

    (數據抓取器 Fetcher) 從 FRED 抓取多個經濟數據序列。

    [v1.1]: 日誌中文化。

    """

    series_ids = config.get('series_ids', [])

    start_date_cfg = config.get('start_date')

    end_date_cfg = config.get('end_date')

    force_reload = config.get('force_reload', False)



    if not series_ids or not isinstance(series_ids, list):

        # --- 修改：中文日誌 ---

        print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] FRED 抓取器錯誤: 配置中缺少 'series_ids' 列表或列表無效。")

        return None

    api_key = globals().get('FRED_API_KEY')



    # --- 處理日期 ---

    try: tz = globals().get('taiwan_tz', timezone.utc)

    except NameError: tz = timezone.utc

    today_str = datetime.now(tz).strftime('%Y-%m-%d')

    final_end_date_str = end_date_cfg if end_date_cfg else today_str

    final_start_date_str = start_date_cfg



    # --- 循環抓取每個 Series ---

    all_series_data = {}

    fetch_success_count = 0

    # --- 修改：中文日誌 ---

    print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] 正在從 FRED 抓取 {len(series_ids)} 個序列...")

    for series_id in series_ids:

        if not isinstance(series_id, str) or not series_id: continue

        # --- 修改：中文日誌 ---

        print(f"  -> 正在處理 FRED 序列: {series_id}...")

        single_series = _fetch_single_fred_series_with_cache(

            series_id=series_id, api_key=api_key,

            start_date_req=final_start_date_str, end_date_req=final_end_date_str,

            force_reload=force_reload

        )

        if single_series is not None and not single_series.empty:

            all_series_data[series_id] = single_series

            fetch_success_count += 1

            # --- 修改：中文日誌 ---

            print(f"     ...成功 (找到/快取 {len(single_series)} 個數據點)")

        else:

             # --- 修改：中文日誌 ---

             print(f"     ...失敗或未找到序列 {series_id} 的數據。")



    # --- 合併結果 ---

    if not all_series_data:

         # --- 修改：中文日誌 ---

        print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] FRED 抓取器錯誤: 未能為請求的序列抓取到任何有效數據: {series_ids}")

        return None



    # --- 修改：中文日誌 ---

    print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] 正在合併 {fetch_success_count} 個成功抓取的 FRED 序列...")

    try:

        combined_df = pd.concat(all_series_data, axis=1, sort=True)



        # --- 對齊到業務日索引並填充 ---

        final_start_dt = pd.to_datetime(final_start_date_str) if final_start_date_str else combined_df.index.min()

        final_end_dt = pd.to_datetime(final_end_date_str)

        if pd.isna(final_start_dt) or pd.isna(final_end_dt):

             raise ValueError("無效的開始或結束日期，無法創建業務日索引。") #<-- 修改：中文錯誤



        bday_index = pd.bdate_range(start=final_start_dt, end=final_end_dt, name='Date')

        aligned_df = combined_df.reindex(bday_index).ffill().bfill()

        aligned_df = aligned_df.loc[final_start_dt:final_end_dt]



        if aligned_df.empty:

             # --- 修改：中文日誌 ---

             print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] FRED 抓取器警告: 對齊/填充後最終 DataFrame 為空。")

             return None

        else:

             all_nan_cols = aligned_df.columns[aligned_df.isna().all()].tolist()

             if all_nan_cols:

                  # --- 修改：中文日誌 ---

                  print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] FRED 抓取器警告: 處理後全為 NaN 的欄位: {all_nan_cols}")

             # --- 修改：中文日誌 ---

             print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] FRED 抓取器完成。返回 DataFrame 維度 {aligned_df.shape}.")

             return aligned_df



    except Exception as e_combine:

         # --- 修改：中文日誌 ---

        print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] FRED 抓取器錯誤: 合併/對齊序列失敗: {e_combine}")

        return None



# --- 修改：中文日誌 ---

print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] Cell 10: Fetcher function 'fetch_fred_data' [日誌中文化 v1.1] defined.")



"""### 11. 數據抓取器定義 - NY Fed (Fetcher Def - NY Fed)"""



# 程式碼儲存格 11: 數據抓取器定義 - NY Fed (Fetcher Def - NY Fed) [日誌中文化 v1.1]

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-



import pandas as pd

import numpy as np

import requests # 需要用於創建 Session

import io # 用於處理 BytesIO

import traceback

from typing import Optional, Dict, Any, List, Tuple # 引入 Tuple

from datetime import datetime

import random # 需要用於 User-Agent



# --- 假設依賴的函數和變數已在之前的儲存格定義 ---

# 需要 Cell 0 的: LIB_STATUS (檢查 openpyxl)

# 需要 Cell 1 的: ACTIVE_CONFIG, DEFAULT_CONFIG

# 需要 Cell 2 的: _make_cache_key, _load_from_cache, _save_to_cache, CACHE_DIR_CELL2, PYARROW_AVAILABLE_CELL2

# 需要 Cell 3 的: get_nested_param, fetch_url, COMMON_USER_AGENTS (或者直接用 requests.Session)



# --- 修改：中文日誌 ---

print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] Cell 11: Fetcher Definition - NY Fed Dealer [日誌中文化 v1.1]...")



# --- 內部輔助函數：解析單個 NY Fed Excel 文件 ---

def _parse_nyfed_excel(excel_content: io.BytesIO, url: str, sbp_cols_config: Dict[str, List[str]]) -> Optional[pd.Series]:

    """(內部輔助) 解析單個 NY Fed Excel 文件內容，返回總持倉 Series。"""

    if not globals().get('LIB_STATUS', {}).get('openpyxl', False):

         # --- 修改：中文日誌 ---

        print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] NY Fed 解析器錯誤: 需要 openpyxl 庫但未找到。")

        return None



    try:

        header_row: Optional[int] = None

        date_col_name: Optional[str] = None

        potential_header_rows: List[int] = [3, 4, 0, 1, 2]

        potential_date_cols: List[str] = ['Effective Date', 'As Of Date', 'Week Ending', 'Date']



        excel_file = pd.ExcelFile(excel_content, engine='openpyxl')



        for h in potential_header_rows:

            try:

                df_peek = pd.read_excel(excel_file, header=h, nrows=5)

                excel_content.seek(0)

                required_data_cols = ['Time Series', 'Value (millions)']

                if all(col in df_peek.columns for col in required_data_cols):

                    header_row = h

                    found_date_col = None

                    for potential_name in potential_date_cols:

                        if potential_name in df_peek.columns:

                            found_date_col = potential_name; break

                    if found_date_col is None and len(df_peek.columns) > 0:

                         first_col_sample = pd.to_datetime(df_peek.iloc[:, 0], errors='coerce')

                         if first_col_sample.notna().mean() > 0.5: found_date_col = df_peek.columns[0]

                    if found_date_col: date_col_name = found_date_col; break

            except Exception: excel_content.seek(0); continue



        if header_row is None or date_col_name is None:

             # --- 修改：中文日誌 ---

            print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] NY Fed 解析器錯誤: 無法確定標頭行或日期列 for URL: {url}")

            return None



        data_long = pd.read_excel(excel_file, header=header_row, index_col=date_col_name, parse_dates=True)



        if not isinstance(data_long.index, pd.DatetimeIndex): data_long.index = pd.to_datetime(data_long.index, errors='coerce')

        data_long = data_long[data_long.index.notna()]

        if data_long.empty: return None

        data_long.index = data_long.index.normalize()

        if data_long.index.tz is not None: data_long.index = data_long.index.tz_localize(None)



        if 'Time Series' not in data_long.columns or 'Value (millions)' not in data_long.columns:

             # --- 修改：中文日誌 ---

            print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] NY Fed 解析器錯誤: 文件 {url} 中缺少必需的欄位 'Time Series' 或 'Value (millions)'。")

            return None



        data_long['Value (millions)'] = pd.to_numeric(data_long['Value (millions)'], errors='coerce')

        data_long.dropna(subset=['Value (millions)', 'Time Series'], inplace=True)

        if data_long.empty: return None



        try:

            data_long_agg = data_long.groupby([data_long.index, 'Time Series'])['Value (millions)'].mean()

            data_wide = data_long_agg.unstack(level='Time Series')

        except Exception as e_pivot:

             # --- 修改：中文日誌 ---

            print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] NY Fed 解析器錯誤: 無法對 {url} 的數據進行 pivot 操作: {e_pivot}")

            return None



        target_cols: List[str] = []; source_type: str = "Unknown"; url_lower = url.lower()

        if 'sbn' in url_lower: source_type = "SBN"; target_cols = [c for c in data_wide.columns if isinstance(c, str) and c.startswith('PDPOSGSC-')]

        elif 'sbp2013' in url_lower: source_type = "SBP2013"; target_cols = sbp_cols_config.get('sbp2013_cols', [])

        elif 'sbp2001' in url_lower: source_type = "SBP2001"; target_cols = sbp_cols_config.get('sbp2001_cols', [])



        if not target_cols:

             # --- 修改：中文日誌 ---

            print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] NY Fed 解析器警告: 未為來自 URL 的文件類型 '{source_type}' 定義需要加總的欄位: {url}")

            return None



        cols_to_sum_actual = [c for c in target_cols if c in data_wide.columns]

        if not cols_to_sum_actual:

             # --- 修改：中文日誌 ---

            print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] NY Fed 解析器錯誤: 在數據中未找到用於加總的目標欄位 ({source_type}) for URL: {url}")

            return None



        try:

            for col in cols_to_sum_actual: data_wide[col] = pd.to_numeric(data_wide[col], errors='coerce')

            daily_total = data_wide[cols_to_sum_actual].sum(axis=1, skipna=False)

            daily_total_cleaned = daily_total.dropna()

            daily_total_cleaned = daily_total_cleaned[daily_total_cleaned.abs() > 1e-9]

            if not daily_total_cleaned.empty: return daily_total_cleaned

            else: return None

        except Exception as e_sum:

             # --- 修改：中文日誌 ---

            print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] NY Fed 解析器錯誤: 為 {url} 加總欄位時失敗: {e_sum}")

            return None



    except Exception as e_main_parse:

         # --- 修改：中文日誌 ---

        print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] NY Fed 解析器錯誤: 解析 {url} 時發生未預期錯誤: {e_main_parse}")

        traceback.print_exc(limit=1)

        return None



# --- 主抓取函數 ---

def fetch_nyfed_data(config: Dict[str, Any]) -> Optional[pd.DataFrame]:

    """

    (數據抓取器 Fetcher) 從 NY Fed 網站抓取、解析並合併多個 Excel 文件中的一級交易商持倉數據。

    [v1.1]: 日誌中文化。

    """

    function_name = "fetch_nyfed_data" # 用於日誌

    if 'get_nested_param' not in globals() or 'DEFAULT_CONFIG' not in globals() or 'ACTIVE_CONFIG' not in globals():

         print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}][{function_name}] 錯誤: 缺少核心配置函數或變量。") #<-- 中文

         return None



    nyfed_cfg = get_nested_param(ACTIVE_CONFIG, DEFAULT_CONFIG, ['systemic_analysis', 'ny_fed'], {})

    urls = nyfed_cfg.get('positions_urls', [])

    sbp_cols_cfg = { 'sbp2013_cols': nyfed_cfg.get('sbp2013_cols', []), 'sbp2001_cols': nyfed_cfg.get('sbp2001_cols', []) }

    start_date_cfg = get_nested_param(ACTIVE_CONFIG, DEFAULT_CONFIG, ['general', 'start_date_input'])

    end_date_cfg = get_nested_param(ACTIVE_CONFIG, DEFAULT_CONFIG, ['general', 'end_date_input'])

    force_reload = config.get('force_reload', False)



    if not urls:

        print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}][{function_name}] 錯誤: 配置中未找到 'positions_urls'。") #<-- 中文

        return None

    if not globals().get('LIB_STATUS', {}).get('openpyxl', False):

         print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}][{function_name}] 錯誤: 需要 'openpyxl' 庫。") #<-- 中文

         return None

    if 'fetch_url' not in globals():

          print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}][{function_name}] 錯誤: 未定義 'fetch_url' 函數。") #<-- 中文

          return None



    # --- 快取處理 ---

    can_use_cache = False; cache_dir_local = None

    if all(f in globals() for f in ['_make_cache_key', '_load_from_cache', '_save_to_cache', 'CACHE_DIR_CELL2']) \

       and globals().get('PYARROW_AVAILABLE_CELL2', False) and globals().get('CACHE_DIR_CELL2'):

        can_use_cache = True; cache_dir_local = globals().get('CACHE_DIR_CELL2')



    cache_key = "CACHE_DISABLED"

    if can_use_cache:

        url_tuple_key = tuple(sorted(urls))

        cache_key = _make_cache_key("nyfed_dealer_combined", url_tuple_key, start_date_cfg or "None", end_date_cfg or "None")

        if not force_reload:

            cached_df = _load_from_cache(cache_key, cache_dir_local)

            if cached_df is not None and not cached_df.empty:

                 # --- 修改：中文日誌 ---

                 print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] NY Fed 抓取器: 從快取載入合併數據。")

                 return cached_df



    # --- 快取未命中或強制刷新 ---

     # --- 修改：中文日誌 ---

    print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] 正在抓取並處理 {len(urls)} 個 NY Fed Excel 文件...")

    all_positions_data: List[pd.Series] = []

    fetch_success_count = 0

    session = requests.Session()

    if 'COMMON_USER_AGENTS' in globals() and COMMON_USER_AGENTS:

         session.headers.update({'User-Agent': random.choice(COMMON_USER_AGENTS)})



    for i, url in enumerate(urls):

         # --- 修改：中文日誌 ---

        print(f"  -> 正在處理文件 {i+1}/{len(urls)} 從 {url.split('/')[-3]}...")

        response = fetch_url(url, session=session, timeout=120) # fetch_url 內部日誌保持英文

        if response and response.status_code == 200:

            try:

                excel_content = io.BytesIO(response.content)

                parsed_series = _parse_nyfed_excel(excel_content, url, sbp_cols_cfg) # 解析器內部日誌已中文化

                if parsed_series is not None and not parsed_series.empty:

                    all_positions_data.append(parsed_series)

                    fetch_success_count += 1

                    # --- 修改：中文日誌 ---

                    print(f"     ...成功 (解析出 {len(parsed_series)} 個數據點)")

                else:

                     # --- 修改：中文日誌 ---

                    print(f"     ...警告: 解析失敗或文件中無數據。")

            except Exception as e_proc:

                # --- 修改：中文日誌 ---

                print(f"     ...錯誤: 處理下載的文件時出錯: {e_proc}")

        else:

            # --- 修改：中文日誌 ---

            print(f"     ...錯誤: 文件下載失敗 (狀態碼: {response.status_code if response else '無響應'}).")



    if not all_positions_data:

        # --- 修改：中文日誌 ---

        print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}][{function_name}] 錯誤: 未能從提供的 URL 中處理任何有效數據。")

        return None



    # --- 修改：中文日誌 ---

    print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] 正在合併來自 {fetch_success_count} 個成功處理文件的數據...")

    try:

        combined_series = pd.concat(all_positions_data).sort_index()

        final_series = combined_series.groupby(level=0).last()

        final_series.name = 'Total_Gross_Positions_Millions'

        final_series = final_series[final_series.abs() > 1e-9].dropna()



        if final_series.empty:

             # --- 修改：中文日誌 ---

            print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}][{function_name}] 警告: 合併後的數據在處理後為空。")

            return None



        final_df = final_series.to_frame()

        try: tz = globals().get('taiwan_tz', timezone.utc)

        except NameError: tz = timezone.utc

        today_str = datetime.now(tz).strftime('%Y-%m-%d')

        final_end_date_str = end_date_cfg if end_date_cfg else today_str

        final_start_date_str = start_date_cfg if start_date_cfg else final_df.index.min().strftime('%Y-%m-%d')



        final_start_dt = pd.to_datetime(final_start_date_str)

        final_end_dt = pd.to_datetime(final_end_date_str)

        bday_index = pd.bdate_range(start=final_start_dt, end=final_end_dt, name='Date')



        aligned_df = final_df.reindex(bday_index).ffill()

        aligned_df = aligned_df.loc[final_start_dt:final_end_dt]



        if aligned_df.empty:

             # --- 修改：中文日誌 ---

             print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}][{function_name}] 警告: 對齊/填充後最終 DataFrame 為空。")

             return None



        if can_use_cache:

            _save_to_cache(aligned_df, cache_key, cache_dir_local)



        # --- 修改：中文日誌 ---

        print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] NY Fed 抓取器完成。返回 DataFrame 維度 {aligned_df.shape}.")

        return aligned_df



    except Exception as e_combine:

         # --- 修改：中文日誌 ---

        print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}][{function_name}] 錯誤: 合併或對齊抓取的序列失敗: {e_combine}")

        return None





# --- 修改：中文日誌 ---

print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] Cell 11: Fetcher function 'fetch_nyfed_data' and helper '_parse_nyfed_excel' [日誌中文化 v1.1] defined.")



"""### 12. 數據抓取器定義 - MOVE 指數 (Fetcher Def - MOVE Index)"""



# 程式碼儲存格 12: 數據抓取器定義 - MOVE 指數 (Fetcher Def - MOVE Index) [日誌中文化 v1.1]

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-



import pandas as pd

import numpy as np

from typing import Optional, Dict, Any

from datetime import datetime



# --- 假設依賴的函數和變數已在之前的儲存格定義 ---

# 需要 Cell 3 的: get_nested_param

# 需要 Cell 8 的: fetch_yf_daily (日誌已在 v1.1 中文化)

# 需要 Cell 1 的: ACTIVE_CONFIG, DEFAULT_CONFIG



# --- 修改：中文日誌 ---

print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] Cell 12: Fetcher Definition - MOVE Index [日誌中文化 v1.1]...")



def fetch_move_index(config: Dict[str, Any]) -> Optional[pd.DataFrame]:

    """

    (數據抓取器 Fetcher) 抓取 ICE BofA MOVE Index 的日線數據。

    通過調用 fetch_yf_daily 實現。

    [v1.1]: 將內部 print 日誌部分中文化。



    :param config: 配置字典... (說明同前)

    :return: Pandas DataFrame 或 None。

    """

    function_name = "fetch_move_index"

     # --- 修改：中文日誌 ---

    print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] == 開始執行: {function_name} ==")



    # --- 檢查依賴函數 ---

    required_funcs = ['fetch_yf_daily', 'get_nested_param']

    if not all(f in globals() for f in required_funcs):

         # --- 修改：中文日誌 ---

         missing_msg = f"缺少必要的函數: {[f for f in required_funcs if f not in globals()]}"

         print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] {function_name} 錯誤: {missing_msg}")

         return None

    if 'ACTIVE_CONFIG' not in globals() or 'DEFAULT_CONFIG' not in globals():

         # --- 修改：中文日誌 ---

         print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] {function_name} 錯誤: 未找到全局配置 ACTIVE_CONFIG 或 DEFAULT_CONFIG。")

         return None



    # --- 從主配置獲取 MOVE Ticker ---

    move_ticker = get_nested_param(

        ACTIVE_CONFIG, DEFAULT_CONFIG,

        ['systemic_analysis', 'yfinance_tickers', 'move'],

        default_value='^MOVE'

    )

    if not move_ticker or not isinstance(move_ticker, str):

         # --- 修改：中文日誌 ---

         print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] {function_name} 警告: 配置中的 MOVE ticker ('{move_ticker}') 無效。使用預設值 '^MOVE'。")

         move_ticker = '^MOVE'



    # --- 準備傳遞給 fetch_yf_daily 的配置 ---

    yf_config = {

        'ticker': move_ticker,

        'start_date': config.get('start_date'),

        'end_date': config.get('end_date'),

        'force_reload': config.get('force_reload', False)

    }



    # --- 調用 fetch_yf_daily ---

    move_df_raw = None

    try:

         # --- 修改：中文日誌 ---

        print(f"  -> 正在調用 fetch_yf_daily (抓取 Ticker: {move_ticker})...")

        # fetch_yf_daily 內部的日誌已經是中文 (v1.1)

        move_df_raw = fetch_yf_daily(yf_config)

    except Exception as e_call:

         # --- 修改：中文日誌 ---

         print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] {function_name} 錯誤: 調用 fetch_yf_daily 時發生異常: {e_call}")

         return None



    # --- 處理返回結果 ---

    if move_df_raw is None or move_df_raw.empty:

         # --- 修改：中文日誌 ---

        print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] {function_name} 警告: fetch_yf_daily 未返回 MOVE ticker '{move_ticker}' 的數據。")

        return None



    # --- 提取數據並重命名 ---

    close_col = 'Adj Close' if 'Adj Close' in move_df_raw.columns else 'Close'

    if close_col not in move_df_raw.columns:

         # --- 修改：中文日誌 ---

         print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] {function_name} 錯誤: 在 {move_ticker} 的抓取數據中未找到必需的欄位 '{close_col}'。")

         return None



    move_df = move_df_raw[[close_col]].copy()

    move_df.rename(columns={close_col: 'MOVE_Level'}, inplace=True)



    # --- 修改：中文日誌 ---

    print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] == {function_name} 已完成. 為 MOVE Index 返回 {len(move_df)} 行數據. ==")

    return move_df





# --- 修改：中文日誌 ---

print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] Cell 12: Fetcher function 'fetch_move_index' [日誌中文化 v1.1] defined.")



"""### 13. 數據抓取器定義 - 長債 ETF (Fetcher Def - LT Bond ETF)"""



# 程式碼儲存格 13: 數據抓取器定義 - 長債 ETF (Fetcher Def - LT Bond ETF)

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-



import pandas as pd

import numpy as np

from typing import Optional, Dict, Any

from datetime import datetime



# --- 假設依賴的函數和變數已在之前的儲存格定義 ---

# 需要 Cell 3 的: get_nested_param

# 需要 Cell 8 的: fetch_yf_daily

# 需要 Cell 1 的: ACTIVE_CONFIG, DEFAULT_CONFIG



print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] Cell 13: Fetcher Definition - Long-Term Bond ETF...")



def fetch_lt_bond_etf_data(config: Dict[str, Any]) -> Optional[pd.DataFrame]:

    """

    (數據抓取器 Fetcher) 抓取指定的長天期公債 ETF 的日線數據。

    通過調用 fetch_yf_daily 實現，使用配置中 systemic_analysis.yfinance_tickers.lt_bond_etf 指定的 Ticker。



    :param config: 配置字典，預期包含:

                   'start_date': str | None - 開始日期 "YYYY-MM-DD"。

                   'end_date': str | None - 結束日期 "YYYY-MM-DD"。

                   'force_reload': bool (可選, 預設 False) - 是否強制重新抓取。

                   (也會間接使用 ACTIVE_CONFIG 中的 systemic_analysis.yfinance_tickers.lt_bond_etf)

    :return: 包含 ETF 價格 (列名為 'ETF_[Ticker]_Price') 的 Pandas DataFrame，或在失敗/未配置時返回 None。

    """

    function_name = "fetch_lt_bond_etf_data"

    # print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] Executing: {function_name}...") # 執行時再打印



    # --- 檢查依賴函數和配置 ---

    required_globals = ['fetch_yf_daily', 'get_nested_param', 'ACTIVE_CONFIG', 'DEFAULT_CONFIG']

    if not all(f in globals() for f in required_globals):

         missing = [f for f in required_globals if f not in globals()]

         print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] {function_name} Error: Missing required functions or global config vars: {missing}")

         return None



    # --- 從主配置獲取 ETF Ticker ---

    etf_ticker = get_nested_param(

        ACTIVE_CONFIG, DEFAULT_CONFIG,

        # 配置路徑

        ['systemic_analysis', 'yfinance_tickers', 'lt_bond_etf'],

        default_value=None # 預設為 None

    )



    # 如果配置中沒有 Ticker，則不執行抓取

    if not etf_ticker or not isinstance(etf_ticker, str):

         print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] {function_name} Info: Long-Term Bond ETF ticker not specified in config. Skipping fetch.")

         return None # 返回 None 表示未執行



    etf_ticker = etf_ticker.strip().upper() # 標準化 Ticker



    # --- 準備調用 fetch_yf_daily 的配置 ---

    yf_config = {

        'ticker': etf_ticker,

        'start_date': config.get('start_date'), # 使用傳入的日期

        'end_date': config.get('end_date'),

        'force_reload': config.get('force_reload', False)

    }



    # --- 調用 fetch_yf_daily ---

    etf_df_raw = None # 初始化

    try:

        # print(f"  -> Calling fetch_yf_daily for ticker: {etf_ticker}...") # Debug

        etf_df_raw = fetch_yf_daily(yf_config) # 調用日線抓取函數

    except Exception as e_call:

         print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] {function_name} Error: Exception during call to fetch_yf_daily for {etf_ticker}: {e_call}")

         return None



    # --- 處理返回結果 ---

    if etf_df_raw is None or etf_df_raw.empty:

        print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] {function_name} Warning: fetch_yf_daily returned no data for ETF ticker '{etf_ticker}'.")

        return None



    # --- 提取數據並重命名 ---

    # ETF 通常使用 'Adj Close' 或 'Close'

    close_col = 'Adj Close' if 'Adj Close' in etf_df_raw.columns and etf_df_raw['Adj Close'].notna().any() else 'Close'

    if close_col not in etf_df_raw.columns:

         print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] {function_name} Error: Required column '{close_col}' not found in fetched data for {etf_ticker}.")

         return None



    # 創建只包含價格的新 DataFrame，並使用包含 Ticker 的名稱重命名列

    etf_col_name = f'ETF_{etf_ticker}_Price' # 動態生成列名

    etf_df = etf_df_raw[[close_col]].copy()

    etf_df.rename(columns={close_col: etf_col_name}, inplace=True)



    print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] {function_name} completed. Returning {len(etf_df)} rows for {etf_ticker}.")

    return etf_df





print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] Cell 13: Fetcher function 'fetch_lt_bond_etf_data' defined.")



"""---



14. 分析器定義 - C1 Stage 1

"""



# 程式碼儲存格 14: 分析器定義 - C1 Stage 1 (Analyzer Def - C1 Stage 1)

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-



import pandas as pd

import numpy as np

from typing import Optional, Dict, Any, Tuple, List # 引入 Tuple, List

from datetime import datetime

import traceback



# --- 假設依賴的函數和變數已在之前的儲存格定義 ---

# 需要 Cell 3 的: get_nested_param

# 需要 Cell 5 的: calculate_hist_vol

# 需要 Cell 1 的: ACTIVE_CONFIG, DEFAULT_CONFIG



print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] Cell 14: Analyzer Definition - C1 Stage 1 Eval...")



def run_stage1_analysis(data: Dict[str, pd.DataFrame], config: Dict[str, Any]) -> Tuple[Optional[Dict[str, Any]], float]:

    """

    (分析器 Analyzer) 執行 Stage 1 基本快速評估。

    基於 VIX, HV, DGS10 的水平和近期變化，生成四個維度的初步評分和理由。



    :param data: 包含輸入數據的字典，預期至少包含 'master_daily_data'，

                 該 DataFrame 應包含 'Adj Close', 'High', 'Low', 'Close', 'VIX', 'DGS10' 等列。

    :param config: 完整的 ACTIVE_CONFIG 字典。

    :return: Tuple[Optional[Dict[str, Any]], float]

             - 第一個元素是包含評估結果的字典，格式如:

               { "維度名稱": {"gauge_value": 分數, "reason": 理由字串}, ... }

               如果計算失敗則為 None。

             - 第二個元素是本次計算的可靠度評分 (0-100)。

    """

    function_name = "run_stage1_analysis"

    print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] Executing: {function_name}...")



    # --- 初始化返回值 ---

    results: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {

        "極短期 (1-7天)": {"gauge_value": np.nan, "reason": "初始化"},

        "短期 (~60天)": {"gauge_value": np.nan, "reason": "初始化"},

        "中期 (~160天)": {"gauge_value": np.nan, "reason": "初始化"},

        "長期 (~10年)": {"gauge_value": np.nan, "reason": "初始化"}

    }

    reliability_score: float = 0.0

    component_reliabilities: Dict[str, float] = {'daily': 0.0, 'dgs10': 0.0, 'vix': 0.0}



    # --- 檢查依賴函數 ---

    required_funcs = ['get_nested_param', 'calculate_hist_vol']

    if not all(f in globals() for f in required_funcs):

         missing = [f for f in required_funcs if f not in globals()]

         print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] {function_name} Error: Missing required functions: {missing}")

         results["極短期 (1-7天)"]["reason"] = "依賴函數缺失"

         return None, 0.0



    try:

        # --- 1. 獲取所需配置參數 ---

        # 使用 get_nested_param 從傳入的 config (即 ACTIVE_CONFIG) 中獲取

        if 'DEFAULT_CONFIG' not in globals(): raise ValueError("DEFAULT_CONFIG not found.") # 確保預設配置存在



        s1_config = get_nested_param(config, DEFAULT_CONFIG, ['stage1_basic'], {})

        hv_short_period: int = get_nested_param(s1_config, {}, ['hv_short_period'], 20)

        hv_mid_period: int = get_nested_param(s1_config, {}, ['hv_mid_period'], 60)

        change_period: int = get_nested_param(s1_config, {}, ['change_period'], 5)

        mapping_cfg: Dict[str, float] = get_nested_param(s1_config, {}, ['mapping'], {})

        thresholds_cfg: Dict[str, float] = get_nested_param(s1_config, {}, ['change_thresholds'], {})



        # --- 2. 提取並驗證輸入數據 ---

        master_daily_data = data.get('master_daily_data') # 假設主流程傳入的 key 是這個

        if not isinstance(master_daily_data, pd.DataFrame) or master_daily_data.empty:

            print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] {function_name} Error: Input data 'master_daily_data' is missing or empty.")

            results["極短期 (1-7天)"]["reason"] = "輸入數據缺失"

            return None, 0.0



        # 確定價格列

        price_col = 'Adj Close' if 'Adj Close' in master_daily_data.columns and master_daily_data['Adj Close'].notna().any() else 'Close'

        required_cols_stage1 = [price_col, 'High', 'Low', 'Close', 'VIX', 'DGS10'] # VIX, DGS10 需要存在

        missing_cols = [c for c in required_cols_stage1 if c not in master_daily_data.columns or master_daily_data[c].isna().all()]

        if missing_cols:

            print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] {function_name} Error: Missing required columns in master_daily_data: {missing_cols}")

            results["極短期 (1-7天)"]["reason"] = f"缺少列: {', '.join(missing_cols)}"

            return None, 0.0



        price_series_s1 = master_daily_data[price_col]

        dgs10_series_s1 = master_daily_data['DGS10']

        vix_series_s1 = master_daily_data['VIX']



        # --- 3. 計算可靠度 (基於數據長度) ---

        min_daily_len: int = hv_mid_period + change_period + 5 # 計算 HV 和變化所需的最少數據

        min_fred_len: int = change_period + 5 # 計算變化所需的最少數據

        valid_daily_count = price_series_s1.dropna().count()

        valid_dgs10_count = dgs10_series_s1.dropna().count()

        valid_vix_count = vix_series_s1.dropna().count()



        if valid_daily_count >= min_daily_len: component_reliabilities['daily'] = 100.0

        elif valid_daily_count > change_period + 1: component_reliabilities['daily'] = 60.0 # 至少能算變化

        else: component_reliabilities['daily'] = 0.0



        if valid_dgs10_count >= min_fred_len: component_reliabilities['dgs10'] = 100.0

        elif valid_dgs10_count > 1: component_reliabilities['dgs10'] = 50.0 # 至少有數據

        else: component_reliabilities['dgs10'] = 0.0



        if valid_vix_count >= min_fred_len: component_reliabilities['vix'] = 100.0

        elif valid_vix_count > 1: component_reliabilities['vix'] = 50.0

        else: component_reliabilities['vix'] = 0.0



        reliability_score = float(np.mean(list(component_reliabilities.values())))



        # 如果核心數據可靠度為 0，無法繼續計算

        if component_reliabilities['daily'] == 0.0 or component_reliabilities['dgs10'] == 0.0 or component_reliabilities['vix'] == 0.0:

            print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] {function_name} Warning: Core data reliability is too low ({reliability_score:.1f}%). Cannot proceed.")

            results["極短期 (1-7天)"]["reason"] = "核心數據可靠度不足"

            return results, reliability_score # 返回空結果和可靠度



        # --- 4. 計算所需指標 ---

        print("  -> Calculating Stage 1 indicators...")

        hv_short = calculate_hist_vol(price_series_s1, window=hv_short_period) if component_reliabilities['daily'] >= 60 else pd.Series(np.nan, index=price_series_s1.index)

        hv_mid = calculate_hist_vol(price_series_s1, window=hv_mid_period) if component_reliabilities['daily'] >= 60 else pd.Series(np.nan, index=price_series_s1.index)



        # 計算近期變化值

        vix_change_val = np.nan

        if component_reliabilities['vix'] >= 50 and len(vix_series_s1.dropna()) > change_period:

            vix_change_val = vix_series_s1.diff(change_period).iloc[-1]



        dgs10_change_val = np.nan

        if component_reliabilities['dgs10'] >= 50 and len(dgs10_series_s1.dropna()) > change_period:

            dgs10_change_val = dgs10_series_s1.diff(change_period).iloc[-1]



        # 獲取最新值

        latest_vix = vix_series_s1.dropna().iloc[-1] if component_reliabilities['vix'] > 0 else np.nan

        latest_hv_short = hv_short.dropna().iloc[-1] if hv_short is not None and hv_short.notna().any() else np.nan

        latest_hv_mid = hv_mid.dropna().iloc[-1] if hv_mid is not None and hv_mid.notna().any() else np.nan

        latest_dgs10 = dgs10_series_s1.dropna().iloc[-1] if component_reliabilities['dgs10'] > 0 else np.nan



        # --- 5. 線性映射與分數計算 ---

        # 內部輔助函數用於線性映射到 0-100

        def _linear_map(value: Optional[float], min_val: float, max_val: float) -> float:

            if pd.isna(value): return 50.0 # 無法評估時給中性分 50

            # 避免除以零

            denominator = max(max_val - min_val, 1e-9)

            score = ((value - min_val) / denominator) * 100.0

            return np.clip(score, 0.0, 100.0) # 限制在 0-100



        # 從配置讀取映射邊界和變化閾值

        vix_min = mapping_cfg.get('vix_min', 10.0); vix_max = mapping_cfg.get('vix_max', 50.0)

        hv_min = mapping_cfg.get('hv_min', 10.0); hv_max_s = mapping_cfg.get('hv_max_short', 40.0); hv_max_m = mapping_cfg.get('hv_max_mid', 50.0)

        dgs10_min = mapping_cfg.get('dgs10_min', 1.0); dgs10_max = mapping_cfg.get('dgs10_max', 6.0)

        vix_up_thr = thresholds_cfg.get('vix_up', 5.0); vix_down_thr = thresholds_cfg.get('vix_down', 3.0)

        dgs10_up_thr = thresholds_cfg.get('dgs10_up', 0.15); dgs10_down_thr = thresholds_cfg.get('dgs10_down', 0.10)



        # 計算各維度分數

        # 極短期 (VS): VIX 水平 + HV 短期水平

        score_vs_vix = _linear_map(latest_vix, vix_min, vix_max)

        score_vs_hv = _linear_map(latest_hv_short, hv_min, hv_max_s)

        results["極短期 (1-7天)"]["gauge_value"] = np.nanmean([score_vs_vix, score_vs_hv])

        results["極短期 (1-7天)"]["reason"] = f"VIX={latest_vix:.1f}, HV{hv_short_period}={latest_hv_short:.1f}" if pd.notna(latest_vix) or pd.notna(latest_hv_short) else "數據不足"



        # 短期 (S): VIX 近期變化 + HV 中期水平

        score_s_vix_chg = 50.0 # 預設中性

        if pd.notna(vix_change_val):

            if vix_change_val >= vix_up_thr: score_s_vix_chg = 100.0

            elif vix_change_val <= -vix_down_thr: score_s_vix_chg = 0.0

            # 線性插值中間狀態

            elif vix_up_thr > 0 and vix_change_val >= 0: score_s_vix_chg = 50.0 + (vix_change_val / vix_up_thr * 50.0)

            elif vix_down_thr > 0 and vix_change_val < 0: score_s_vix_chg = 50.0 - (abs(vix_change_val) / vix_down_thr * 50.0)

            score_s_vix_chg = np.clip(score_s_vix_chg, 0, 100)

        score_s_hv = _linear_map(latest_hv_mid, hv_min, hv_max_m)

        results["短期 (~60天)"]["gauge_value"] = np.nanmean([score_s_vix_chg, score_s_hv])

        results["短期 (~60天)"]["reason"] = f"VIX Chg({change_period}d)={vix_change_val:.2f}, HV{hv_mid_period}={latest_hv_mid:.1f}" if pd.notna(vix_change_val) or pd.notna(latest_hv_mid) else "數據不足"



        # 中期 (M): DGS10 水平 + DGS10 近期變化

        score_m_dgs_lvl = _linear_map(latest_dgs10, dgs10_min, dgs10_max) # 利率越高，風險越高 (直接映射)

        score_m_dgs_chg = 50.0 # 預設中性

        if pd.notna(dgs10_change_val):

            if dgs10_change_val >= dgs10_up_thr: score_m_dgs_chg = 100.0

            elif dgs10_change_val <= -dgs10_down_thr: score_m_dgs_chg = 0.0

            # 線性插值

            elif dgs10_up_thr > 0 and dgs10_change_val >= 0: score_m_dgs_chg = 50.0 + (dgs10_change_val / dgs10_up_thr * 50.0)

            elif dgs10_down_thr > 0 and dgs10_change_val < 0: score_m_dgs_chg = 50.0 - (abs(dgs10_change_val) / dgs10_down_thr * 50.0)

            score_m_dgs_chg = np.clip(score_m_dgs_chg, 0, 100)

        results["中期 (~160天)"]["gauge_value"] = np.nanmean([score_m_dgs_lvl, score_m_dgs_chg])

        results["中期 (~160天)"]["reason"] = f"10Y={latest_dgs10:.2f}%, Chg({change_period}d)={dgs10_change_val:.2f}" if pd.notna(latest_dgs10) or pd.notna(dgs10_change_val) else "數據不足"



        # 長期 (L): DGS10 水平 (簡化版，只看利率水平)

        results["長期 (~10年)"]["gauge_value"] = score_m_dgs_lvl

        results["長期 (~10年)"]["reason"] = f"10Y Treasury Level = {latest_dgs10:.2f}%" if pd.notna(latest_dgs10) else "數據不足"



        # --- 6. 整理最終結果 ---

        for k in results:

            if pd.notna(results[k]["gauge_value"]):

                results[k]["gauge_value"] = round(results[k]["gauge_value"], 1)

            else: # 確保無效分數是 NaN

                 results[k]["gauge_value"] = np.nan



        print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] {function_name} completed. Reliability: {reliability_score:.1f}%")

        return results, reliability_score



    except Exception as e_s1:

        print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] {function_name} Error: Unexpected error during calculation: {e_s1}")

        traceback.print_exc(limit=2)

        # 出錯時返回初始空結果和計算出的（可能不準的）可靠度

        results = { k: {"gauge_value": np.nan, "reason": "計算錯誤"} for k in results }

        return results, reliability_score





print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] Cell 14: Analyzer function 'run_stage1_analysis' defined.")



"""### 15. 分析器定義 - C1 Risk VS (Analyzer Def - C1 Risk VS)"""



# 程式碼儲存格 15: 分析器定義 - C1 Risk VS (Analyzer Def - C1 Risk VS)

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-



import pandas as pd

import numpy as np

from typing import Optional, Dict, Any, Tuple, List # 引入 Tuple, List

from datetime import datetime

import traceback



# 嘗試導入 scipy.stats (如果 Scheme 2 需要)

try:

    from scipy import stats as scipy_stats

    SCIPY_AVAILABLE = True

except ImportError:

    print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] Cell 15 Warning: scipy not found. VS Model Scheme 2 will not work.")

    SCIPY_AVAILABLE = False

    scipy_stats = None # 確保變數存在



# --- 假設依賴的函數和變數已在之前的儲存格定義 ---

# 需要 Cell 1 的: ACTIVE_CONFIG, DEFAULT_CONFIG

# 需要 Cell 3 的: get_nested_param

# 需要 Cell 4 的: calculate_sma

# 需要 Cell 5 的: calculate_atr

# 需要 Cell 6 的: calculate_deviation_pct, get_rolling_percentile_rank



print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] Cell 15: Analyzer Definition - C1 Risk VS...")



def run_vs_model(data: Dict[str, pd.DataFrame], config: Dict[str, Any]) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:

    """

    (分析器 Analyzer) 執行 C1 Stage 2 VS (極短期) 風險模型計算。

    基於小時線數據、ATR 和 MA 乖離。包含兩種計算方案。



    :param data: 包含輸入數據的字典，預期必須包含 'yf_hourly'，值為小時線 OHLCV DataFrame。

                 (鍵名 'yf_hourly' 需要與主流程準備數據時使用的鍵名一致)

    :param config: 完整的 ACTIVE_CONFIG 字典。

    :return: 包含 VS 模型結果的字典，格式如:

             {

                 'risk_score': float | np.nan, # 最終風險評分 (0-100)

                 'reliability': float,         # 可靠度 (目前簡單設為 100 或 0)

                 'history': pd.Series | None, # 風險分數歷史序列 (用於繪圖)

                 'details': Dict             # 包含計算細節 (方案, MA週期, 乖離率, ATR等)

             }

             如果計算失敗則返回 None。

    """

    function_name = "run_vs_model"

    print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] Executing: {function_name}...")



    # --- 初始化返回值 ---

    results = {

        'risk_score': np.nan,

        'reliability': 0.0,

        'history': None,

        'details': {'calculation_scheme': None, 'error': None} # 初始化 details

    }



    # --- 檢查依賴函數 ---

    required_funcs = ['get_nested_param', 'calculate_atr', 'calculate_sma', 'calculate_deviation_pct', 'get_rolling_percentile_rank']

    if not all(f in globals() for f in required_funcs):

         missing = [f for f in required_funcs if f not in globals()]

         error_msg = f"Missing required functions: {missing}"

         print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] {function_name} Error: {error_msg}")

         results['details']['error'] = error_msg

         return results # 返回帶有錯誤信息的結果



    try:

        # --- 1. 獲取配置參數 ---

        if 'DEFAULT_CONFIG' not in globals(): raise ValueError("DEFAULT_CONFIG not found.")

        vs_config = get_nested_param(config, DEFAULT_CONFIG, ['stage2_risk_models_v1', 'vs'], {})

        atr_period = vs_config.get('atr_period', 14)

        calc_scheme = vs_config.get('calculation_scheme', 1)

        hist_chart_hours = vs_config.get('hist_chart_hours', 630)

        smoothing_period_hr = vs_config.get('smoothing_period_hours', 10) # VS 趨勢圖平滑週期

        results['details']['calculation_scheme'] = calc_scheme



        # 方案 1 參數

        s1_params = vs_config.get('scheme1', {})

        s1_short_min=int(s1_params.get('short_ma_min', 1)); s1_short_max=int(s1_params.get('short_ma_max', 20))

        s1_long_min=int(s1_params.get('long_ma_min', 40)); s1_long_max=int(s1_params.get('long_ma_max', 150))

        s1_dev_lookback=int(s1_params.get('deviation_lookback_hours', 630))



        # 方案 2 參數

        s2_params = vs_config.get('scheme2', {})

        s2_low_s=int(s2_params.get('low_vol_ma_short', 10)); s2_low_l=int(s2_params.get('low_vol_ma_long', 100))

        s2_high_s=int(s2_params.get('high_vol_ma_short', 5)); s2_high_l=int(s2_params.get('high_vol_ma_long', 50))



        # 檢查方案 2 的依賴

        if calc_scheme == 2 and not SCIPY_AVAILABLE:

            raise ValueError("'scipy' library needed for VS Model Scheme 2 is not available.")



        # --- 2. 獲取並驗證小時數據 ---

        hourly_data = data.get('yf_hourly') # Key 需與協調函數傳入的一致

        if not isinstance(hourly_data, pd.DataFrame) or hourly_data.empty:

            raise ValueError("Input data 'yf_hourly' is missing or empty.")



        required_cols = ['High', 'Low', 'Close']

        price_col = 'Adj Close' if 'Adj Close' in hourly_data.columns and hourly_data['Adj Close'].notna().any() else 'Close'

        if price_col not in hourly_data.columns: required_cols.append(price_col)

        if not all(col in hourly_data.columns for col in required_cols):

            raise ValueError(f"Hourly data missing columns: {[c for c in required_cols if c not in hourly_data.columns]}")



        # 檢查數據長度

        max_period_needed = 1

        if calc_scheme == 1: max_period_needed = max(s1_long_max, atr_period, s1_dev_lookback)

        elif calc_scheme == 2: max_period_needed = max(s2_low_l, s2_high_l, atr_period)

        min_rows_needed = max_period_needed + 10 # 增加 buffer

        if len(hourly_data) < min_rows_needed:

            raise ValueError(f"Hourly data length ({len(hourly_data)}) < required ({min_rows_needed}) for scheme {calc_scheme}.")



        print(f"  -> Using '{price_col}' column from hourly data.")

        price_series_hr = hourly_data[price_col].copy().astype(float) # 確保是 float



        # --- 3. 計算通用指標 (ATR) ---

        print("  -> Calculating hourly ATR...")

        atr_hr = calculate_atr(hourly_data, period=atr_period)

        if atr_hr is None or atr_hr.isna().all(): raise ValueError("Failed to calculate hourly ATR.")



        # 標準化 ATR (ATR / Price * 100)

        price_hr_safe = price_series_hr.replace(0, np.nan)

        norm_atr_raw = atr_hr / price_hr_safe * 100

        normalized_atr = norm_atr_raw.replace([np.inf, -np.inf], np.nan).dropna()

        if normalized_atr.empty: raise ValueError("Normalized ATR calculation resulted in empty series.")



        atr_median = normalized_atr.median(); atr_p01 = normalized_atr.quantile(0.01); atr_p99 = normalized_atr.quantile(0.99)

        if pd.isna(atr_median) or pd.isna(atr_p01) or pd.isna(atr_p99):

            print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] {function_name} Warning: Could not calculate valid ATR distribution.")



        latest_norm_atr = normalized_atr.iloc[-1] if not normalized_atr.empty else np.nan

        results['details']['latest_normalized_atr'] = round(latest_norm_atr, 4) if pd.notna(latest_norm_atr) else None

        results['details']['atr_median'] = round(atr_median, 4) if pd.notna(atr_median) else None

        results['details']['atr_p01'] = round(atr_p01, 4) if pd.notna(atr_p01) else None

        results['details']['atr_p99'] = round(atr_p99, 4) if pd.notna(atr_p99) else None



        # --- 4. 根據方案計算風險分數歷史 ---

        risk_score_series = pd.Series(np.nan, index=hourly_data.index, dtype=float) # 初始化



        if calc_scheme == 2:

            # --- 方案二：固定 MA 組 ---

            print(f"  -> Calculating VS using Scheme 2 (Fixed MAs: L={s2_low_s}/{s2_low_l}, H={s2_high_s}/{s2_high_l})...")

            # 計算兩組 MA 和乖離率

            sma_s_h = calculate_sma(price_series_hr, s2_high_s); sma_l_h = calculate_sma(price_series_hr, s2_high_l)

            sma_s_l = calculate_sma(price_series_hr, s2_low_s); sma_l_l = calculate_sma(price_series_hr, s2_low_l)

            dev_high_vol = calculate_deviation_pct(sma_s_h, sma_l_h)

            dev_low_vol = calculate_deviation_pct(sma_s_l, sma_l_l)



            # 獲取歷史乖離數據 (移除 NaN)

            hist_dev_high_clean = dev_high_vol.dropna().values

            hist_dev_low_clean = dev_low_vol.dropna().values

            if len(hist_dev_high_clean) < 20 or len(hist_dev_low_clean) < 20: # 至少需要一些數據點來計算百分位

                raise ValueError("Not enough historical deviation data points for Scheme 2 percentile calculation.")



            # 判斷高低波動狀態

            target_index = hourly_data.index

            atr_aligned = normalized_atr.reindex(target_index).ffill().bfill()

            is_high_volatility = True if pd.isna(atr_median) else (atr_aligned > atr_median) # 無法判斷時預設高波動

            is_high_volatility = is_high_volatility.fillna(True)



            # 計算每個時間點的風險分數 (使用 percentileofscore)

            scores_list = []

            current_deviations_aligned = pd.Series(np.where(is_high_volatility, dev_high_vol, dev_low_vol), index=target_index)



            for idx in target_index: # 迭代索引比直接用 apply 可能更清晰

                current_dev = current_deviations_aligned.get(idx)

                score = np.nan

                if pd.notna(current_dev):

                    hist_array_to_use = hist_dev_high_clean if is_high_volatility.get(idx, True) else hist_dev_low_clean

                    # kind='rank' 處理相同值情況，與 rolling().rank(pct=True) 類似

                    score = scipy_stats.percentileofscore(hist_array_to_use, current_dev, kind='rank')

                scores_list.append(score)



            risk_score_series_calc = pd.Series(scores_list, index=target_index).clip(0, 100)

            risk_score_series.update(risk_score_series_calc)



            # 記錄細節

            last_idx = target_index[-1]

            results['details']['chosen_short_ma'] = int(np.where(is_high_volatility.get(last_idx, True), s2_high_s, s2_low_s))

            results['details']['chosen_long_ma'] = int(np.where(is_high_volatility.get(last_idx, True), s2_high_l, s2_low_l))

            results['details']['current_deviation'] = round(current_deviations_aligned.get(last_idx), 4) if pd.notna(current_deviations_aligned.get(last_idx)) else None

            results['details']['volatility_state'] = 'High' if is_high_volatility.get(last_idx, True) else 'Low'



        else: # 預設方案一：動態 MA

            print("  -> Calculating VS using Scheme 1 (Dynamic MA)...")

            # 計算 ATR 百分位 (0-1)

            atr_percentile = 0.5 # 預設

            if pd.notna(latest_norm_atr) and pd.notna(atr_p01) and pd.notna(atr_p99):

                atr_range = atr_p99 - atr_p01

                if abs(atr_range) > 1e-9: atr_percentile = np.clip((latest_norm_atr - atr_p01) / atr_range, 0, 1)



            # 動態選擇 MA 週期

            s_short = int(np.clip(np.round(s1_short_max - atr_percentile * (s1_short_max - s1_short_min)), s1_short_min, s1_short_max))

            s_long = int(np.clip(np.round(s1_long_max - atr_percentile * (s1_long_max - s1_long_min)), s1_long_min, s1_long_max))

            if s_short >= s_long: s_long = min(s1_long_max, s_short + max(1, int(s1_long_min * 0.1))) # 確保 long > short

            if s_short >= s_long: s_short = max(s1_short_min, s_long - 1)



            final_short_ma = s_short; final_long_ma = s_long;

            results['details']['chosen_short_ma'] = final_short_ma

            results['details']['chosen_long_ma'] = final_long_ma

            print(f"    - Latest ATR Percentile: {atr_percentile:.2f}, Chosen MAs: {final_short_ma}h / {final_long_ma}h")



            # 計算乖離率

            if len(price_series_hr) < final_long_ma + 1: raise ValueError(f"Data length insufficient for chosen MA {final_long_ma}")

            sma_short = calculate_sma(price_series_hr, final_short_ma)

            sma_long = calculate_sma(price_series_hr, final_long_ma)

            deviation_series = calculate_deviation_pct(sma_short, sma_long)

            if deviation_series.isna().all(): raise ValueError("Calculated deviation series is all NaN (Scheme 1)")



            latest_dev = deviation_series.dropna().iloc[-1] if not deviation_series.dropna().empty else np.nan

            results['details']['current_deviation'] = round(latest_dev, 4) if pd.notna(latest_dev) else None



            # 計算乖離率的滾動百分位排名作為風險分數

            min_p_dev_rank = max(10, int(s1_dev_lookback * 0.6)) # 乖離率排名需要一些點

            risk_score_series_calc = get_rolling_percentile_rank(deviation_series, window=s1_dev_lookback, min_periods=min_p_dev_rank)

            risk_score_series.update(risk_score_series_calc.clip(0, 100))



        # --- 5. 整理最終輸出 ---

        final_hist_scores = None

        if risk_score_series.notna().any():

             # 應用趨勢圖平滑 (如果需要，但通常在繪圖函數做)

             # risk_score_series = calculate_ema(risk_score_series, smoothing_period_hr) # 或 SMA

             # 截取歷史

             final_hist_scores = risk_score_series.tail(hist_chart_hours).copy() if hist_chart_hours and hist_chart_hours > 0 else risk_score_series.copy()



        latest_score = risk_score_series.dropna().iloc[-1] if not risk_score_series.dropna().empty else np.nan

        if pd.notna(latest_score):

            results['risk_score'] = round(float(latest_score), 1)

            results['reliability'] = 100.0 # 成功計算出分數，可靠度 100

        else: # 無法計算最終分數

            results['risk_score'] = np.nan

            results['reliability'] = 0.0



        results['history'] = final_hist_scores

        # 添加平滑週期到 details，供繪圖函數使用

        results['details']['smoothing_period'] = smoothing_period_hr



        print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] {function_name} completed. Score: {results['risk_score']}, Reliability: {results['reliability']:.1f}%")

        return results



    except ValueError as ve: # 捕獲數據或參數驗證錯誤

        error_msg = f"Data/Param validation failed: {ve}"

        print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] {function_name} Error: {error_msg}")

        results['details']['error'] = error_msg

        results['reliability'] = 0.0 # 驗證失敗可靠度為 0

        return results

    except Exception as e_main: # 捕捉其他所有錯誤

        error_msg = f"Unexpected error: {e_main}"

        print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] {function_name} Error: {error_msg}")

        traceback.print_exc(limit=2)

        results['details']['error'] = error_msg

        results['reliability'] = 0.0 # 計算失敗可靠度為 0

        return results





print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] Cell 15: Analyzer function 'run_vs_model' defined.")



"""### 16. 分析器定義 - C1 Risk SML (Analyzer Def - C1 Risk SML)"""



# 程式碼儲存格 16: 分析器定義 - C1 Risk SML (Analyzer Def - C1 Risk SML) [日誌中文化 v1.2 - 完整]

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-



import pandas as pd

import numpy as np

from typing import Optional, Dict, Any, Tuple, List, Callable

from datetime import datetime

import time

import traceback



# --- 假設依賴... ---



print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] Cell 16: Analyzer Definition - C1 Risk SML [日誌中文化 v1.2 - 完整]...")



def run_sml_model(data: Dict[str, pd.DataFrame], model_type: str, config: Dict[str, Any]) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:

    """

    (分析器 Analyzer) 執行 C1 Stage 2 S/M/L 風險模型計算。

    [v1.2]: 將內部所有 print 日誌部分中文化。



    :param data: ... (說明同前)

    :param model_type: ...

    :param config: ...

    :return: ...

    """

    model_type_lower = model_type.lower()

    if model_type_lower not in ['s', 'm', 'l']:

        # --- 修改：中文日誌 ---

        print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] run_sml_model 錯誤: 無效的 model_type '{model_type}'. 必須是 's', 'm', 或 'l'.")

        return None

    function_name = f"run_{model_type_lower}_model"

    model_type_full = {"s": "短期(S)", "m": "中期(M)", "l": "長期(L)"}.get(model_type_lower)

    # --- 修改：中文日誌 ---

    print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] == 開始執行: {function_name} for {model_type_full} ==")



    results = { 'risk_score': np.nan, 'reliability': 0.0, 'history': None,

                'details': {'final_window_used': None, 'indicators_status': {}, 'error': None} }



    # --- 檢查依賴 (同前) ---

    required_funcs = ['get_nested_param', 'get_rolling_percentile_rank', 'map_percentile_to_risk']

    essential_indicators = ['calculate_sma', 'calculate_deviation_pct', 'calculate_hist_vol', 'calculate_roc', 'calculate_yield_spread']

    required_funcs.extend(essential_indicators)

    if not all(f in globals() for f in required_funcs) or 'DEFAULT_CONFIG' not in globals() or 'ACTIVE_CONFIG' not in globals():

         missing = [f for f in required_funcs if f not in globals()]

         if 'DEFAULT_CONFIG' not in globals(): missing.append('DEFAULT_CONFIG')

         if 'ACTIVE_CONFIG' not in globals(): missing.append('ACTIVE_CONFIG')

         error_msg = f"缺少必要的函數或全局配置: {missing}" #<-- 中文

         print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] {function_name} 錯誤: {error_msg}")

         results['details']['error'] = error_msg

         return results



    try:

        # --- 1. 獲取參數 ---

        s2_config = get_nested_param(config, DEFAULT_CONFIG, ['stage2_risk_models_v1'], {})

        model_config = get_nested_param(s2_config, {}, [model_type_lower], {})

        common_params = get_nested_param(s2_config, {}, ['common_params'], {})



        if not model_config.get('enabled', True):

            print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] {function_name} 信息: 模型 '{model_type_full}' 在配置中被禁用.") #<-- 中文

            return None



        indicator_list: List[str] = model_config.get('indicators', [])

        initial_window: int = int(model_config.get('normalization_window', 252 if model_type_lower == 's' else (504 if model_type_lower == 'm' else 5000)))

        hist_days: int = int(model_config.get('hist_chart_days', 360))

        enable_auto_adjust = common_params.get('enable_auto_window_adjustment', True)

        min_reliability_target = float(common_params.get('min_reliability_target', 100.0))

        window_step = int(common_params.get('window_adjustment_step', 100))

        min_window = int(common_params.get('min_fallback_window', 100))



        if not indicator_list: raise ValueError("指標列表 (Indicator list) 為空.") #<-- 中文

        if window_step <= 0: window_step = 100

        if min_window <= 10: min_window = 10



        # --- 2. 獲取並驗證數據 ---

        master_daily_data = data.get('master_daily_data')

        if not isinstance(master_daily_data, pd.DataFrame) or master_daily_data.empty:

            raise ValueError("輸入數據 'master_daily_data' 缺失或為空.") #<-- 中文



        # --- 修改：中文日誌 ---

        print(f"  -> 使用指標: {', '.join(indicator_list)}")

        print(f"  -> 初始標準化窗口: {initial_window} 天")

        if enable_auto_adjust: print(f"  -> 自動窗口調整: 已啟用 (目標 Reliability >= {min_reliability_target:.1f}%, 步長 {window_step}d, 最小 {min_window}d)")

        else: print(f"  -> 自動窗口調整: 已禁用")



        # --- 3. 準備指標計算器映射 (同前) ---

        indicator_calculators: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {}

        all_funcs_found = True

        price_series = master_daily_data.get('Adj Close', master_daily_data.get('Close'))

        if price_series is None: raise ValueError("在 master_daily_data 中未找到價格序列 ('Adj Close' 或 'Close').") #<-- 中文



        potential_calculators = { # (省略完整定義，假設與之前一致)

            "Price/MA50 Dev%": {'func_name': 'calculate_deviation_pct', 'params': {'series1': price_series, 'series2': lambda: calculate_sma(price_series, common_params.get('s_ma_period', 50))}, 'map': 'U-Shape'},

            "HV20": {'func_name': 'calculate_hist_vol', 'params': {'series': price_series, 'window': common_params.get('s_hv_period', 20)}, 'map': 'Direct'},

            "RSI(14)": {'func_name': 'calculate_rsi', 'params': {'series': price_series, 'period': common_params.get('s_rsi_period', 14)}, 'map': 'U-Shape'},

            "ROC(10)": {'func_name': 'calculate_roc', 'params': {'series': price_series, 'period': common_params.get('s_roc10_period', 10)}, 'map': 'U-Shape'},

            "ROC(20)": {'func_name': 'calculate_roc', 'params': {'series': price_series, 'period': common_params.get('s_roc20_period', 20)}, 'map': 'U-Shape'},

            "VIX Level": {'func_name': None, 'params': {'series': master_daily_data.get('VIX')}, 'map': 'Direct'},

            "Price/MA200 Dev%": {'func_name': 'calculate_deviation_pct', 'params': {'series1': price_series, 'series2': lambda: calculate_sma(price_series, common_params.get('m_ma_period', 200))}, 'map': 'U-Shape'},

            "ROC(120)": {'func_name': 'calculate_roc', 'params': {'series': price_series, 'period': common_params.get('m_roc_period', 120)}, 'map': 'U-Shape'},

            "VIX vs MA50 State": {'func_name': 'calculate_vix_vs_ma_state', 'params': {'vix_series': master_daily_data.get('VIX'), 'period': common_params.get('m_vix_ma_period', 50)}, 'map': 'U-Shape'},

            "Spread 10Y-3M": {'func_name': 'calculate_yield_spread', 'params': {'series1': master_daily_data.get('DGS10'), 'series2': master_daily_data.get('DTB3')}, 'map': 'Inverse'},

            "MOVE Level": {'func_name': None, 'params': {'series': master_daily_data.get('MOVE_Level')}, 'map': 'Direct'},

            "UNRATE 12m Change": {'func_name': 'calculate_unrate_change', 'params': {'unrate_series': master_daily_data.get('UNRATE'), 'period': common_params.get('l_unrate_change_period', 252)}, 'map': 'Direct'},

            "CPI YoY Trend": {'func_name': 'calculate_cpi_trend', 'params': {'cpi_yoy_series': master_daily_data.get('CPI_YoY'), 'period': common_params.get('l_cpi_ma_period', 252)}, 'map': 'Direct'},

            "VIX 12m Avg Level": {'func_name': 'calculate_vix_avg', 'params': {'vix_series': master_daily_data.get('VIX'), 'period': common_params.get('l_vix_avg_period', 252)}, 'map': 'Direct'},

            "Price > MA200 Status": {'func_name': 'calculate_price_vs_ma_status', 'params': {'price_series': price_series, 'period': common_params.get('l_ma_period', 200)}, 'map': 'SPECIAL_BOOL_INVERSE'},

        }



        for name in indicator_list:

             if name in potential_calculators:

                  info = potential_calculators[name]

                  func_name = info.get('func_name')

                  if func_name:

                      if func_name in globals() and callable(globals()[func_name]):

                           indicator_calculators[name] = {'func': globals()[func_name], 'params': info['params'], 'map': info['map']}

                      else:

                           print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] {function_name} 錯誤: 指標 '{name}' 需要的函數 '{func_name}' 未找到!") #<-- 中文

                           all_funcs_found = False; break

                  else:

                       if isinstance(info['params'].get('series'), pd.Series):

                            indicator_calculators[name] = {'func': lambda series: series, 'params': info['params'], 'map': info['map']}

                       else:

                            print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] {function_name} 錯誤: 指標 '{name}' 的源數據序列未找到或無效.") #<-- 中文

                            all_funcs_found = False; break

             else:

                  print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] {function_name} 錯誤: 配置列表中存在未知指標 '{name}'.") #<-- 中文

                  all_funcs_found = False; break

        if not all_funcs_found: return None



        # --- 4. 創建嘗試窗口列表 (同前) ---

        windows_to_try = []

        initial_window = max(initial_window, min_window)

        if enable_auto_adjust:

            current_w = initial_window

            while current_w >= min_window:

                windows_to_try.append(current_w)

                if current_w - window_step < min_window and current_w != min_window:

                     if min_window not in windows_to_try: windows_to_try.append(min_window)

                     break

                current_w -= window_step

        else: windows_to_try = [initial_window]

        windows_to_try = sorted(list(set(windows_to_try)), reverse=True)

        if not windows_to_try: raise ValueError("沒有有效的標準化窗口可供嘗試。") #<-- 中文

        print(f"  -> 嘗試的窗口 (Windows to try): {windows_to_try}") #<-- 中文



        # --- 5. 循環嘗試窗口大小 ---

        attempt_results = {}

        final_window_used = None

        loop_start_time = time.time()



        for current_window in windows_to_try:

            # --- 修改：中文日誌 ---

            print(f"\n  >> 正在嘗試窗口大小: {current_window} 天...")

            window_start_time_inner = time.time()

            component_scores_dict = {}

            indicators_valid_latest = {}



            # --- 5a. 計算、標準化、映射 ---

            for indicator_name in indicator_list:

                # ...(計算 risk_contribution 邏輯不變，內部錯誤已中文化)...

                 calc_info = indicator_calculators.get(indicator_name)

                 risk_contribution = pd.Series(np.nan, index=master_daily_data.index, dtype=float)

                 if calc_info:

                     try:

                         params_resolved = {}

                         params_ok = True

                         for p_name, p_value in calc_info['params'].items():

                              if callable(p_value) and not isinstance(p_value, (pd.Series, pd.DataFrame)):

                                   resolved_val = p_value()

                                   if resolved_val is None or (isinstance(resolved_val, pd.Series) and resolved_val.isna().all()):

                                        params_ok = False; break

                                   params_resolved[p_name] = resolved_val

                              else:

                                   if isinstance(p_value, pd.Series) and p_value.isna().all(): params_ok = False; break

                                   if p_value is None and p_name != 'vix_series': params_ok = False; break

                                   params_resolved[p_name] = p_value

                         if not params_ok: raise ValueError("輸入數據缺失或全為 NaN.")



                         raw_series = calc_info['func'](**params_resolved)



                         if raw_series is not None and isinstance(raw_series, pd.Series) and raw_series.notna().any():

                             mapping_type = calc_info.get('map', 'Direct')

                             if mapping_type == 'SPECIAL_BOOL_INVERSE':

                                 risk_contribution = raw_series.map({True: 0.0, False: 100.0}).astype(float)

                             else:

                                 percentile_series = get_rolling_percentile_rank(raw_series, window=current_window)

                                 risk_contribution = map_percentile_to_risk(percentile_series, mapping_type=mapping_type)

                     except Exception as e_ind_calc:

                         print(f"    ! 處理指標 '{indicator_name}' 時出錯: {e_ind_calc}") #<-- 中文

                         risk_contribution = pd.Series(np.nan, index=master_daily_data.index, dtype=float)

                 component_scores_dict[indicator_name] = risk_contribution

                 last_valid_idx = risk_contribution.last_valid_index()

                 indicators_valid_latest[indicator_name] = pd.notna(risk_contribution.get(last_valid_idx)) if last_valid_idx is not None else False



            # --- 5b. 計算可靠度 & 聚合 (同前) ---

            num_total_indicators = len(indicator_list)

            num_valid_latest = sum(1 for name in indicator_list if indicators_valid_latest.get(name, False))

            current_reliability = (num_valid_latest / num_total_indicators) * 100.0 if num_total_indicators > 0 else 0.0

            df_component_scores = pd.DataFrame(component_scores_dict).reindex(columns=indicator_list)

            valid_indicator_mask = df_component_scores.notna()

            num_valid_indicators_per_row = valid_indicator_mask.sum(axis=1)

            scores_sum_per_row = df_component_scores.sum(axis=1, skipna=True)

            current_hist_scores = pd.Series(np.nan, index=df_component_scores.index, dtype=float)

            valid_rows_mask = num_valid_indicators_per_row > 0

            if valid_rows_mask.any():

                 current_hist_scores.loc[valid_rows_mask] = (scores_sum_per_row[valid_rows_mask] / num_valid_indicators_per_row[valid_rows_mask]).clip(0, 100)

            current_hist_scores = current_hist_scores.round(2)

            current_score = current_hist_scores.dropna().iloc[-1] if not current_hist_scores.dropna().empty else np.nan



            # --- 5c. 記錄結果 ---

            attempt_results[current_window] = {'score': current_score, 'reliability': current_reliability, 'hist': current_hist_scores}

            score_str = f"{current_score:.1f}" if pd.notna(current_score) else 'NaN'

            window_end_time_inner = time.time()

            # --- 修改：中文日誌 ---

            print(f"    => 窗口(Window) {current_window}d: Reliability={current_reliability:.1f}%, 最新 Score={score_str} (耗時: {window_end_time_inner - window_start_time_inner:.2f}s)")



            # --- 5d. 檢查終止條件 ---

            if enable_auto_adjust:

                if current_reliability >= min_reliability_target:

                    # --- 修改：中文日誌 ---

                    print(f"    * 可靠度目標 (Reliability Target >= {min_reliability_target:.1f}%) 已達成。使用窗口(Using window) {current_window}d。")

                    final_window_used = current_window; break

            else: final_window_used = current_window; break



        # --- 循環結束 ---

        if final_window_used is None:

            final_window_used = windows_to_try[-1]

             # --- 修改：中文日誌 ---

            print(f"    ! 未達到可靠度目標或自動調整已禁用。使用最終嘗試的窗口(Using final tried window) {final_window_used}d。")



        if final_window_used in attempt_results:

            final_result_data = attempt_results[final_window_used]

            results['risk_score'] = float(final_result_data['score']) if pd.notna(final_result_data['score']) else np.nan

            results['reliability'] = float(final_result_data['reliability'])

            hist_series = final_result_data['hist']

            if hist_series is not None and not hist_series.dropna().empty:

                 results['history'] = hist_series.tail(hist_days) if hist_days and hist_days > 0 else hist_series

            results['details']['final_window_used'] = final_window_used

            results['details']['indicators_status'] = indicators_valid_latest

             # --- 修改：中文日誌 ---

            print(f"  -> {model_type_full} 最終結果: Score={results['risk_score']:.1f}, Reliability={results['reliability']:.1f}%, 窗口(Window)={final_window_used}d")

        else:

             raise ValueError("任何嘗試的窗口都沒有找到結果。") #<-- 中文



        return results



    except ValueError as ve:

        error_msg = f"數據/參數驗證錯誤: {ve}" #<-- 中文

        print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] {function_name} 錯誤: {error_msg}")

        results['details']['error'] = error_msg; results['reliability'] = 0.0

        return results

    except Exception as e_main:

        error_msg = f"未預期錯誤: {e_main}" #<-- 中文

        print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] {function_name} 錯誤: {error_msg}")

        traceback.print_exc(limit=2)

        results['details']['error'] = error_msg; results['reliability'] = 0.0

        return results





print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] Cell 16: Analyzer function 'run_sml_model' [日誌中文化 v1.2 - 完整] defined.")



"""### 17. 分析器定義 - 系統壓力指數 (Analyzer Def - Systemic Stress)"""



# 程式碼儲存格 17: 分析器定義 - 系統壓力指數 (Analyzer Def - Systemic Stress) [日誌中文化 v1.1]

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-



import pandas as pd

import numpy as np

from typing import Optional, Dict, Any, Tuple, List, Union, Callable # 確保導入 Union 和 Callable

from datetime import datetime

import traceback



# --- 假設依賴的函數和變數已在之前的儲存格定義 ---

# 需要 Cell 1 的: ACTIVE_CONFIG, DEFAULT_CONFIG

# 需要 Cell 3 的: get_nested_param

# 需要 Cell 4 的: calculate_sma, calculate_ema

# 需要 Cell 6 的: get_rolling_percentile_rank

# 需要 Cell 7 的: calculate_sofr_dev, calculate_yield_spread



print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] Cell 17: Analyzer Definition - Systemic Stress [日誌中文化 v1.1]...")



# --- MACD 計算函數 (確保存在) ---

# (假設 Cell 5 或其他地方已定義 calculate_macd)

if 'calculate_macd' not in globals():

    print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] Cell 17 警告: 未找到 calculate_macd 函數。將定義本地版本。")

    def calculate_macd(series: pd.Series, fast_period: int = 12, slow_period: int = 26, signal_period: int = 9) -> Tuple[pd.Series, pd.Series, pd.Series]:

        if 'calculate_ema' not in globals():

            print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] MACD 錯誤: 未找到 'calculate_ema' 函數。")

            nan_series = pd.Series(np.nan, index=getattr(series, 'index', None))

            return nan_series, nan_series, nan_series

        try:

            ema_fast = calculate_ema(series, period=fast_period, adjust=False)

            ema_slow = calculate_ema(series, period=slow_period, adjust=False)

            macd_line = ema_fast - ema_slow

            signal_line = calculate_ema(macd_line, period=signal_period, adjust=False)

            histogram = macd_line - signal_line

            return macd_line, signal_line, histogram

        except Exception as e:

            print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] 計算 MACD 時出錯: {e}")

            nan_series = pd.Series(np.nan, index=getattr(series, 'index', None))

            return nan_series, nan_series, nan_series





# --- 系統壓力分析主函數 ---

def run_systemic_analysis(data: Dict[str, Optional[Union[pd.DataFrame, pd.Series]]], config: Dict[str, Any]) -> Optional[pd.DataFrame]:

    """

    (分析器 Analyzer) 執行系統性壓力指數分析。

    [v1.1]: 將內部 print 日誌部分中文化。



    :param data: 包含輸入數據的字典... (其餘說明不變)

    :param config: 完整的 ACTIVE_CONFIG 字典。

    :return: Pandas DataFrame 或 None。

    """

    function_name = "run_systemic_analysis"
