    # --- 修改：中文日誌 ---

    print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] == 開始執行: {function_name} ==")



    # --- 檢查依賴 ---

    required_funcs = ['get_nested_param', 'calculate_sofr_dev', 'calculate_yield_spread',

                      'get_rolling_percentile_rank', 'calculate_sma', 'calculate_ema', 'calculate_macd']

    if not all(f in globals() for f in required_funcs) or 'DEFAULT_CONFIG' not in globals():

         missing = [f for f in required_funcs if f not in globals()] + (['DEFAULT_CONFIG'] if 'DEFAULT_CONFIG' not in globals() else [])

         # --- 修改：中文日誌 ---

         print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] {function_name} 錯誤: 缺少必要的函數或全局配置: {missing}")

         return None



    try:

        # --- 1. 獲取配置參數 ---

        sys_config = get_nested_param(config, DEFAULT_CONFIG, ['systemic_analysis'], {})

        weights_cfg = get_nested_param(sys_config, {}, ['stress_index_weights'], {})

        params_cfg = get_nested_param(sys_config, {}, ['stress_index_params'], {})

        macd_cfg = get_nested_param(sys_config, {}, ['macd_params'], {})



        window = params_cfg.get('rolling_window_days', 252)

        min_periods_rank = max(5, int(window * 0.6))

        smoothing_window = params_cfg.get('smoothing_window_stress_index', 5)

        threshold_ratio = params_cfg.get('threshold_ratio_color', 90)

        enable_macd = macd_cfg.get('enable_macd_momentum_plot', True)

        macd_fast = macd_cfg.get('fast_period', 12)

        macd_slow = macd_cfg.get('slow_period', 26)

        macd_signal = macd_cfg.get('signal_period', 9)

        macd_colors = {'blue': macd_cfg.get('color_blue', '#6495ED'),

                       'green': macd_cfg.get('color_green', '#3CB371'),

                       'red': macd_cfg.get('color_red', '#B22222')}



        # --- 2. 準備輸入數據 DataFrame ---

        # --- 修改：中文日誌 ---

        print("  -> 正在準備系統性分析所需數據...")

        series_list_to_concat = {}

        # ... (從 data 字典獲取 Series 的邏輯不變) ...

        sofr = data.get('sofr'); dgs10 = data.get('dgs10'); dgs2 = data.get('dgs2')

        move_series = data.get('move'); vix_series = data.get('vix')

        positions_df = data.get('nyfed_dealer'); reserves_series = data.get('reserves')

        if isinstance(sofr, pd.Series): series_list_to_concat['SOFR'] = sofr

        if isinstance(dgs10, pd.Series): series_list_to_concat['DGS10'] = dgs10

        if isinstance(dgs2, pd.Series): series_list_to_concat['DGS2'] = dgs2

        if isinstance(move_series, pd.DataFrame) and 'MOVE_Level' in move_series.columns: series_list_to_concat['MOVE_Level'] = move_series['MOVE_Level']

        elif isinstance(move_series, pd.Series): series_list_to_concat['MOVE_Level'] = move_series

        if isinstance(vix_series, pd.DataFrame) and 'VIX' in vix_series.columns: series_list_to_concat['VIX'] = vix_series['VIX']

        elif isinstance(vix_series, pd.Series): series_list_to_concat['VIX'] = vix_series

        if isinstance(positions_df, pd.DataFrame) and 'Total_Gross_Positions_Millions' in positions_df.columns: series_list_to_concat['Total_Gross_Positions_Millions'] = positions_df['Total_Gross_Positions_Millions']

        if isinstance(reserves_series, pd.DataFrame) and 'Reserves' in reserves_series.columns: series_list_to_concat['Reserves'] = reserves_series['Reserves']

        elif isinstance(reserves_series, pd.Series): series_list_to_concat['Reserves'] = reserves_series



        if not series_list_to_concat: raise ValueError("在輸入的 'data' 字典中未找到用於系統性分析的有效 Series。") #<-- 修改：中文錯誤



        work_df = pd.concat(series_list_to_concat, axis=1)

        work_df = work_df.ffill().bfill()

        # --- 修改：中文日誌 ---

        print(f"    - 已創建工作 DataFrame，包含欄位: {list(work_df.columns)}")



        # --- 3. 計算基礎衍生指標 ---

        # --- 修改：中文日誌 ---

        print("  -> 正在計算基礎衍生指標...")

        sofr_dev_series = calculate_sofr_dev(work_df.get('SOFR'), period=60)

        if sofr_dev_series is None or sofr_dev_series.isna().all():

            print("    ! 警告: 無法計算 SOFR 偏差 (SOFR_Dev)。") #<-- 修改：中文日誌

            work_df['SOFR_Dev'] = np.nan

        else: work_df['SOFR_Dev'] = sofr_dev_series



        spread_series = calculate_yield_spread(work_df.get('DGS10'), work_df.get('DGS2'))

        if spread_series is None or spread_series.isna().all():

             print("    ! 警告: 無法計算 10Y-2Y 利差 (Spread_10Y2Y)。") #<-- 修改：中文日誌

             work_df['Spread_10Y2Y'] = np.nan

        else: work_df['Spread_10Y2Y'] = spread_series



        if 'Total_Gross_Positions_Millions' in work_df and 'Reserves' in work_df:

             reserves_safe = work_df['Reserves'].replace(0, np.nan)

             if reserves_safe.notna().any(): # 確保分母有效

                  work_df['Pos_Res_Ratio'] = work_df['Total_Gross_Positions_Millions'] / reserves_safe

                  work_df['Pos_Res_Ratio'].replace([np.inf, -np.inf], np.nan, inplace=True)

             else: work_df['Pos_Res_Ratio'] = np.nan; print("    ! 警告: 無法計算持倉/儲備比率 (Reserves 數據無效)。") #<-- 修改：中文日誌

        else: work_df['Pos_Res_Ratio'] = np.nan; print("    ! 警告: 缺少計算持倉/儲備比率所需數據 (Positions 或 Reserves)。") #<-- 修改：中文日誌



        # --- 4. 計算各成分滾動百分位排名 (0-100) ---

        # --- 修改：中文日誌 ---

        print(f"  -> 正在計算滾動百分位排名 (窗口={window}d, 最小期數={min_periods_rank})...")

        perc_ranks = pd.DataFrame(index=work_df.index)

        component_map = { # 映射到 work_df 中的列名

            'sofr_dev': 'SOFR_Dev', 'spread_inv': 'Spread_10Y2Y', 'move': 'MOVE_Level',

            'vix': 'VIX', 'gross_pos': 'Total_Gross_Positions_Millions', 'pos_res_ratio': 'Pos_Res_Ratio'

        }

        available_components = {}



        for key, col_name in component_map.items():

            if col_name in work_df and work_df[col_name].notna().any():

                 rank_series = get_rolling_percentile_rank(work_df[col_name], window=window, min_periods=min_periods_rank)

                 if rank_series.notna().any():

                      # 注意：spread_inv 需要反轉百分位 (數值越低風險越高 -> 排名越低風險越高 -> 反轉後分數越高風險越高)

                      perc_ranks[key] = (100.0 - rank_series) if key == 'spread_inv' else rank_series

                      available_components[key] = True

                 else: print(f"    ! 警告: 指標 '{col_name}' 的百分位排名計算結果全為 NaN。") #<-- 修改：中文日誌

            # else: print(f"    - 信息: 指標 '{col_name}' 數據不足或不存在，跳過百分位排名。") #<-- 修改：中文日誌 (可選)



        # --- 5. 計算加權原始壓力指數 ---

         # --- 修改：中文日誌 ---

        print("  -> 正在計算加權原始壓力指數...")

        weights = {k: weights_cfg.get(comp_key_in_cfg, 0)

                   for k, comp_key_in_cfg in zip(available_components.keys(), # 只考慮實際可用的成分

                                                 ['SOFR_Dev', 'Spread_10Y2Y_Inv', 'MOVE_Level', 'VIX',

                                                  'Total_Gross_Positions_Millions', 'Pos_Res_Ratio_Cond'])}



        active_components = {k: w for k, w in weights.items() if available_components.get(k, False) and w > 0}

        if not active_components: raise ValueError("沒有帶正權重的可用成分來計算指數。") #<-- 修改：中文錯誤



        total_weight = sum(active_components.values());

        if total_weight <= 1e-9: raise ValueError("啟用的權重總和為零。") #<-- 修改：中文錯誤

        normalized_weights = {k: w / total_weight for k, w in active_components.items()}

         # --- 修改：中文日誌 ---

        print(f"    - 啟用的成分及歸一化權重: { {k: f'{w:.2%}' for k, w in normalized_weights.items()} }")



        raw_index = pd.Series(0.0, index=work_df.index); actual_weight_sum = pd.Series(0.0, index=work_df.index)

        for name, weight in normalized_weights.items():

            if name in perc_ranks.columns:

                 # 使用 clip 確保排名在 0-100，fillna(50) 將缺失排名視為中性

                 rank_series = perc_ranks[name].clip(0, 100).fillna(50.0)

                 if name == 'pos_res_ratio': # 條件權重

                     original_ratio = work_df.get('Pos_Res_Ratio', pd.Series(np.nan, index=work_df.index))

                     # 只有在比率大於閾值且排名有效時才貢獻權重

                     condition_met_mask = (original_ratio >= threshold_ratio) & perc_ranks[name].notna()

                     raw_index.loc[condition_met_mask] += rank_series.loc[condition_met_mask] * weight

                     actual_weight_sum.loc[condition_met_mask] += weight

                 else: # 直接貢獻

                     condition_met_mask = perc_ranks[name].notna()

                     raw_index.loc[condition_met_mask] += rank_series.loc[condition_met_mask] * weight

                     actual_weight_sum.loc[condition_met_mask] += weight



        actual_weight_sum.replace(0, np.nan, inplace=True) # 避免除以零

        work_df['Dealer_Stress_Index_Raw'] = (raw_index / actual_weight_sum).clip(0, 100) # 不再乘以 100



        # --- 6. 指數平滑 ---

        # --- 修改：中文日誌 ---

        print(f"  -> 正在平滑原始指數 (窗口={smoothing_window}d)...")

        if smoothing_window > 1:

            min_periods_smooth = max(1, int(smoothing_window * 0.5))

            work_df['Dealer_Stress_Index'] = work_df['Dealer_Stress_Index_Raw'].rolling(window=smoothing_window, min_periods=min_periods_smooth, center=True).mean().clip(0, 100)

        else:

            work_df['Dealer_Stress_Index'] = work_df['Dealer_Stress_Index_Raw']



        stress_index_calculated = work_df['Dealer_Stress_Index'].notna().any()

        if not stress_index_calculated: print("    ! 警告: 最終壓力指數全為 NaN。") #<-- 修改：中文日誌

        else: print(f"    - 最終壓力指數已計算 ({work_df['Dealer_Stress_Index'].count()} 個數據點).") #<-- 修改：中文日誌



        # --- 7. 計算 MACD ---

        work_df['Stress_Index_MACD'] = np.nan; work_df['Stress_Index_MACD_Signal'] = np.nan

        work_df['Stress_Index_MACD_Hist'] = np.nan; work_df['Stress_Index_MACD_Color'] = 'grey'

        if enable_macd and stress_index_calculated:

             # --- 修改：中文日誌 ---

            print(f"  -> 正在計算壓力指數的 MACD ({macd_fast},{macd_slow},{macd_signal})...")

            macd_line, signal_line, histogram = calculate_macd(

                work_df['Dealer_Stress_Index'], macd_fast, macd_slow, macd_signal

            )

            work_df['Stress_Index_MACD'] = macd_line; work_df['Stress_Index_MACD_Signal'] = signal_line

            work_df['Stress_Index_MACD_Hist'] = histogram



            if histogram.notna().any():

                hist_diff = histogram.diff()

                conditions = [ (hist_diff > 0) & (histogram >= 0), (hist_diff > 0) & (histogram < 0), (hist_diff <= 0) ]

                colors = [macd_colors['blue'], macd_colors['green'], macd_colors['red']]

                work_df['Stress_Index_MACD_Color'] = np.select(conditions, colors, default='grey')

                first_valid_idx = histogram.first_valid_index()

                if first_valid_idx is not None:

                     first_val = histogram[first_valid_idx]

                     work_df.loc[first_valid_idx, 'Stress_Index_MACD_Color'] = macd_colors['blue'] if first_val >= 0 else macd_colors['green']

                # --- 修改：中文日誌 ---

                print("    - MACD 計算與著色完成。")

            else: print("    ! 警告: MACD Histogram 全為 NaN。") #<-- 修改：中文日誌

        else: print("  -> 跳過 MACD 計算。") #<-- 修改：中文日誌



        # 添加百分位排名到輸出 DataFrame (可選)

        perc_ranks.columns = [f"{col}_PercRank" for col in perc_ranks.columns]

        work_df = pd.concat([work_df, perc_ranks], axis=1)



        # --- 修改：中文日誌 ---

        print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] == {function_name} 已完成. ==")

        return work_df.copy()



    except ValueError as ve:

         # --- 修改：中文日誌 ---

        print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] {function_name} 錯誤: {ve}")

        traceback.print_exc(limit=2); return None

    except Exception as e_main:

         # --- 修改：中文日誌 ---

        print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] {function_name} 錯誤: 未預期錯誤: {e_main}")

        traceback.print_exc(limit=2); return None





print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] Cell 17: Analyzer function 'run_systemic_analysis' [日誌中文化 v1.1] defined.")



"""---



### 23. 繪圖器定義 - C1 PNG 導出 (Plotter Def - C1 PNG Export)

"""



# 程式碼儲存格 23: 繪圖器定義 - C1 PNG 導出 (Plotter Def - C1 PNG Export) [v1.1 - 加入 Smoothing Debug]

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-



import pandas as pd

import numpy as np

from typing import Optional, Dict, Any, Tuple, List

from datetime import datetime

import traceback

import os



# --- 假設依賴的庫、函數和變數已在之前的儲存格定義 ---

# 需要 Cell 0 的: LIB_STATUS (檢查 plotly, kaleido, pillow), go (如果下面重新導入)

# 需要 Cell 1 的: ACTIVE_CONFIG, DEFAULT_CONFIG

# 需要 Cell 2 的: CACHE_DIR_CELL2 (用於存放臨時文件)

# 需要 Cell 3 的: get_nested_param

# 需要 Cell 18 的: create_plotly_gauge

# 需要 Cell 19 的: plot_plotly_trend

# 需要導入: from PIL import Image as PIL_Image (如果 Cell 0 導入不足)

# 確保 os 模塊已導入 (通常在 Cell 0 完成)

if 'os' not in globals(): import os

if 'PIL_Image' not in globals():

    try:

        from PIL import Image as PIL_Image

        PIL_OK = True

    except ImportError:

        PIL_OK = False

else: PIL_OK = True # Assume OK if already imported



# --- 再次檢查依賴庫的可用性 ---

PLOTLY_OK = globals().get('LIB_STATUS', {}).get('plotly', False) and 'go' in globals()

KALEIDO_OK = globals().get('LIB_STATUS', {}).get('kaleido', False)

PIL_OK = PIL_OK and globals().get('LIB_STATUS', {}).get('pillow', False) # Check status flag too



if not (PLOTLY_OK and KALEIDO_OK and PIL_OK):

     print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] Cell 23 Warning: Missing dependencies for PNG export (Plotly={PLOTLY_OK}, Kaleido={KALEIDO_OK}, Pillow={PIL_OK}). Defining empty functions.")

     # 定義空函數以避免 NameError

     def combine_images_grid(*args, **kwargs) -> bool: return False

     def generate_combined_png_export(*args, **kwargs) -> Optional[str]: return None

else:

    print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] Cell 23: Plotter Definition - C1 PNG Export [v1.1 - Added Smoothing Debug]...") # v1.1



    # --- 輔助函數：合併圖片網格 (移植自 V6, 無需修改) ---

    def combine_images_grid(

        image_paths_dict: Dict[str, Optional[str]], # 字典，鍵如 'vs_gauge', 值為圖片路徑

        output_path: str,                           # 最終合併圖片的保存路徑

        grid_size: Tuple[int, int] = (4, 2),      # 網格佈局 (行, 列)

        img_size: Tuple[int, int] = (400, 350),   # 單個子圖的目標尺寸

        border: int = 15,                           # 子圖間的邊框寬度

        bg_color: str = 'white'                     # 合併圖的背景色

    ) -> bool:

        """(內部輔助) 將多張圖片按預設順序合併成網格圖。"""

        if not PIL_OK or 'PIL_Image' not in globals():

             print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] Combine Grid Error: Pillow (PIL) library not available.")

             return False

        if not image_paths_dict:

             print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] Combine Grid Error: No image paths provided.")

             return False

        valid_paths = {k: p for k, p in image_paths_dict.items() if p and isinstance(p, str) and os.path.isfile(p)}

        if not valid_paths:

            print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] Combine Grid Error: No valid image files found.")

            return False

        ordered_keys = ['vs_gauge', 'vs_trend', 's_gauge', 's_trend', 'm_gauge', 'm_trend', 'l_gauge', 'l_trend']

        rows, cols = grid_size; img_w, img_h = img_size

        final_w = cols * img_w + (cols + 1) * border; final_h = rows * img_h + (rows + 1) * border

        try: combined_image = PIL_Image.new('RGB', (final_w, final_h), color=bg_color)

        except Exception as e_create: print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] Combine Grid Error: Failed to create blank image: {e_create}"); return False

        try:

            key_idx = 0

            for r in range(rows):

                for c in range(cols):

                    if key_idx >= len(ordered_keys): break

                    key = ordered_keys[key_idx]; key_idx += 1

                    paste_x = c * (img_w + border) + border; paste_y = r * (img_h + border) + border

                    img_path = valid_paths.get(key)

                    if img_path:

                        try:

                            img = PIL_Image.open(img_path)

                            if img.size != (img_w, img_h): img = img.resize((img_w, img_h), PIL_Image.Resampling.LANCZOS)

                            combined_image.paste(img, (paste_x, paste_y)); img.close()

                        except Exception as e_paste: print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] Combine Grid Warning: Failed to process/paste image {key} ({os.path.basename(img_path)}): {e_paste}")

                if key_idx >= len(ordered_keys): break

            combined_image.save(output_path)

            print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] Combined image saved successfully: {output_path}")

            return True

        except Exception as e_combine: print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] Combine Grid Error: Failed during image combining process: {e_combine}"); return False

        finally:

             if 'combined_image' in locals(): combined_image.close()





    # --- 主函數：生成合併的 PNG 導出 (加入偵錯 Print) ---

    def generate_combined_png_export(

        analysis_results: Dict[str, Any], # 包含 risk_vs, risk_sml_s 等鍵的字典

        config: Dict[str, Any], # ACTIVE_CONFIG

        output_dir: Optional[str] = None # 輸出目錄，None 則使用快取目錄或當前目錄

        ) -> Optional[str]:

        """

        (繪圖工具) 生成 C1 Stage 2 風險模型的合併 PNG 儀表板。

        強制使用英文標籤和指定尺寸。

        [v1.1]: 加入偵錯 Print 語句檢查 smoothing_period。



        :param analysis_results: 包含 'risk_vs', 'risk_sml_s', 'risk_sml_m', 'risk_sml_l' 鍵的字典...

        :param config: ACTIVE_CONFIG。

        :param output_dir: 保存最終 PNG 的目錄。

        :return: 最終 PNG 文件的路徑，如果失敗則返回 None。

        """

        function_name = "generate_combined_png_export"

        print(f"\n[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] Executing: {function_name}...")



        # --- 依賴和配置檢查 (同前) ---

        if not (PLOTLY_OK and KALEIDO_OK and PIL_OK): print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] {function_name} Error: Missing dependencies (Plotly/Kaleido/Pillow). Cannot generate PNG."); return None

        if 'create_plotly_gauge' not in globals() or 'plot_plotly_trend' not in globals() or 'combine_images_grid' not in globals(): print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] {function_name} Error: Missing required plotting or combining functions."); return None

        png_cfg = get_nested_param(config, DEFAULT_CONFIG, ['plotting', 'stage2_png'], {})

        # *** 注意：這裡不再檢查 enable_option，因為調用此函數的前提就是用戶選擇了 'y' ***

        # if not png_cfg.get('enable_option', False): print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] {function_name} Info: PNG export is disabled in configuration."); return None



        # --- 獲取參數 (同前) ---

        png_w = png_cfg.get('single_width', 400); png_h = png_cfg.get('single_height', 350)

        png_rows = png_cfg.get('layout_rows', 4); png_cols = png_cfg.get('layout_cols', 2)

        png_border = png_cfg.get('combine_border', 15)

        target_ticker = get_nested_param(config, DEFAULT_CONFIG, ['general', 'target_ticker'], 'default')

        safe_ticker_name = target_ticker.replace('^','').replace('/','_')



        # --- 確定輸出目錄和檔名 (同前) ---

        if output_dir is None: output_dir = globals().get('CACHE_DIR_CELL2', ".")

        if not os.path.exists(output_dir):

            try: os.makedirs(output_dir)

            except OSError: output_dir = "."

        temp_png_dir = os.path.join(output_dir, f"temp_png_{safe_ticker_name}_{datetime.now().strftime('%H%M%S')}")

        try: os.makedirs(temp_png_dir)

        except OSError: print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] {function_name} Warning: Cannot create temporary directory '{temp_png_dir}'. Using output directory."); temp_png_dir = output_dir

        final_output_path = os.path.join(output_dir, f"risk_dashboard_s2_{safe_ticker_name}_EN.png")



        # --- 重新生成英文圖表並保存為臨時 PNG ---

        temp_image_paths: Dict[str, Optional[str]] = {k: None for k in ['vs_gauge', 'vs_trend', 's_gauge', 's_trend', 'm_gauge', 'm_trend', 'l_gauge', 'l_trend']}

        # *** 使用 Cell 31 修正後的鍵名 ***

        models_to_plot = [('vs', 'risk_vs'), ('s', 'risk_sml_s'), ('m', 'risk_sml_m'), ('l', 'risk_sml_l')]

        overall_success = True



        print("  -> Generating temporary English PNG files...")

        for model_key_short, result_key in models_to_plot:

            model_results = analysis_results.get(result_key)

            if not isinstance(model_results, dict):

                 print(f"    ! Warning: Results for '{result_key}' not found or invalid. Skipping its plots.")

                 continue # 跳過這個模型的繪圖



            score = model_results.get('risk_score'); reli = model_results.get('reliability', 0.0)

            hist = model_results.get('history'); details = model_results.get('details', {})

            final_window = details.get('final_window_used') # SML 可能有



            # -- 生成 Gauge --

            gauge_title_key_map = {'vs':'VS', 's':'Short-Term (S)', 'm':'Mid-Term (M)', 'l':'Long-Term (L)'}

            gauge_main_title = f"{gauge_title_key_map.get(model_key_short, model_key_short.upper())} Score"

            temp_gauge_path = os.path.join(temp_png_dir, f"temp_{model_key_short}_gauge_en.png")

            try:

                fig_g = create_plotly_gauge(risk_score=score, reliability=reli, main_title=gauge_main_title, force_lang='en', figure_width=png_w, figure_height=png_h)

                if fig_g: fig_g.write_image(temp_gauge_path, engine='kaleido', width=png_w, height=png_h, scale=1); temp_image_paths[f"{model_key_short}_gauge"] = temp_gauge_path

                else: raise ValueError("create_plotly_gauge returned None")

            except Exception as e_write_g: print(f"    ! Error generating/saving {model_key_short} gauge PNG: {e_write_g}"); overall_success = False



            # -- 生成 Trend --

            trend_title = f"{gauge_title_key_map.get(model_key_short, model_key_short.upper())} Trend"

            if final_window: trend_title += f" (Window: {final_window}d)"

            temp_trend_path = os.path.join(temp_png_dir, f"temp_{model_key_short}_trend_en.png")

            series_t = 'hourly' if model_key_short == 'vs' else 'daily'

            smoothing_t = get_nested_param(config, DEFAULT_CONFIG, ['plotting','stage2_plotly','smoothing_type'], 'EMA')



            # *** 獲取平滑週期 (從 details 或 config) ***

            smoothing_p = details.get('smoothing_period') # 嘗試從 analysis_results 的 details 獲取

            if smoothing_p is None: # 如果 details 中沒有，則從 config 獲取

                s2_model_cfg = get_nested_param(config, DEFAULT_CONFIG, ['stage2_risk_models_v1', model_key_short], {})

                if series_t == 'hourly': smoothing_p = s2_model_cfg.get('smoothing_period_hours', 10)

                else: smoothing_p = s2_model_cfg.get('smoothing_period_days', 5)



            if isinstance(hist, pd.Series) and not hist.empty:

                 try:

                     # *** 新增偵錯 Print：檢查傳遞的平滑週期 ***

                     print(f"  [DEBUG Cell 23] Plotting trend for '{model_key_short}'. Passing smoothing_period = {smoothing_p}")



                     fig_t = plot_plotly_trend(risk_score_series=hist, title=trend_title,

                                               smoothing_type=smoothing_t,

                                               smoothing_period=smoothing_p, #<- 傳遞獲取到的平滑週期

                                               series_type=series_t, force_lang='en',

                                               figure_width=png_w, figure_height=png_h)

                     if fig_t: fig_t.write_image(temp_trend_path, engine='kaleido', width=png_w, height=png_h, scale=1); temp_image_paths[f"{model_key_short}_trend"] = temp_trend_path

                     else: raise ValueError("plot_plotly_trend returned None")

                 except Exception as e_write_t: print(f"    ! Error generating/saving {model_key_short} trend PNG: {e_write_t}"); overall_success = False



        # --- 合併圖片 ---

        if not any(p for p in temp_image_paths.values() if p): print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] {function_name} Error: No temporary PNG files were generated. Cannot combine."); overall_success = False

        elif overall_success:

             print("  -> Combining temporary PNG files...")

             combine_ok = combine_images_grid(image_paths_dict=temp_image_paths, output_path=final_output_path, grid_size=(png_rows, png_cols), img_size=(png_w, png_h), border=png_border)

             if not combine_ok: overall_success = False



        # --- 清理臨時文件 ---

        print("  -> Cleaning up temporary PNG files...")

        cleaned_count = 0

        for path in temp_image_paths.values():

            if path and os.path.exists(path):

                try: os.remove(path); cleaned_count += 1

                except OSError as e_clean: print(f"    ! Warning: Failed to remove temp file '{os.path.basename(path)}': {e_clean}")

        if temp_png_dir != output_dir:

            try:

                if not os.listdir(temp_png_dir): os.rmdir(temp_png_dir)

            except OSError as e_rmdir: print(f"    ! Warning: Failed to remove temporary directory '{temp_png_dir}': {e_rmdir}")

        print(f"    - Cleaned up {cleaned_count} temporary files.")



        # --- 返回最終結果 ---

        if overall_success and os.path.exists(final_output_path):

            print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] {function_name} completed successfully. Output: {final_output_path}")

            return final_output_path

        else:

            print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] {function_name} failed to generate the combined PNG.")

            return None





print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] Cell 23: PNG Export functions defined [v1.1 - Added Smoothing Debug].")



"""---



24. 分析流程協調函數 - 基礎分析

"""



# 程式碼儲存格 24: 分析流程協調函數 - 基礎分析 (Orchestration Func - Basic)

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-



import pandas as pd

import numpy as np

from typing import Optional, Dict, Any, Tuple # 引入 Tuple

from datetime import datetime

import traceback



# --- 假設依賴的函數和變數已在之前的儲存格定義 ---

# 需要 Cell 1 的: ACTIVE_CONFIG, DEFAULT_CONFIG, DO_RUN_STAGE1, DO_PLOT_STAGE1 (控制標誌)

# 需要 Cell 3 的: get_nested_param

# 需要 Cell 8 的: fetch_yf_daily

# 需要 Cell 10 的: fetch_fred_data

# 需要 Cell 14 的: run_stage1_analysis

# 需要 Cell 18 的: plot_stage1_four_gauges



print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] Cell 24: Orchestration Function Definition - Basic Analysis...")



def run_basic_analysis(config: Dict[str, Any]) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:

    """

    (流程協調函數) 執行基礎分析流程。

    主要包括：抓取 Stage 1 所需數據 -> 執行 Stage 1 分析 -> 繪製 Stage 1 圖表。



    :param config: 完整的 ACTIVE_CONFIG 字典。

    :return: 包含基礎分析結果的字典，或在失敗時返回 None。

    """

    function_name = "run_basic_analysis"

    print(f"\n[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] Starting: {function_name}...")

    start_time = datetime.now()



    # --- 初始化結果字典 ---

    basic_run_results = {

        "stage1_results": None,

        "stage1_reliability": 0.0,

        "status": "Not Run",

        "error": None

    }



    # --- 檢查執行開關 ---

    # 從 Cell 1 提取的控制標誌 (假設已在全局)

    if not globals().get('DO_RUN_STAGE1', False):

         print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] {function_name} Skipped: 'run_stage1_eval' is disabled in execution_control.")

         basic_run_results["status"] = "Skipped (Disabled)"

         return basic_run_results



    # --- 檢查依賴函數 ---

    required_funcs = ['fetch_yf_daily', 'fetch_fred_data', 'run_stage1_analysis', 'plot_stage1_four_gauges', 'get_nested_param']

    if not all(f in globals() for f in required_funcs):

         missing = [f for f in required_funcs if f not in globals()]

         error_msg = f"Missing required functions: {missing}"

         print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] {function_name} Error: {error_msg}")

         basic_run_results["status"] = "Failed"

         basic_run_results["error"] = error_msg

         return basic_run_results



    try:

        # --- 1. 數據抓取 ---

        print("\n  -> [Basic] Step 1: Fetching data for Stage 1...")

        fetch_results = {}

        master_data_basic = None



        # 提取必要的配置

        ticker = get_nested_param(config, DEFAULT_CONFIG, ['general', 'target_ticker'])

        start_date = get_nested_param(config, DEFAULT_CONFIG, ['general', 'start_date_input'])

        end_date = get_nested_param(config, DEFAULT_CONFIG, ['general', 'end_date_input'])

        stage1_cfg = get_nested_param(config, DEFAULT_CONFIG, ['stage1_basic'], {})

        fetch_days_yf = stage1_cfg.get('fetch_days_daily_yf', 365)

        fetch_days_fred = stage1_cfg.get('fetch_days_fred', 90)

        force_reload_flag = config.get('force_reload_all', False) # 假設有一個全局強制刷新標誌



        # 計算抓取起始日期 (留一些 buffer)

        eff_end_date = pd.to_datetime(end_date) if end_date else datetime.now(globals().get('taiwan_tz', timezone.utc))

        start_date_yf = (eff_end_date - timedelta(days=fetch_days_yf + 30)).strftime('%Y-%m-%d')

        start_date_fred = (eff_end_date - timedelta(days=fetch_days_fred + 10)).strftime('%Y-%m-%d')



        # 抓取 YF Daily

        yf_cfg = {'ticker': ticker, 'start_date': start_date_yf, 'end_date': end_date, 'force_reload': force_reload_flag}

        yf_df = fetch_yf_daily(yf_cfg)

        if yf_df is not None: fetch_results['yf_daily'] = yf_df



        # 抓取 FRED (VIX, DGS10)

        fred_ids_s1 = ['VIXCLS', 'DGS10'] # Stage 1 需要的

        fred_cfg = {'series_ids': fred_ids_s1, 'start_date': start_date_fred, 'end_date': end_date, 'force_reload': force_reload_flag}

        fred_df = fetch_fred_data(fred_cfg)

        if fred_df is not None: fetch_results['fred_s1'] = fred_df



        # --- 數據整合 (基礎版) ---

        print("\n  -> [Basic] Step 2: Integrating data...")

        if 'yf_daily' in fetch_results:

            master_data_basic = fetch_results['yf_daily'].copy()

            if 'fred_s1' in fetch_results:

                 # 合併 FRED 數據 (只需 VIX, DGS10)

                 fred_to_join = fetch_results['fred_s1'][['VIXCLS', 'DGS10']].rename(columns={'VIXCLS':'VIX'})

                 master_data_basic = master_data_basic.join(fred_to_join, how='left')

                 # 簡單填充 (向前再向後)

                 master_data_basic[['VIX', 'DGS10']] = master_data_basic[['VIX', 'DGS10']].ffill().bfill()

            else: # 確保列存在

                 master_data_basic['VIX'] = np.nan

                 master_data_basic['DGS10'] = np.nan



            if master_data_basic.empty or master_data_basic[['Adj Close','High','Low','Close','VIX','DGS10']].isna().all().all():

                 raise ValueError("Basic data integration resulted in empty or all-NaN essential data.")

            print("    - Basic data integration successful.")

        else:

             raise ValueError("Failed to fetch essential YF daily data for basic analysis.")



        # --- 2. 執行 Stage 1 分析 ---

        print("\n  -> [Basic] Step 3: Running Stage 1 Analysis...")

        data_for_analyzer = {'master_daily_data': master_data_basic}

        s1_results, s1_reliability = run_stage1_analysis(data_for_analyzer, config)



        basic_run_results['stage1_results'] = s1_results

        basic_run_results['stage1_reliability'] = s1_reliability



        if s1_results is None:

            raise ValueError("Stage 1 analysis function returned None.")

        print("    - Stage 1 analysis successful.")



        # --- 3. 繪製 Stage 1 圖表 ---

        # 檢查繪圖開關 (從 Cell 1 讀取的全局變數)

        if globals().get('DO_PLOT_STAGE1', False):

             print("\n  -> [Basic] Step 4: Plotting Stage 1 Gauges...")

             s1_plot_cfg = get_nested_param(config, DEFAULT_CONFIG, ['plotting', 'stage1_plotly'], {})

             lang_cfg = get_nested_param(config, DEFAULT_CONFIG, ['general', 'language'], 'zh')



             fig_s1 = plot_stage1_four_gauges(

                 stage1_results=s1_results,

                 reliability=s1_reliability,

                 lang=lang_cfg, # 使用配置中的語言

                 # force_lang=None, # 基本分析通常不需要強制英文

                 figure_width=s1_plot_cfg.get('figure_width', 800),

                 figure_height=s1_plot_cfg.get('figure_height', 600),

                 number_fontsize=s1_plot_cfg.get('number_fontsize', 30)

             )

             if fig_s1:

                  # 在 Notebook 中顯示 Plotly 圖表需要 display

                  try:

                      from IPython.display import display

                      display(fig_s1)

                      print("    - Stage 1 plot displayed.")

                  except ImportError:

                       print("    ! Warning: IPython.display not available to show Plotly chart inline.")

             else:

                  print("    ! Warning: Failed to generate Stage 1 plot.")

        else:

             print("\n  -> [Basic] Step 4: Plotting Stage 1 Gauges skipped (disabled in config).")



        basic_run_results["status"] = "Success"



    except Exception as e_main:

        error_msg = f"Error during basic analysis: {e_main}"

        print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] {function_name} Error: {error_msg}")

        traceback.print_exc(limit=2)

        basic_run_results["status"] = "Failed"

        basic_run_results["error"] = error_msg



    end_time = datetime.now()

    duration = (end_time - start_time).total_seconds()

    print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] {function_name} Finished. Status: {basic_run_results['status']}. Duration: {duration:.2f}s")



    return basic_run_results # 返回包含結果的字典





print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] Cell 24: Orchestration function 'run_basic_analysis' defined.")



"""### 25. 分析流程協調函數 - 進階分析 (Orchestration Func - Advanced)"""



# 程式碼儲存格 25: 分析流程協調函數 - 進階分析 (Orchestration Func - Advanced) [v1.4 - Status 改為英文]

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-



import pandas as pd

import numpy as np

from typing import Optional, Dict, Any, Tuple, List

from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone # 確保導入

import traceback



# --- 假設依賴的函數和變數已在之前的儲存格定義 ---

# ... (依賴列表同 v1.2) ...



print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] Cell 25: Orchestration Function Definition - Advanced Analysis [v1.4 - Status to English]...") # v1.4



def run_advanced_analysis(config: Dict[str, Any]) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:

    """

    (流程協調函數) 執行進階分析流程。

    包含基礎分析 (Stage 1) 和完整的 Stage 2 (VS, S, M, L) 分析與繪圖。

    [v1.4]: 將返回 status 改為英文 "Success"/"Failed"。日誌中文化。

    """

    function_name = "run_advanced_analysis"

    # --- 修改：中文日誌 ---

    print(f"\n[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] == 開始執行: {function_name} ==")

    start_time = datetime.now()



    # --- 初始化結果字典 ---

    advanced_run_results = {

        "stage1_results": None, "stage1_reliability": 0.0,

        "vs_results": None, "s_results": None, "m_results": None, "l_results": None,

        "master_daily_data": None,

        "hourly_data": None,

        "status": "Not Run", #<- 初始狀態

        "error": None,

        "duration": 0.0

    }



    # --- 檢查依賴函數 ---

    required_funcs = [

        'fetch_yf_daily', 'fetch_yf_hourly', 'fetch_fred_data', 'fetch_move_index',

        'run_stage1_analysis', 'run_vs_model', 'run_sml_model',

        'create_plotly_gauge', 'plot_stage1_four_gauges', 'plot_plotly_trend',

        'get_nested_param'

    ]

    if not all(f in globals() for f in required_funcs):

         missing = [f for f in required_funcs if f not in globals()]

         # --- 修改：中文日誌 ---

         error_msg = f"缺少必要的函數: {missing}"

         print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] {function_name} 錯誤: {error_msg}")

         # *** 修改: 狀態改為英文 ***

         advanced_run_results["status"] = "Failed (Missing Dependencies)"; advanced_run_results["error"] = error_msg

         return advanced_run_results



    try:

        # --- 1. 數據抓取 ---

        # --- 修改：中文日誌 ---

        print("\n  -> [進階] 步驟 1: 正在抓取 Stage 1 & S/M/L 所需數據...")

        fetch_results = {}

        master_daily_data = None



        # 提取配置 (日誌保持英文以利追溯)

        ticker = get_nested_param(config, DEFAULT_CONFIG, ['general', 'target_ticker'], 'UNKNOWN')

        start_date = get_nested_param(config, DEFAULT_CONFIG, ['general', 'start_date_input'])

        end_date = get_nested_param(config, DEFAULT_CONFIG, ['general', 'end_date_input'])

        force_reload_flag = config.get('force_reload_all', False)



        # 確定日線抓取的最早起始日期 (日誌保持英文)

        sml_lookback_days = 3000

        try: tz = globals().get('taiwan_tz', timezone.utc)

        except NameError: tz = timezone.utc

        eff_end_date = pd.to_datetime(end_date) if end_date else datetime.now(tz)

        start_date_daily_fetch = (eff_end_date - timedelta(days=sml_lookback_days)).strftime('%Y-%m-%d')

        config_start_dt = pd.to_datetime(start_date) if start_date else None

        if config_start_dt and pd.to_datetime(start_date_daily_fetch) < config_start_dt:

             start_date_daily_fetch = start_date



        # 抓取 YF Daily (函數內部的日誌目前還是英文)

        yf_cfg = {'ticker': ticker, 'start_date': start_date_daily_fetch, 'end_date': end_date, 'force_reload': force_reload_flag}

        yf_df = fetch_yf_daily(yf_cfg)

        if yf_df is not None: fetch_results['yf_daily'] = yf_df

        else: raise ValueError(f"無法抓取必要的 YF 日線數據 for {ticker}") #<-- 修改：中文錯誤



        # 抓取 FRED (函數內部的日誌目前還是英文)

        print("    -> 正在收集 FRED IDs for Stage1, SML, and Systemic analysis...") #<-- 修改：中文日誌

        fred_ids_s1 = ['VIXCLS', 'DGS10']

        s2_config = get_nested_param(config, DEFAULT_CONFIG, ['stage2_risk_models_v1'], {})

        fred_ids_sml = []

        for model_key in ['s', 'm', 'l']:

             # ...(省略 SML ID 收集邏輯)...

             indicators = get_nested_param(s2_config, {}, [model_key, 'indicators'], [])

             if "Spread 10Y-3M" in indicators: fred_ids_sml.extend(['DGS10', 'DTB3'])

             if "UNRATE 12m Change" in indicators: fred_ids_sml.append('UNRATE')

             if "CPI YoY Trend" in indicators: fred_ids_sml.append('CPIAUCSL')

             if any(ind in ["VIX vs MA50 State", "VIX 12m Avg Level"] for ind in indicators): fred_ids_sml.append('VIXCLS')

        systemic_fred_series_config = get_nested_param(config, DEFAULT_CONFIG, ['systemic_analysis', 'fred_series'], {})

        fred_ids_systemic = list(systemic_fred_series_config.values())

        all_fred_ids = list(set(fred_ids_s1 + fred_ids_sml + fred_ids_systemic))

        print(f"      -> 信息: 合併後請求的 FRED IDs: {sorted(all_fred_ids)}") #<-- 修改：中文日誌

        fred_cfg = {'series_ids': all_fred_ids, 'start_date': start_date_daily_fetch, 'end_date': end_date, 'force_reload': force_reload_flag}

        fred_df = fetch_fred_data(fred_cfg)

        if fred_df is not None: fetch_results['fred_all'] = fred_df

        else: print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] {function_name} 警告: 部分或全部 FRED 數據抓取失敗。") #<-- 修改：中文日誌



        # 抓取 MOVE (函數內部的日誌目前還是英文)

        sml_needs_move = False

        for model_key in ['s', 'm', 'l']:

             indicators = get_nested_param(s2_config, {}, [model_key, 'indicators'], [])

             if "MOVE Level" in indicators:

                 sml_needs_move = True; break

        if sml_needs_move:

            move_cfg = {'start_date': start_date_daily_fetch, 'end_date': end_date, 'force_reload': force_reload_flag}

            move_df = fetch_move_index(move_cfg)

            if move_df is not None: fetch_results['move'] = move_df

        else: print("    -> 跳過抓取 MOVE (配置的 S/M/L 指標不需要).") #<-- 修改：中文日誌





        # --- 數據整合 ---

        print("\n  -> [進階] 步驟 2: 正在整合每日數據...") #<-- 修改：中文日誌

        master_daily_data = fetch_results['yf_daily'].copy()

        if 'fred_all' in fetch_results:

             fred_to_join = fetch_results['fred_all'].rename(columns={'VIXCLS':'VIX'}, errors='ignore')

             master_daily_data = master_daily_data.join(fred_to_join, how='left')

             for fred_id in fred_ids_systemic:

                 target_col = 'VIX' if fred_id == 'VIXCLS' else fred_id

                 if target_col not in master_daily_data.columns:

                      print(f"    ! 整合後警告: 欄位 for FRED ID '{fred_id}' (應為 '{target_col}') 缺失!") #<-- 修改：中文日誌

                 elif master_daily_data[target_col].isna().all():

                      print(f"    ! 整合後警告: 欄位 '{target_col}' (來自 FRED ID '{fred_id}') 全為 NaN!") #<-- 修改：中文日誌

        if 'move' in fetch_results:

             master_daily_data = master_daily_data.join(fetch_results['move'], how='left')

             if 'MOVE_Level' not in master_daily_data.columns or master_daily_data['MOVE_Level'].isna().all():

                 print("    ! 整合後警告: 欄位 'MOVE_Level' 缺失或全為 NaN!") #<-- 修改：中文日誌



        # 計算 CPI YoY

        cpi_col = 'CPIAUCSL'; yoy_col = 'CPI_YoY'

        indicators_l = get_nested_param(s2_config, {}, ['l', 'indicators'], [])

        if "CPI YoY Trend" in indicators_l:

            if cpi_col in master_daily_data.columns:

                common_params = get_nested_param(s2_config, {}, ['common_params'], {})

                cpi_periods = common_params.get('l_cpi_ma_period', 252)

                if len(master_daily_data[cpi_col].dropna()) > cpi_periods:

                    master_daily_data[yoy_col] = master_daily_data[cpi_col].pct_change(periods=cpi_periods) * 100

                else: master_daily_data[yoy_col] = np.nan

            else: master_daily_data[yoy_col] = np.nan



        # 重命名儲備金列

        reserves_key = None; reserves_id = None

        for key, val in systemic_fred_series_config.items():

             if key == 'reserves': reserves_key = key; reserves_id = val; break

        if reserves_id and reserves_id in master_daily_data.columns and 'Reserves' not in master_daily_data.columns:

             master_daily_data.rename(columns={reserves_id: 'Reserves'}, inplace=True)

             print(f"    -> 已重命名欄位 '{reserves_id}' 為 'Reserves'.") #<-- 修改：中文日誌

        elif reserves_id and 'Reserves' not in master_daily_data.columns:

             print(f"    ! 警告: 預期的儲備金欄位 '{reserves_id}' 整合後未找到.") #<-- 修改：中文日誌



        # 最終填充與裁剪

        numeric_cols = master_daily_data.select_dtypes(include=np.number).columns

        master_daily_data[numeric_cols] = master_daily_data[numeric_cols].ffill().bfill()

        final_start_date = start_date

        final_end_date = end_date if end_date else datetime.now(tz).strftime('%Y-%m-%d')

        master_daily_data = master_daily_data.loc[final_start_date:final_end_date]



        if master_daily_data.empty: raise ValueError("整合後的 Master daily data 為空.") #<-- 修改：中文錯誤

        advanced_run_results['master_daily_data'] = master_daily_data

        print("    - 每日數據整合成功。") #<-- 修改：中文日誌



        # --- 抓取小時數據 (VS 需要) ---

        hourly_data = None

        if globals().get('DO_RUN_VS', True): # 假設 VS 預設啟用

             print("\n  -> [進階] 步驟 3: 正在抓取 VS 模型所需的小時數據...") #<-- 修改：中文日誌

             vs_cfg = get_nested_param(config, DEFAULT_CONFIG, ['stage2_risk_models_v1', 'vs'], {})

             hourly_lookback = vs_cfg.get('fetch_hourly_initial_days', 600)

             start_date_hourly_fetch = (eff_end_date - timedelta(days=hourly_lookback)).strftime('%Y-%m-%d')

             if config_start_dt and pd.to_datetime(start_date_hourly_fetch) < config_start_dt:

                  start_date_hourly_fetch = start_date

             hourly_cfg = {'ticker': ticker, 'start_date': start_date_hourly_fetch, 'end_date': end_date, 'force_reload': force_reload_flag}

             hourly_data = fetch_yf_hourly(hourly_cfg) # fetch_yf_hourly 內部日誌已修改

             if hourly_data is not None:

                  advanced_run_results['hourly_data'] = hourly_data

                  print("    - 小時數據抓取成功。") #<-- 修改：中文日誌

             else:

                  # --- 修改：中文日誌 ---

                  print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] {function_name} 警告: 小時數據抓取失敗. VS 模型將被跳過.")

        else: print("\n  -> [進階] 步驟 3: 跳過抓取小時數據 (VS 模型被禁用).") #<-- 修改：中文日誌



        # --- 4. 執行 Stage 1 分析 ---

        if globals().get('DO_RUN_STAGE1', True): # 假設 Stage 1 預設啟用

            print("\n  -> [進階] 步驟 4: 正在執行 Stage 1 分析...") #<-- 修改：中文日誌

            s1_data_input = {'master_daily_data': master_daily_data.copy()}

            s1_results, s1_reliability = run_stage1_analysis(s1_data_input, config) # run_stage1_analysis 內部日誌保持英文

            advanced_run_results['stage1_results'] = s1_results

            advanced_run_results['stage1_reliability'] = s1_reliability

            if s1_results: print("    - Stage 1 分析成功。") #<-- 修改：中文日誌

            else: print("    ! 警告: Stage 1 分析失敗。") #<-- 修改：中文日誌



            # 繪製 Stage 1 圖表

            if globals().get('DO_PLOT_STAGE1', True) and s1_results: # 假設繪圖預設啟用

                 print("\n  -> [進階] 步驟 4b: 正在繪製 Stage 1 儀表圖...") #<-- 修改：中文日誌

                 s1_plot_cfg = get_nested_param(config, DEFAULT_CONFIG, ['plotting', 'stage1_plotly'], {})

                 lang_cfg = get_nested_param(config, DEFAULT_CONFIG, ['general', 'language'], 'zh')

                 fig_s1 = plot_stage1_four_gauges(

                     stage1_results=s1_results, reliability=s1_reliability, lang=lang_cfg,

                     figure_width=s1_plot_cfg.get('figure_width', 800),

                     figure_height=s1_plot_cfg.get('figure_height', 600),

                     number_fontsize=s1_plot_cfg.get('number_fontsize', 30)

                 ) # plot_stage1_four_gauges 內部日誌保持英文

                 if fig_s1:

                      try: from IPython.display import display; display(fig_s1)

                      except ImportError: pass

                      print("    - Stage 1 圖表已顯示。") #<-- 修改：中文日誌

                 else: print("    ! 警告: 無法生成 Stage 1 圖表。") #<-- 修改：中文日誌

        else: print("\n  -> [進階] 步驟 4: 跳過 Stage 1 分析 (已被禁用).") #<-- 修改：中文日誌



        # --- 5. 執行 Stage 2 分析 ---

        print("\n  -> [進階] 步驟 5: 正在執行 Stage 2 分析...") #<-- 修改：中文日誌

        stage2_results_for_plot = {}



        # 執行 VS

        if globals().get('DO_RUN_VS', True): # 假設預設啟用

            if hourly_data is not None:

                 vs_data_input = {'yf_hourly': hourly_data.copy()}

                 vs_result = run_vs_model(vs_data_input, config) # run_vs_model 內部日誌保持英文

                 if vs_result:

                      advanced_run_results['vs_results'] = vs_result

                      stage2_results_for_plot['vs'] = vs_result

                      print("    - VS 模型計算成功。") #<-- 修改：中文日誌

                 else: print("    ! 警告: VS 模型計算失敗。") #<-- 修改：中文日誌

            else: print("    - 跳過 VS 模型 (小時數據不可用).") #<-- 修改：中文日誌

        else: print("    - 跳過 VS 模型 (已被禁用).") #<-- 修改：中文日誌



        # 執行 SML

        if globals().get('DO_RUN_SML', True): # 假設預設啟用

            sml_data_input = {'master_daily_data': master_daily_data.copy()}

            for model_key in ['s', 'm', 'l']:

                 if get_nested_param(config, DEFAULT_CONFIG, ['stage2_risk_models_v1', model_key, 'enabled'], False):

                      print(f"    - 正在運行 {model_key.upper()} 模型...") #<-- 修改：中文日誌

                      sml_result = run_sml_model(sml_data_input, model_key, config) # run_sml_model 內部日誌保持英文

                      if sml_result:

                           advanced_run_results[f'{model_key}_results'] = sml_result

                           stage2_results_for_plot[model_key] = sml_result

                           print(f"      -> {model_key.upper()} 模型計算成功。") #<-- 修改：中文日誌

                      else: print(f"      ! 警告: {model_key.upper()} 模型計算失敗。") #<-- 修改：中文日誌

                 else: print(f"    - 跳過 {model_key.upper()} 模型 (模型配置中禁用).") #<-- 修改：中文日誌

        else: print("    - 跳過 SML 模型 (全局禁用).") #<-- 修改：中文日誌



        # --- 6. 繪製 Stage 2 圖表 ---

        if globals().get('DO_PLOT_STAGE2', True) and stage2_results_for_plot: # 假設預設啟用

             print("\n  -> [進階] 步驟 6: 正在繪製 Stage 2 儀表圖與趨勢圖...") #<-- 修改：中文日誌

             s2_plot_cfg = get_nested_param(config, DEFAULT_CONFIG, ['plotting', 'stage2_plotly'], {})

             lang_cfg = get_nested_param(config, DEFAULT_CONFIG, ['general', 'language'], 'zh')

             s2_smooth_type = s2_plot_cfg.get('smoothing_type', 'EMA')



             plot_order_s2 = ['vs', 's', 'm', 'l']

             name_map_s2 = {'vs': '極短期 (VS)', 's': '短期 (S)', 'm': '中期 (M)', 'l': '長期 (L)'}



             for key in plot_order_s2:

                  if key in stage2_results_for_plot:

                       res = stage2_results_for_plot[key]

                       score = res.get('risk_score')

                       reli = res.get('reliability', 0.0)

                       hist = res.get('history')

                       details = res.get('details', {})

                       s_period = None

                       s_cfg_section = get_nested_param(config, DEFAULT_CONFIG, ['stage2_risk_models_v1', key], {})

                       if key == 'vs': s_period = s_cfg_section.get('smoothing_period_hours', 10)

                       else: s_period = s_cfg_section.get('smoothing_period_days', 5)



                       # 繪製 Gauge (create_plotly_gauge 內部日誌保持英文)

                       title_g = f"{name_map_s2.get(key, key.upper())}"

                       fig_g = create_plotly_gauge(

                           risk_score=score, reliability=reli, main_title=title_g, lang=lang_cfg,

                           figure_width=s2_plot_cfg.get('gauge_width', 450),

                           figure_height=s2_plot_cfg.get('gauge_height', 380)

                       )

                       if fig_g:

                            print(f"\n--- [ {key.upper()} Gauge ] ---")

                            try: from IPython.display import display; display(fig_g)

                            except ImportError: pass

                       else: print(f"    ! 未能生成 {key.upper()} 儀表圖。") #<-- 修改：中文日誌



                       # 繪製 Trend (plot_plotly_trend 內部日誌保持英文)

                       if isinstance(hist, pd.Series) and not hist.empty:

                            title_t = f"{name_map_s2.get(key, key.upper())} 趨勢"

                            if 'final_window_used' in details and details['final_window_used']: title_t += f" (窗口: {details['final_window_used']}d)"

                            trend_width = int(s2_plot_cfg.get('gauge_width', 450) * s2_plot_cfg.get('trend_width_factor', 1.8))

                            trend_width = max(trend_width, s2_plot_cfg.get('trend_min_width', 700))

                            fig_t = plot_plotly_trend(

                                risk_score_series=hist, title=title_t,

                                smoothing_type=s2_smooth_type, smoothing_period=s_period,

                                series_type='hourly' if key == 'vs' else 'daily', lang=lang_cfg,

                                figure_width=trend_width,

                                figure_height=s2_plot_cfg.get('trend_height', 380)

                            )

                            if fig_t:

                                 print(f"\n--- [ {key.upper()} 趨勢圖 ] ---")

                                 try: from IPython.display import display; display(fig_t)

                                 except ImportError: pass

                            else: print(f"    ! 未能生成 {key.upper()} 趨勢圖。") #<-- 修改：中文日誌

                       print("-" * 30)

        else: print("\n  -> [進階] 步驟 6: 跳過繪製 Stage 2 (被禁用或無結果).") #<-- 修改：中文日誌



        # *** 修改：狀態改為英文 ***

        advanced_run_results["status"] = "Success"



    except ValueError as ve:

        # --- 修改：中文日誌 ---

        error_msg = f"進階分析期間發生數據/參數驗證錯誤: {ve}"

        print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] {function_name} 錯誤: {error_msg}")

        # *** 修改: 狀態改為英文 ***

        advanced_run_results["status"] = "Failed (Validation Error)"; advanced_run_results["error"] = str(ve) # 使用 str(ve)

    except Exception as e_main:

        # --- 修改：中文日誌 ---

        error_msg = f"進階分析期間發生未預期錯誤: {e_main}"

        print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] {function_name} 錯誤: {error_msg}")

        traceback.print_exc(limit=2)

        # *** 修改: 狀態改為英文 ***

        advanced_run_results["status"] = "Failed (Unexpected Error)"; advanced_run_results["error"] = str(e_main) # 使用 str(e_main)



    end_time = datetime.now()

    duration = (end_time - start_time).total_seconds()

    if isinstance(advanced_run_results, dict):

        advanced_run_results['duration'] = duration



    # --- 修改：中文日誌 (完成狀態部分保持，但返回的 status 已改) ---

    print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] == {function_name} 執行完畢. 狀態: {advanced_run_results.get('status', 'Unknown')}. 耗時: {duration:.2f}秒 ==") # 使用 .get 以防萬一



    return advanced_run_results





print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] Cell 25: Orchestration function 'run_advanced_analysis' defined [v1.4 - Status to English].")



"""### 26. 分析流程協調函數 - 深度分析 (Orchestration Func - Deep)"""



# 程式碼儲存格 26: 分析流程協調函數 - 深度分析 (Orchestration Func - Deep) [v1.3 - 含 ETF 合併 & 英文 Status]

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-



import pandas as pd

import numpy as np

from typing import Optional, Dict, Any, Tuple

from datetime import datetime, timezone # 確保導入 timezone

import traceback



# --- 假設依賴的函數和變數已在之前的儲存格定義 ---

# 需要 Cell 1 的: ACTIVE_CONFIG, DEFAULT_CONFIG, DO_RUN_SYSTEMIC, DO_PLOT_SYSTEMIC, DO_GENERATE_PNG (控制標誌)

# 需要 Cell 3 的: get_nested_param

# 需要 Cell 11 的: fetch_nyfed_data

# 需要 Cell 13 的: fetch_lt_bond_etf_data

# 需要 Cell 17 的: run_systemic_analysis

# 需要 Cells 20-23 的: Plotter 函數 (plot_systemic_matplotlib, plot_gemini_gauge_mpl, plot_trend_colored, generate_combined_png_export)

# 需要 Cell 25 的: run_advanced_analysis (核心依賴)



print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] Cell 26: Orchestration Function Definition - Deep Analysis [v1.3 - Incl ETF & Eng Status]...") # v1.3



def run_deep_analysis(config: Dict[str, Any]) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:

    """

    (流程協調函數) 執行深度分析流程。

    包含進階分析的所有步驟，並額外執行完整的系統性壓力分析與繪圖，以及可選的 PNG 導出。

    [v1.3]: 加入了 ETF 數據的抓取與合併邏輯，並將返回 status 改為英文。日誌中文化。

    """

    function_name = "run_deep_analysis"

    print(f"\n[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] == 開始執行: {function_name} ==")

    start_time = datetime.now()



    # --- 初始化結果字典 ---

    deep_run_results = {

        "status": "Not Run", #<- 初始狀態

        "error": None,

        "advanced_analysis_results": None,

        "nyfed_data": None,

        "etf_data": None, #<-- 放 ETF data

        "systemic_analysis_results": None,

        "png_export_path": None,

        "duration": 0.0

    }



    # --- 檢查依賴函數 ---

    required_funcs = ['run_advanced_analysis', 'fetch_nyfed_data', 'fetch_lt_bond_etf_data', #<- 包含 ETF fetcher

                      'run_systemic_analysis', 'plot_systemic_matplotlib', 'plot_gemini_gauge_mpl',

                      'plot_trend_colored', 'generate_combined_png_export', 'get_nested_param']

    if not all(f in globals() for f in required_funcs):

         missing = [f for f in required_funcs if f not in globals()]

         error_msg = f"缺少必要的函數: {missing}"

         print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] {function_name} 錯誤: {error_msg}")

         # *** 修改: 狀態改為英文 ***

         deep_run_results["status"] = "Failed (Missing Dependencies)"; deep_run_results["error"] = error_msg

         return deep_run_results



    try:

        # --- 1. 執行進階分析 ---

        print("\n  -> [深度] 步驟 1: 正在執行進階分析 (Stage 1 & Stage 2)...")

        advanced_results = run_advanced_analysis(config) #<- 使用已修改 status 的 v1.4 版本

        if advanced_results is None or advanced_results.get("status") != "Success": #<- 檢查英文 "Success"

            error_msg_adv = advanced_results.get("error", "進階分析失敗且無特定錯誤信息。") if advanced_results else "進階分析返回 None。"

            raise ValueError(f"前置任務進階分析失敗: {error_msg_adv}")

        deep_run_results["advanced_analysis_results"] = advanced_results

        print("    - 進階分析成功完成。")



        # --- 2. 執行系統性壓力分析 (如果啟用) ---

        systemic_results_df = None

        if globals().get('DO_RUN_SYSTEMIC', True):

             print("\n  -> [深度] 步驟 2: 正在執行系統性壓力分析...")

             # --- 2a. 獲取 NY Fed 數據 ---

             nyfed_fetch_config = { 'start_date': get_nested_param(config, DEFAULT_CONFIG, ['general', 'start_date_input']), 'end_date': get_nested_param(config, DEFAULT_CONFIG, ['general', 'end_date_input']), 'force_reload': config.get('force_reload_all', False) }

             print("    -> 正在抓取 NY Fed 數據...")

             nyfed_df = fetch_nyfed_data(nyfed_fetch_config)

             if nyfed_df is not None: deep_run_results['nyfed_data'] = nyfed_df; print(f"      - NY Fed 數據抓取成功 ({len(nyfed_df)} 行)。")

             else: print(f"    ! 警告: 無法抓取 NY Fed 數據。系統性分析可能不完整。")



             # --- 2b. 準備系統性分析所需數據 ---

             master_daily_data = advanced_results.get('master_daily_data')

             if not isinstance(master_daily_data, pd.DataFrame) or master_daily_data.empty:

                  raise ValueError("在進階分析結果中未找到 master_daily_data 或其為空。")

             data_for_systemic = { 'sofr': master_daily_data.get('SOFR'), 'dgs10': master_daily_data.get('DGS10'), 'dgs2': master_daily_data.get('DGS2'), 'move': master_daily_data.get('MOVE_Level'), 'vix': master_daily_data.get('VIX'), 'reserves': master_daily_data.get('Reserves'), 'nyfed_dealer': nyfed_df }

             data_for_systemic = {k: v for k, v in data_for_systemic.items() if v is not None}



             # --- 2c. 執行系統性分析計算 ---

             systemic_results_df = run_systemic_analysis(data_for_systemic, config)

             if systemic_results_df is not None and not systemic_results_df.empty:

                  # 將計算結果存儲起來，稍後可能與 ETF 合併

                  print("      - 系統性分析計算成功。")

             else:

                  print("    ! 警告: 系統性分析計算失敗或返回空結果。")

                  # 即使計算失敗，仍嘗試使用 master_daily_data 進行後續步驟（例如抓取 ETF）

                  systemic_results_df = master_daily_data.copy() if isinstance(master_daily_data, pd.DataFrame) else pd.DataFrame()



             # --- 2d. 抓取並合併 ETF 數據 (如果需要繪圖) ---

             etf_df = None

             etf_plotting_enabled = get_nested_param(config, DEFAULT_CONFIG, ['systemic_analysis', 'plotting_options', 'enable_lt_bond_etf_plot'], False)

             etf_ticker_cfg = get_nested_param(config, DEFAULT_CONFIG, ['systemic_analysis', 'yfinance_tickers', 'lt_bond_etf'])



             # 只有在配置啟用、Ticker有效時才嘗試抓取和合併

             if etf_plotting_enabled and etf_ticker_cfg:

                 print("    -> 正在抓取 ETF 數據...")

                 etf_fetch_config = { 'start_date': get_nested_param(config, DEFAULT_CONFIG, ['general', 'start_date_input']), 'end_date': get_nested_param(config, DEFAULT_CONFIG, ['general', 'end_date_input']), 'force_reload': config.get('force_reload_all', False) }

                 etf_df = fetch_lt_bond_etf_data(etf_fetch_config) # fetch_lt_bond_etf_data 內部有自己的日誌



                 if etf_df is not None and not etf_df.empty:

                      deep_run_results['etf_data'] = etf_df

                      # *** 合併到 systemic_results_df ***

                      if isinstance(systemic_results_df, pd.DataFrame):

                           # 使用 outer join 保留所有日期

                           systemic_results_df = systemic_results_df.join(etf_df, how='outer')

                           print(f"      - ETF 數據抓取並合併成功 ({len(etf_df)} 行)。")

                      else: # 如果 systemic_results_df 因故不是 DataFrame，則無法合併

                           print("    ! 警告: systemic_results_df 不是有效的 DataFrame，無法合併 ETF 數據。")

                           systemic_results_df = etf_df # 至少保留 ETF 數據

                 else:

                      print("    ! 警告: 無法抓取或 ETF 數據為空。")

             # --- 合併結束後，將最終包含（或不含）ETF 的 DF 存入結果 ---

             if isinstance(systemic_results_df, pd.DataFrame):

                 deep_run_results['systemic_analysis_results'] = systemic_results_df



             # --- 2e/步驟3: 繪製系統性分析圖表 (使用可能包含 ETF 的 DataFrame) ---

             if globals().get('DO_PLOT_SYSTEMIC', True):

                  print("\n  -> [深度] 步驟 3: 正在繪製系統性分析結果...")

                  # *** 使用 deep_run_results 中存儲的最終 systemic_analysis_results ***

                  df_to_plot = deep_run_results.get('systemic_analysis_results')

                  if isinstance(df_to_plot, pd.DataFrame) and not df_to_plot.empty:

                       try: tz = globals().get('taiwan_tz', timezone.utc)

                       except NameError: tz = timezone.utc

                       start_dt_plot = pd.to_datetime(get_nested_param(config, DEFAULT_CONFIG, ['general', 'start_date_input']))

                       end_dt_plot = pd.to_datetime(get_nested_param(config, DEFAULT_CONFIG, ['general', 'end_date_input']))

                       if pd.isna(end_dt_plot): end_dt_plot = datetime.now(tz)



                       print("    -> 正在繪製多面板時間序列圖...")

                       plot_systemic_matplotlib(df_to_plot, config, start_dt_plot, end_dt_plot) #<- 使用更新後的 v1.4 繪圖函數



                       # ... (Gemini Gauge 和 Trend Colored Plot 邏輯保持不變) ...

                       latest_stress_val = df_to_plot['Dealer_Stress_Index'].dropna().iloc[-1] if 'Dealer_Stress_Index' in df_to_plot and not df_to_plot['Dealer_Stress_Index'].dropna().empty else np.nan

                       if pd.notna(latest_stress_val):

                            level_desc_en_sys = "Cannot Evaluate"; color_cat_sys = "Unknown"

                            if latest_stress_val < 60: color_cat_sys = "Green"; level_desc_en_sys = "Normal"

                            elif latest_stress_val < 80: color_cat_sys = "Yellow"; level_desc_en_sys = "High Stress"

                            else: color_cat_sys = "Red"; level_desc_en_sys = "Extreme Stress"

                            print("\n    -> 正在繪製 Gemini 主觀儀表板...")

                            plot_gemini_gauge_mpl(latest_stress_val, level_desc_en_sys, color_cat_sys)

                       else: print("    ! 警告: 因缺少最新壓力指數值，無法繪製 Gemini 儀表板。")

                       trend_plot_days_cfg = get_nested_param(config, DEFAULT_CONFIG, ['systemic_analysis', 'plotting_options', 'trend_plot_days'], 60)

                       print("\n    -> 正在繪製近期彩色趨勢圖...")

                       stress_series_for_trend = df_to_plot.get('Dealer_Stress_Index')

                       if isinstance(stress_series_for_trend, pd.Series):

                           plot_trend_colored(stress_series_for_trend, trend_plot_days_cfg)

                       else:

                           print("    ! 警告: 無法獲取壓力指數序列來繪製彩色趨勢圖。")





                  else:

                       print("    ! 警告: 無有效數據傳遞給系統性繪圖函數。")

             else:

                  print("\n  -> [深度] 步驟 3: 跳過繪製系統性分析 (已被禁用).")



        else:

             print("\n  -> [深度] 步驟 2 & 3: 跳過系統性壓力和繪圖 (已被禁用).")



        # --- 4. 執行 PNG 導出 (保持不變) ---

        if globals().get('DO_GENERATE_PNG', False):

             print("\n  -> [深度] 步驟 4: 正在生成 Stage 2 合併 PNG 導出...")

             s2_results_for_png = {'risk_vs': advanced_results.get('vs_results'), 'risk_sml_s': advanced_results.get('s_results'), 'risk_sml_m': advanced_results.get('m_results'), 'risk_sml_l': advanced_results.get('l_results'),}

             s2_results_for_png = {k: v for k, v in s2_results_for_png.items() if v is not None}

             if s2_results_for_png:

                  png_path = generate_combined_png_export(s2_results_for_png, config)

                  if png_path: deep_run_results['png_export_path'] = png_path; print(f"    - PNG 導出成功: {png_path}")

                  else: print("    ! 警告: PNG 導出生成失敗。")

             else: print("    - 跳過 PNG 導出 (未找到有效的 Stage 2 結果)。")

        else:

             print("\n  -> [深度] 步驟 4: 跳過 PNG 導出 (已被禁用).")



        # *** 修改：狀態改為英文 ***

        deep_run_results["status"] = "Success"



    except ValueError as ve:

         error_msg = f"深度分析期間發生數據/參數驗證錯誤: {ve}"

         print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] {function_name} 錯誤: {error_msg}")

         if 'advanced_results' in locals() and isinstance(advanced_results, dict): deep_run_results['advanced_analysis_results'] = advanced_results

         # *** 修改: 狀態改為英文 ***

         deep_run_results["status"] = "Failed (Validation Error)"; deep_run_results["error"] = str(ve)

    except Exception as e_main:

         error_msg = f"深度分析期間發生未預期錯誤: {e_main}"

         print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] {function_name} 錯誤: {error_msg}")

         traceback.print_exc(limit=2)

         if 'advanced_results' in locals() and isinstance(advanced_results, dict): deep_run_results['advanced_analysis_results'] = advanced_results

         # *** 修改: 狀態改為英文 ***

         deep_run_results["status"] = "Failed (Unexpected Error)"; deep_run_results["error"] = str(e_main)



    end_time = datetime.now()

    duration = (end_time - start_time).total_seconds()

    if isinstance(deep_run_results, dict):

        deep_run_results['duration'] = duration



    print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] == {function_name} 執行完畢. 狀態: {deep_run_results.get('status', 'Unknown')}. 耗時: {duration:.2f}秒 ==")

    return deep_run_results



print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] Cell 26: Orchestration function 'run_deep_analysis' defined [v1.3 - Incl ETF & Eng Status].")



"""---"""

# --- 臨時測試 yfinance ---

import yfinance as yf

ticker_to_test = '^IXIC' # 或者讀取 ACTIVE_CONFIG['general']['target_ticker']

print(f"正在直接測試 yfinance 能否抓取 {ticker_to_test}...")



# 模擬 run_basic_analysis 計算的日期範圍

end_date_test = None # 或者讀取 ACTIVE_CONFIG['general']['end_date_input']

fetch_days_test = 365 # 或者讀取 ACTIVE_CONFIG['stage1_basic']['fetch_days_daily_yf']

try:

    tz_test = globals().get('taiwan_tz', timezone.utc) # 獲取時區

    eff_end_date_test = pd.to_datetime(end_date_test) if end_date_test else datetime.now(tz_test)

    start_date_test = (eff_end_date_test - timedelta(days=fetch_days_test + 30)).strftime('%Y-%m-%d')

    api_end_dt_req_test = eff_end_date_test + timedelta(days=1) # End date +1 for yf.download

    api_end_str_req_test = api_end_dt_req_test.strftime('%Y-%m-%d')



    print(f"測試日期範圍: {start_date_test} 到 {api_end_str_req_test}")



    test_data = yf.download(ticker_to_test,

                            start=start_date_test,

                            end=api_end_str_req_test, # 注意：yf.download 的 end 是不包含的

                            progress=False,

                            repair=True)



    if test_data is not None and not test_data.empty:

        print("\n直接 yfinance 測試成功:")

        print(test_data.tail()) # 顯示最後幾行數據

    else:

        print("\n直接 yfinance 測試失敗：未返回數據。")



except Exception as e_test:

    print(f"\n直接 yfinance 測試失敗，錯誤訊息: {e_test}")
