"""# 主程式"""



# @title 18: 繪圖器定義 - Plotly 儀表板 {"display-mode":"form"}

# 程式碼儲存格 18: 繪圖器定義 - Plotly 儀表板 (Plotter Def - Plotly Gauge)

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-



import pandas as pd

import numpy as np

from typing import Optional, Dict, Any, List # 引入 List

from datetime import datetime

import traceback



# --- 假設 Plotly 已在 Cell 0 導入並檢查 ---

# 需要 LIB_STATUS['plotly'], go, make_subplots



# 檢查 Plotly 是否可用

if not globals().get('LIB_STATUS', {}).get('plotly', False) or 'go' not in globals() or 'make_subplots' not in globals():

    print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] Cell 18 Warning: Plotly library or subplots not available. Gauge plots cannot be generated.")

    # 定義空函數以避免 NameError

    def create_plotly_gauge(*args, **kwargs): return None

    def plot_stage1_four_gauges(*args, **kwargs): return None

else:

    print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] Cell 18: Plotter Definition - Plotly Gauge...")



    # --- 函數 1: 創建單個 Plotly 儀表圖 ---

    def create_plotly_gauge(

        risk_score: Optional[float], # 輸入的分數 (0-100) 或 NaN

        reliability: float = 100.0,  # 可靠度 (影響標題)

        gauge_title_key: str = "default_gauge_title", # 用於查找標籤的鍵

        main_title: Optional[str] = None, # 圖表主標題 (若為 None，使用預設)

        lang: str = 'zh',           # 當前語言 ('zh' or 'en')

        force_lang: Optional[str] = None, # 強制語言 ('en' 或 None)

        figure_width: int = 450,    # 圖寬

        figure_height: int = 380    # 圖高

    ) -> Optional['go.Figure']:     # 返回 Plotly Figure 對象或 None

        """

        (繪圖工具) 創建單個 Plotly 風險儀表圖。

        支持中英文標籤和強制英文模式。



        :param risk_score: 要顯示的風險分數 (0-100)，或 np.nan 表示無數據。

        :param reliability: 數據可靠度 (%)，用於標題顯示。

        :param gauge_title_key: 在 labels 中查找儀表內部標題的鍵。

        :param main_title: 圖表頂部的主標題。

        :param lang: 當前界面語言。

        :param force_lang: 強制使用的語言 (通常為 'en' 用於 PNG 導出)。

        :param figure_width: 圖表寬度。

        :param figure_height: 圖表高度。

        :return: Plotly Figure 對象或 None。

        """

        function_name = "create_plotly_gauge"

        # print(f"Executing: {function_name}...") # Debug



        try:

            # --- 決定實際使用的語言和字體 ---

            use_lang = force_lang if force_lang else lang

            font_family = 'Arial, sans-serif' if use_lang == 'en' else None # 英文強制使用 Arial



            # --- 標籤定義 ---

            labels_en = {"default_main_title":"Risk Score", "default_gauge_title":"Risk Score (0-100)", "unavailable":"Data Unavailable"}

            labels_zh = {"default_main_title":"風險值", "default_gauge_title":"風險評分 (0-100)", "unavailable":"數據不足"}

            target_labels = labels_en if use_lang == 'en' else labels_zh



            # --- 數值與標題處理 ---

            gauge_value: float = 0.0 # 儀表顯示值

            title_text_gauge = target_labels.get(gauge_title_key, target_labels['default_gauge_title']) # 儀表內部標題

            number_format = ".1f"; number_suffix = "" # 預設格式



            if pd.notna(risk_score):

                gauge_value = float(np.clip(risk_score, 0, 100))

            else:

                gauge_value = 0.0 # 無效數據時指針指向 0

                title_text_gauge = target_labels['unavailable'] # 標題顯示數據不足

                number_format = "" # 不顯示數字格式



            # 組合主標題 (包含可靠度)

            final_main_title = main_title if main_title else target_labels['default_main_title']

            if reliability < 99.9: # 只有可靠度不足 100 時才顯示

                 reliability_str = f"(Reliability: {reliability:.0f}%)" if use_lang == 'en' else f"(可靠度: {reliability:.0f}%)"

                 final_main_title = f"{final_main_title} {reliability_str}"



            # --- 儀表樣式 ---

            gauge_style = {

                'axis': {'range': [0, 100], 'tickwidth': 1, 'tickcolor': "darkblue", 'tickfont': {'size': 10}},

                'bar': {'color': "rgba(0,0,0,0.7)", 'thickness': 0.3}, # 中間的數值條

                'bgcolor': "white", 'borderwidth': 1, 'bordercolor': "gray",

                'steps': [ # 顏色區段

                    {'range': [0, 25], 'color': 'rgba(0,255,0,0.3)'},    # Green

                    {'range': [25, 50], 'color': 'rgba(255,255,0,0.4)'}, # Yellow

                    {'range': [50, 75], 'color': 'rgba(255,165,0,0.5)'}, # Orange

                    {'range': [75, 100], 'color': 'rgba(255,0,0,0.6)'}   # Red

                ],

                # 'threshold': { # 可選的閾值線

                #      'line': {'color': "red", 'width': 3}, 'thickness': 0.75, 'value': 75

                # }

            }



            # --- 創建圖表 ---

            fig = go.Figure()

            fig.add_trace(go.Indicator(

                mode = "gauge+number",

                value = gauge_value,

                number = {'suffix': number_suffix, 'font':{'size':50, 'family': font_family}, 'valueformat': number_format}, # 應用字體

                title = {'text': title_text_gauge, 'font': {'size': 16, 'family': font_family}}, # 應用字體

                gauge = gauge_style,

                domain = {'x': [0.1, 0.9], 'y': [0.1, 0.9]} # 調整儀表在圖中的位置和大小

            ))



            fig.update_layout(

                title={'text': final_main_title, 'y':0.95, 'x':0.5, 'xanchor': 'center', 'yanchor': 'top', 'font':{'size':16, 'family': font_family}}, # 應用字體

                height=figure_height, width=figure_width,

                margin=dict(l=20, r=20, t=60, b=20), # 調整邊距

                font=dict(family=font_family) # 全局字體

            )

            return fig



        except Exception as e:

            print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] Error creating Plotly gauge '{main_title or gauge_title_key}': {e}")

            traceback.print_exc(limit=1)

            return None





    # --- 函數 2: 繪製 Stage 1 的 2x2 儀表圖 ---

    def plot_stage1_four_gauges(

        stage1_results: Optional[Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]]], # Stage 1 的結果字典

        reliability: float = 100.0, # Stage 1 的整體可靠度

        lang: str = 'zh',

        force_lang: Optional[str] = None,

        figure_width: int = 800,

        figure_height: int = 600,

        number_fontsize: int = 30

    ) -> Optional['go.Figure']:

        """

        (繪圖工具) 繪製 Stage 1 基本評估的 2x2 Plotly 儀表圖。

        支持中英文標籤和強制英文模式。



        :param stage1_results: Stage 1 的結果字典，鍵是維度名稱。

        :param reliability: Stage 1 的整體可靠度。

        :param lang: 當前界面語言。

        :param force_lang: 強制使用的語言。

        :param figure_width: 圖表寬度。

        :param figure_height: 圖表高度。

        :param number_fontsize: 儀表板中間數字的字體大小。

        :return: Plotly Figure 對象或 None。

        """

        function_name = "plot_stage1_four_gauges"

        # print(f"Executing: {function_name}...") # Debug



        # 檢查 Plotly 是否可用

        if not globals().get('LIB_STATUS', {}).get('plotly', False) or go is None or 'make_subplots' not in globals():

            print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] {function_name} Error: Plotly library or subplots not available.")

            return None

        if not stage1_results or not isinstance(stage1_results, dict):

            print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] {function_name} Error: Invalid or empty stage1_results data.")

            return None



        try:

            # --- 決定語言和字體 ---

            use_lang = force_lang if force_lang else lang

            font_family = 'Arial, sans-serif' if use_lang == 'en' else None



            # --- 標籤定義 ---

            labels_en = { "極短期 (1-7天)": "Very Short (VS)", "短期 (~60天)": "Short (S)",

                          "中期 (~160天)": "Mid-Term (M)", "長期 (~10年)": "Long-Term (L)",

                          "main_title": f"Multi-Dimension Risk Quick Estimate (Reliability: {reliability:.0f}%)", "unavailable": "N/A"}

            labels_zh = { "極短期 (1-7天)": "極短期", "短期 (~60天)": "短期",

                          "中期 (~160天)": "中期", "長期 (~10年)": "長期",

                          "main_title": f"多維度風險初步評估 (可靠度: {reliability:.0f}%)", "unavailable": "無"}

            target_labels = labels_en if use_lang == 'en' else labels_zh



            # 確定繪圖順序和子圖標題

            ordered_dims = ["極短期 (1-7天)", "短期 (~60天)", "中期 (~160天)", "長期 (~10年)"]

            dims_to_plot = [d for d in ordered_dims if d in stage1_results]

            subplot_titles = [target_labels.get(d, d) for d in dims_to_plot]



            if not dims_to_plot: return None



            # --- 創建子圖 ---

            plot_rows, plot_cols = 2, 2

            fig = make_subplots(

                rows=plot_rows, cols=plot_cols,

                specs=[[{'type': 'indicator'}]*plot_cols]*plot_rows, # 指定類型

                subplot_titles=subplot_titles, # 子圖標題

                vertical_spacing=0.25, horizontal_spacing=0.15

            )



            # --- 定義儀表樣式 ---

            style = {

                'axis': {'range': [0, 100], 'showticklabels': False, 'ticks': ''},

                'bar': {'color': "rgba(0,0,0,0.6)", 'thickness': 0.4},

                'bgcolor': "white", 'borderwidth': 1, 'bordercolor': "gray",

                'steps': [ {'range': [0, 25], 'color': 'rgba(0,255,0,0.3)'}, {'range': [25, 50], 'color': 'rgba(255,255,0,0.4)'},

                           {'range': [50, 75], 'color': 'rgba(255,165,0,0.5)'}, {'range': [75, 100], 'color': 'rgba(255,0,0,0.6)'}]

            }



            # --- 填充子圖 ---

            pos = [(1, 1), (1, 2), (2, 1), (2, 2)] # 位置

            for i, dim_key in enumerate(dims_to_plot):

                 if i >= len(pos): break

                 res = stage1_results.get(dim_key, {});

                 score_orig = res.get("gauge_value", np.nan)

                 # 處理 NaN

                 display_value = 0.0 if pd.isna(score_orig) else float(np.clip(score_orig, 0, 100))

                 display_text = target_labels['unavailable'] if pd.isna(score_orig) else "" # NaN 時顯示文字

                 value_format = "" if pd.isna(score_orig) else ".1f" # NaN 不格式化數字



                 r, c = pos[i]

                 fig.add_trace(go.Indicator(

                     mode = "gauge+number",

                     value = display_value,

                     number = {'suffix': display_text, 'font':{'size': number_fontsize, 'family': font_family}, 'valueformat': value_format},

                     title = {'text': ''}, # 子圖標題已設置

                     gauge = style

                 ), row=r, col=c)



            # --- 更新整體佈局 ---

            fig.update_layout(

                title_text=target_labels['main_title'], title_x=0.5, title_font_size=18,

                height=figure_height, width=figure_width,

                margin=dict(l=30, r=30, t=90, b=30),

                font=dict(family=font_family) # 全局字體

            )

            # 調整子圖標題的位置和字體

            for anno in fig.layout.annotations:

                 anno.update(y=anno.y * 0.98, font=dict(size=14, family=font_family)) # 稍微向下移並應用字體



            return fig



        except Exception as e:

            print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] Error plotting Stage 1 gauges: {e}")

            traceback.print_exc(limit=1)

            return None





print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] Cell 18: Plotly Gauge functions defined.")



"""### 19. 繪圖器定義 - Plotly 趨勢圖 (Plotter Def - Plotly Trend)"""



# 程式碼儲存格 19: 繪圖器定義 - Plotly 趨勢圖 (Plotter Def - Plotly Trend) [v1.2 - 恢復原始 Raw Line]

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-



import pandas as pd

import numpy as np

from typing import Optional, Dict, Any, List, Callable # 引入 Callable

from datetime import datetime

import traceback



# --- 假設依賴的庫和函數已在之前的儲存格定義 ---

# 需要 Cell 0 的: LIB_STATUS (檢查 plotly), go

# 需要 Cell 4 的: calculate_sma, calculate_ema



# 檢查 Plotly 是否可用

if not globals().get('LIB_STATUS', {}).get('plotly', False) or 'go' not in globals():

    print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] Cell 19 Warning: Plotly not available. Trend plots cannot be generated.")

    def plot_plotly_trend(*args, **kwargs): return None

else:

    print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] Cell 19: Plotter Definition - Plotly Trend [v1.2 - Original Raw Line Restored]...") # v1.2



    def plot_plotly_trend(

        risk_score_series: Optional[pd.Series],

        title: str, # 標題由調用者根據語言傳入

        smoothing_type: str = 'EMA', # 平滑類型: 'EMA' 或 'SMA'

        smoothing_period: Optional[int] = None, # 平滑週期 (None 或 <=1 表示不平滑)

        series_type: str = 'daily', # 用於標籤 ('daily' 或 'hourly')

        thresholds: List[float] = [25, 50, 75], # 風險區間閾值

        figure_width: int = 700,

        figure_height: int = 380,

        lang: str = 'zh',

        force_lang: Optional[str] = None # 強制語言 ('en')

    ) -> Optional['go.Figure']:

        """

        (繪圖工具) 繪製 Plotly 歷史風險趨勢圖，支援平滑和顏色分區。

        [v1.2]: Restored original raw line style. (中文註解保留)



        :param risk_score_series: 包含歷史風險分數的 Pandas Series (索引為日期/時間)。

        :param title: 圖表主標題 (應已包含模型類型和可靠度信息)。

        :param smoothing_type: 平滑類型 ('EMA' or 'SMA')。

        :param smoothing_period: 平滑週期 (期數)，None 或 <=1 表示不平滑。

        :param series_type: 序列類型 ('daily' or 'hourly')，影響標籤。

        :param thresholds: 風險區間劃分閾值列表。

        :param figure_width: 圖表寬度。

        :param figure_height: 圖表高度。

        :param lang: 當前語言。

        :param force_lang: 強制語言。

        :return: Plotly Figure 對象或 None。

        """

        function_name = "plot_plotly_trend"



        if not globals().get('LIB_STATUS', {}).get('plotly', False) or go is None:

            print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] {function_name} Error: Plotly library not available.")

            return None



        use_lang = force_lang if force_lang else lang

        font_family = 'Arial, sans-serif' if use_lang == 'en' else None



        labels = {

            'zh': {"no_data": "數據不足無法繪製趨勢圖", "yaxis_title": "風險值 (0-100)",

                   "legend_title": "風險區間", "xaxis_title": "日期/時間", "raw_value": "原始值",

                   "smooth": "平滑", "zones": ['低 (0-25)', '中低 (25-50)', '中高 (50-75)', '高 (75-100)']},

            'en': {"no_data": "Insufficient data for trend plot", "yaxis_title": "Risk Score (0-100)",

                   "legend_title": "Risk Zone", "xaxis_title": "Date/Time", "raw_value": "Raw",

                   "smooth": "Smooth", "zones": ['Low (0-25)', 'Med-Low (25-50)', 'Med-High (50-75)', 'High (75-100)']}

        }

        text = labels.get(use_lang, labels['en'])



        if risk_score_series is None or not isinstance(risk_score_series, pd.Series) or risk_score_series.dropna().empty:

            print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] {function_name} Info: {text['no_data']} ({title}).")

            return None



        plot_series = risk_score_series.dropna().copy().clip(0, 100)

        if len(plot_series) < 2:

             print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] {function_name} Info: Not enough valid points ({len(plot_series)}) to plot trend ({title}).")

             return None



        smoothed_series: Optional[pd.Series] = None

        plot_raw = False

        actual_smoothing_type: Optional[str] = None

        smoothing_period_actual = int(smoothing_period) if smoothing_period is not None and smoothing_period > 1 else 0



        if smoothing_period_actual > 1:

            smooth_type_upper = smoothing_type.upper() if isinstance(smoothing_type, str) else 'EMA'

            smooth_func: Optional[Callable] = None

            smooth_func_name = None

            if smooth_type_upper == 'EMA':

                 smooth_func_name = 'calculate_ema'

                 if smooth_func_name in globals(): smooth_func = globals()[smooth_func_name]; actual_smoothing_type = 'EMA'

            elif smooth_type_upper == 'SMA':

                 smooth_func_name = 'calculate_sma'

                 if smooth_func_name in globals(): smooth_func = globals()[smooth_func_name]; actual_smoothing_type = 'SMA'



            if smooth_func and len(plot_series) >= smoothing_period_actual:

                try:

                    temp_smoothed = smooth_func(plot_series, period=smoothing_period_actual)

                    if temp_smoothed is not None and not temp_smoothed.dropna().empty:

                        smoothed_series = temp_smoothed.dropna().clip(0, 100)

                        plot_raw = True

                except Exception as e_smooth:

                    print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] {function_name} Warning: Error during {actual_smoothing_type or smooth_type_upper} smoothing: {e_smooth}")

            elif not smooth_func:

                 print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] {function_name} Warning: Smoothing function '{smooth_func_name or smoothing_type}' not found. Skipping smoothing.")



        display_series = smoothed_series if plot_raw and smoothed_series is not None else plot_series

        if display_series.empty:

            print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] {function_name} Error: No valid data points left after processing for {title}.")

            return None



        try:

            fig = go.Figure()

            colors = ['rgba(0,128,0,0.7)', 'rgba(255,215,0,0.7)', 'rgba(255,140,0,0.7)', 'rgba(220,20,60,0.7)']

            thresholds_sorted = sorted(list(set([0] + thresholds + [100])))

            bounds = sorted(list(set([b for b in thresholds_sorted if 0 <= b <= 100] + [0, 100.1])))



            zone_names = text['zones']

            if len(zone_names) != len(bounds) - 1:

                 print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] {function_name} Warning: Number of zone names ({len(zone_names)}) does not match number of zones ({len(bounds)-1}). Using default names.")

                 zone_names = [f"Zone {i+1}" for i in range(len(bounds)-1)]



            x_all = display_series.index

            y_all = display_series.values



            for i in range(len(bounds) - 1):

                lower = bounds[i]; upper = bounds[i+1]

                mask = (y_all >= lower) & (y_all < upper)

                y_masked = display_series.where(mask)

                if not y_masked.dropna().empty:

                    zone_color = colors[i % len(colors)]

                    zone_name = zone_names[i]

                    fig.add_trace(go.Scatter(

                        x=x_all, y=y_masked, mode='lines', name=zone_name,

                        line=dict(color=zone_color, width=2),

                        showlegend=True, connectgaps=False

                    ))



            # --- 繪製原始數據線 (如果進行了平滑) ---

            if plot_raw:

                fig.add_trace(go.Scatter(

                    x=plot_series.index, y=plot_series.values, mode='lines', name=text['raw_value'],

                    # *** 恢復為原始的淡灰色細線樣式 ***

                    line=dict(color='rgba(100, 100, 100, 0.3)', width=1), #<- 已改回

                    showlegend=False, hoverinfo='skip'

                ))



            # --- 添加閾值水平線 ---

            for thr in thresholds:

                 if 0 < thr < 100:

                      fig.add_hline(y=thr, line_dash="dash", line_color="grey",

                                    annotation_text=f"{thr}", annotation_position="bottom right",

                                    annotation_font=dict(family=font_family, size=10))



            # --- 更新標題和佈局 ---

            yaxis_title_text = text['yaxis_title']

            if plot_raw and actual_smoothing_type:

                smoothing_unit = "h" if series_type == 'hourly' else "d"

                smoothing_label_text = f"{smoothing_period_actual}{smoothing_unit} {actual_smoothing_type} {text['smooth']}"

                yaxis_title_text = f"{text['yaxis_title']} ({smoothing_label_text})"



            fig.update_layout(

                title={'text': title, 'x': 0.5, 'xanchor': 'center'},

                xaxis_title=text['xaxis_title'],

                yaxis_title=yaxis_title_text,

                yaxis_range=[-5, 105],

                legend_title_text=text['legend_title'],

                width=figure_width, height=figure_height,

                margin=dict(l=50, r=20, t=70, b=50),

                font=dict(family=font_family),

                legend=dict(font=dict(family=font_family)),

                hovermode='x unified'

            )

            fig.update_xaxes(title_font=dict(family=font_family, size=12), tickfont=dict(family=font_family, size=10))

            fig.update_yaxes(title_font=dict(family=font_family, size=12), tickfont=dict(family=font_family, size=10))



            return fig



        except Exception as e:

            print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] Error creating Plotly trend plot for '{title}': {e}")

            traceback.print_exc(limit=1)

            return None



print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] Cell 19: Plotter function 'plot_plotly_trend' defined [v1.2 - Original Raw Line Restored].")



"""### 20. 繪圖器定義 - Matplotlib 系統圖 (Plotter Def - Matplotlib Systemic)"""



# 程式碼儲存格 20: 繪圖器定義 - Matplotlib 系統圖 (Plotter Def - Matplotlib Systemic) [v1.5 - 加入圖片儲存]

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-



import pandas as pd

import numpy as np

from typing import Optional, Dict, Any, Tuple, List, Callable

from datetime import datetime

import traceback

import os # <--- 確保導入 os 模塊



# --- 假設依賴的庫和變數已在之前的儲存格定義 ---

# 需要 Cell 0 的: LIB_STATUS (檢查 matplotlib), plt, mdates, mpatches, GridSpec, to_rgba

# 需要 Cell 1 的: ACTIVE_CONFIG, DEFAULT_CONFIG

# 需要 Cell 3 的: get_nested_param

# 需要 Cell 4 的: calculate_sma, calculate_ema



# 檢查 Matplotlib 是否可用

if not globals().get('LIB_STATUS', {}).get('matplotlib', False) or 'plt' not in globals() or 'mdates' not in globals() or 'mpatches' not in globals() or 'GridSpec' not in globals():

    print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] Cell 20 Warning: Matplotlib or required components not available. Cannot generate systemic plots.")

    def _plot_single_series_matplotlib(*args, **kwargs): pass

    def plot_systemic_matplotlib(*args, **kwargs): pass

else:

     # --- 修改：日誌標題更新 ---

    print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] Cell 20: Plotter Definition - Matplotlib Systemic [v1.5 - Added Savefig]...") # v1.5



    # --- Matplotlib 輔助函數：繪製單個序列 (保持不變 v1.4) ---

    def _plot_single_series_matplotlib(ax: plt.Axes,

                                         series: Optional[pd.Series],

                                         title: str,

                                         color: str ='blue',

                                         ylabel: Optional[str] = None,

                                         smoothing_type: Optional[str] = None,

                                         smoothing_period: Optional[int] = None,

                                         show_legend: bool = True,

                                         date_format: str = "%Y-%m",

                                         add_zero_line: bool = False,

                                         invert_y: bool = False):

        # (此輔助函數 _plot_single_series_matplotlib 保持與上一版本相同，無需修改)

        # ... (請使用上一版本 v1.4 提供的 _plot_single_series_matplotlib 完整代碼) ...

        if series is None or series.dropna().empty:

            ax.text(0.5, 0.5, 'No Data Available', ha='center', va='center', transform=ax.transAxes, color='grey', fontsize=9)

            ax.set_title(title, fontsize=10); ax.set_yticks([]); ax.set_xticks([])

            ax.set_facecolor('#e0e0e0')

            return

        plot_series = series.dropna().copy()

        smoothed_series = None; plot_raw = False; actual_smoothing_type = None

        smoothing_period_actual = int(smoothing_period) if smoothing_period is not None and smoothing_period > 1 else 0

        if smoothing_period_actual > 1 and smoothing_type:

            smooth_type_upper = smoothing_type.upper(); smooth_func: Optional[Callable] = None; required_smooth_func = None

            if smooth_type_upper == 'EMA' and 'calculate_ema' in globals(): smooth_func = globals()['calculate_ema']; actual_smoothing_type = 'EMA'

            elif smooth_type_upper == 'SMA' and 'calculate_sma' in globals(): smooth_func = globals()['calculate_sma']; actual_smoothing_type = 'SMA'

            if smooth_func and len(plot_series) >= smoothing_period_actual:

                try:

                    temp_smoothed = smooth_func(plot_series, period=smoothing_period_actual)

                    if temp_smoothed is not None and not temp_smoothed.dropna().empty: smoothed_series = temp_smoothed.dropna(); plot_raw = True

                except Exception as e_sm: print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] Warning: Failed to apply {actual_smoothing_type or smooth_type_upper} smoothing for '{title}': {e_sm}")

        display_series = smoothed_series if plot_raw and smoothed_series is not None else plot_series

        if display_series.empty:

            ax.text(0.5, 0.5, 'No Data After Processing', ha='center', va='center', transform=ax.transAxes, color='grey', fontsize=9)

            ax.set_title(title, fontsize=10); ax.set_yticks([]); ax.set_xticks([]); return

        line_label = f"{actual_smoothing_type}({smoothing_period_actual})" if plot_raw and actual_smoothing_type else "Value"

        line, = ax.plot(display_series.index, display_series.values, color=color, linewidth=1.5, label=line_label, zorder=2)

        if plot_raw:

            raw_label = "Raw Data" if actual_smoothing_type else None

            if raw_label: ax.plot(plot_series.index, plot_series.values, color=color, alpha=0.25, linewidth=0.8, label=raw_label, zorder=1)

        ax.set_title(title, fontsize=10); ax.grid(True, linestyle='--', alpha=0.6, zorder=0)

        if ylabel: ax.set_ylabel(ylabel, fontsize=8)

        ax.xaxis.set_major_formatter(mdates.DateFormatter(date_format)); ax.tick_params(axis='both', which='major', labelsize=8); ax.margins(x=0.01)

        if add_zero_line: ax.axhline(0, color='black', linestyle='--', linewidth=0.8, alpha=0.7, zorder=1)

        if invert_y: ax.invert_yaxis()

        if show_legend and line_label:

             if not (line_label == "Value" and not plot_raw): ax.legend(fontsize=7, loc='best')





    # --- Matplotlib 主繪圖函數 (加入圖片儲存) ---

    def plot_systemic_matplotlib(

        final_df: pd.DataFrame,

        config: Dict[str, Any],

        start_dt_obj: Optional[datetime] = None,

        end_dt_obj: Optional[datetime] = None

        ) -> None:

        """

        (繪圖工具) 使用 Matplotlib 繪製系統性風險的多面板時間序列圖，並儲存圖片。

        [v1.5]: Added fig.savefig(). (中文註解保留)

        """

        function_name = "plot_systemic_matplotlib"

        print(f"\n[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] --- [{function_name}] Generating & Saving Systemic Risk Plots ---") #<- 修改標題



        # ... (依賴檢查和配置獲取部分保持不變) ...

        if not globals().get('LIB_STATUS', {}).get('matplotlib', False) or plt is None: print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] {function_name} Error: Matplotlib is not available."); return

        if '_plot_single_series_matplotlib' not in globals(): print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] {function_name} Error: Missing plotting helper function."); return

        if not isinstance(final_df, pd.DataFrame) or final_df.empty: print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] {function_name} Error: Input DataFrame 'final_df' is invalid."); return

        sys_cfg=get_nested_param(config, DEFAULT_CONFIG, ['systemic_analysis'],{}); plot_opts=get_nested_param(sys_cfg,{},['plotting_options'],{}); stress_params=get_nested_param(sys_cfg,{},['stress_index_params'],{}); macd_params=get_nested_param(sys_cfg,{},['macd_params'],{}); yf_tickers_cfg=get_nested_param(sys_cfg,{},['yfinance_tickers'],{})

        smoothing_type=plot_opts.get('smoothing_type','EMA'); smoothing_period=stress_params.get('smoothing_window_stress_index',5); stress_threshold_color_switch=stress_params.get('threshold_high_stress_color',55); ratio_thr=stress_params.get('threshold_ratio_color',90); enable_macd=macd_params.get('enable_macd_momentum_plot',True); macd_p=(macd_params.get('fast_period',12),macd_params.get('slow_period',26),macd_params.get('signal_period',9)); macd_colors_tuple=(macd_params.get('color_blue','#6495ED'),macd_params.get('color_green','#3CB371'),macd_params.get('color_red','#B22222')); enable_etf=plot_opts.get('enable_lt_bond_etf_plot',True); etf_ticker=yf_tickers_cfg.get('lt_bond_etf'); etf_col=f'ETF_{etf_ticker}_Price' if etf_ticker else None; move_ticker=yf_tickers_cfg.get('move','^MOVE')



        # --- 確定繪製子圖 (同前) ---

        plots_to_draw = { # Base check if column exists and has any non-NA data

            'SOFR': 'SOFR' in final_df.columns and final_df['SOFR'].notna().any(), 'Spread': 'Spread_10Y2Y' in final_df.columns and final_df['Spread_10Y2Y'].notna().any(),

            'Volatility': 'MOVE_Level' in final_df.columns and final_df['MOVE_Level'].notna().any(), 'VIX': 'VIX' in final_df.columns and final_df['VIX'].notna().any(),

            'GrossPos': 'Total_Gross_Positions_Millions' in final_df.columns and final_df['Total_Gross_Positions_Millions'].notna().any(), 'Reserves': 'Reserves' in final_df.columns and final_df['Reserves'].notna().any(),

            'Ratio': 'Pos_Res_Ratio' in final_df.columns and final_df['Pos_Res_Ratio'].notna().any(), 'StressIndex': 'Dealer_Stress_Index' in final_df.columns and final_df['Dealer_Stress_Index'].notna().any(),

            'MACD': enable_macd and 'Stress_Index_MACD_Hist' in final_df and final_df['Stress_Index_MACD_Hist'].notna().any(),

            'ETF': enable_etf and etf_col and etf_col in final_df.columns and final_df[etf_col].notna().any()

        }

        active_plots = {k:v for k,v in plots_to_draw.items() if v}

        num_plots = len(active_plots)

        if num_plots == 0: print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] {function_name} Info: No data available to plot."); return



        fig = None # Initialize fig to None

        try:

            with plt.style.context('seaborn-v0_8-whitegrid'):

                plt.rcParams.update({'axes.facecolor':'#f0f0f0', 'figure.facecolor':'#f0f0f0', 'grid.color':'#cccccc', 'grid.linestyle':'--', 'grid.alpha':0.6, 'axes.edgecolor':'#333333', 'axes.labelcolor':'#333333', 'xtick.color':'#333333', 'ytick.color':'#333333', 'text.color':'#333333', 'axes.titlecolor':'#333333'})

                rows = (num_plots + 1) // 2; cols = 2

                fig = plt.figure(figsize=(14, rows * 2.8)) # Assign fig here

                gs = GridSpec(rows, cols, figure=fig, hspace=0.5, wspace=0.18)

                axes_list = []

                plot_idx = 0

                plot_order = ['SOFR', 'Spread', 'Volatility', 'VIX', 'GrossPos', 'Reserves', 'StressIndex', 'Ratio', 'MACD', 'ETF'] # Swapped order

                col_map = {'SOFR': 'SOFR', 'Spread': 'Spread_10Y2Y', 'Volatility': 'MOVE_Level', 'VIX': 'VIX', 'GrossPos': 'Total_Gross_Positions_Millions', 'Reserves': 'Reserves', 'Ratio': 'Pos_Res_Ratio', 'StressIndex': 'Dealer_Stress_Index', 'MACD': 'Stress_Index_MACD_Hist', 'ETF': etf_col}



                # --- 循環繪製子圖 (內部邏輯與 v1.4 基本相同) ---

                for plot_key in plot_order:

                    if active_plots.get(plot_key):

                        if plot_idx >= rows * cols: break

                        ax = fig.add_subplot(gs[plot_idx // cols, plot_idx % cols])

                        axes_list.append(ax)

                        col_name = col_map.get(plot_key); plot_data = final_df.get(col_name)

                        title=""; ylabel=""; color='C0'; add_zero=False; invert=False; apply_smooth=False; date_fmt="%y-%m-%d"

                        # ... (子圖參數設定與 v1.4 完全相同) ...

                        if plot_key == 'SOFR': title="SOFR (%)"; ylabel="Rate (%)"; color='purple'; date_fmt="%Y-%m"

                        elif plot_key == 'Spread': title="Spread 10Y-2Y (BPS)"; ylabel="BPS"; color='orange'; add_zero=True; plot_data = plot_data * 100 if plot_data is not None else None; date_fmt="%Y-%m"

                        elif plot_key == 'Volatility': title=f"{move_ticker or 'MOVE'} Level"; ylabel="Index"; color='#00AEAE'; apply_smooth=True

                        elif plot_key == 'VIX': title="VIX Index"; ylabel="Index"; color='magenta'; apply_smooth=True

                        elif plot_key == 'GrossPos': title="Dealer Net Treasury Pos (Bil $)"; ylabel="Bil USD"; color='green'; add_zero=True; plot_data = plot_data / 1000 if plot_data is not None else None; apply_smooth=True

                        elif plot_key == 'Reserves': title="Total Reserve Balances (Tril $)"; ylabel="Tril USD"; color='goldenrod'; add_zero=True; plot_data = plot_data / 1e6 if plot_data is not None else None

                        elif plot_key == 'Ratio': title="Positions / Reserves Ratio"; ylabel="Ratio"; color='#FF8C00'; add_zero=True; apply_smooth=True

                        elif plot_key == 'StressIndex': title=f"Systemic Stress Index {'(Smoothed)' if smoothing_period > 1 else ''}"; ylabel="Index (0-100)"; apply_smooth=False # Use specific logic below

                        elif plot_key == 'ETF': title=f"LT Bond ETF ({etf_ticker or 'N/A'})"; ylabel="Price"; color='#0077CC'

                        elif plot_key == 'MACD': title=f"Stress Idx MACD {macd_p}"; ylabel="Momentum"; apply_smooth=False



                        if plot_data is not None and not plot_data.dropna().empty:

                            # --- 壓力指數雙色線段繪製邏輯 (同 v1.4) ---

                            if plot_key == 'StressIndex':

                                # ... (StressIndex 繪圖代碼與 v1.4 完全相同) ...

                                stress_series = plot_data.dropna().clip(0, 100); color_below = 'lightpink'; color_above = 'red'; legend_added_below = False; legend_added_above = False

                                for i in range(len(stress_series) - 1):

                                    y1=stress_series.iloc[i]; y2=stress_series.iloc[i+1]; x1=stress_series.index[i]; x2=stress_series.index[i+1]; segment_color = color_above if y1 >= stress_threshold_color_switch else color_below; label = None

                                    if segment_color == color_below and not legend_added_below: label = f"< {stress_threshold_color_switch}"; legend_added_below = True

                                    elif segment_color == color_above and not legend_added_above: label = f">= {stress_threshold_color_switch}"; legend_added_above = True

                                    ax.plot([x1, x2], [y1, y2], color=segment_color, linestyle='-', linewidth=1.5, solid_capstyle='round', label=label)

                                ax.axhline(stress_threshold_color_switch, color='grey', linestyle='--', linewidth=0.8, alpha=0.7, zorder=0)

                                ax.set_title(title, fontsize=10); ax.set_ylabel(ylabel, fontsize=8); ax.grid(True, linestyle='--', alpha=0.6, zorder=0); ax.xaxis.set_major_formatter(mdates.DateFormatter(date_fmt)); ax.tick_params(axis='both', which='major', labelsize=8)

                                ax.margins(x=0.01); current_ylim = ax.get_ylim(); ax.set_ylim(bottom=min(0, current_ylim[0]), top=105); handles, labels_leg = ax.get_legend_handles_labels();

                                if handles: ax.legend(handles=handles, labels=labels_leg, fontsize=7, loc='best')

                           # --- MACD 繪製邏輯 (同 v1.4) ---

                            elif plot_key == 'MACD':

                                # ... (MACD 繪圖代碼與 v1.4 完全相同) ...

                                macd_hist_data = plot_data; macd_color_data = final_df.get('Stress_Index_MACD_Color', 'grey').reindex(macd_hist_data.index).fillna('grey')

                                time_diffs = np.diff(mdates.date2num(macd_hist_data.index)); avg_diff = np.nanmean(time_diffs) if len(time_diffs) > 0 else 1.0; bar_width = avg_diff * 0.8

                                ax.bar(macd_hist_data.index, macd_hist_data.values, color=macd_color_data.values, width=bar_width, align='center', alpha=0.7); ax.axhline(0, color='#333333', linestyle='-', linewidth=0.8)

                                ax.set_title(title, fontsize=10); ax.set_ylabel(ylabel, fontsize=8); ax.grid(True, linestyle='--', alpha=0.6); ax.xaxis.set_major_formatter(mdates.DateFormatter(date_fmt)); plt.setp(ax.get_xticklabels(), rotation=15, ha='right', fontsize=8); ax.margins(x=0.01)

                                leg_patch = [mpatches.Patch(color=c, label=l) for c,l in zip(macd_colors_tuple, ['Rising (+)', 'Rising (-)', 'Falling'])]; ax.legend(handles=leg_patch, fontsize=7, loc='best', title="Histogram Trend")

                            else:

                                # 調用輔助函數繪製其他圖表 (同 v1.4)

                                # ... (_plot_single_series_matplotlib 調用和 Ratio 特殊效果代碼與 v1.4 完全相同) ...

                                _plot_single_series_matplotlib(ax=ax, series=plot_data, title=title, color=color, ylabel=ylabel, smoothing_type=smoothing_type if apply_smooth else None, smoothing_period=smoothing_period if apply_smooth else None, show_legend=apply_smooth, add_zero_line=add_zero, invert_y=invert, date_format=date_fmt)

                                if plot_key == 'Ratio':

                                    try:

                                        above = plot_data.where(plot_data >= ratio_thr);

                                        if not above.dropna().empty: ax.plot(above.index, above.values, color='#CC0000', linewidth=1.5, label=f'>={ratio_thr:.0f}', zorder=3)

                                        ax.axhline(ratio_thr, color='grey', linestyle='--', linewidth=1.0, label=f'Threshold ({ratio_thr:.0f})', zorder=1); handles, labels_leg = ax.get_legend_handles_labels();

                                        if handles: ax.legend(handles=handles, labels=labels_leg, fontsize=7, loc='best')

                                    except Exception as e_ratio: print(f"! Error adding ratio threshold effect: {e_ratio}")

                        else:

                            ax.set_title(title, fontsize=10); ax.text(0.5, 0.5, 'Data Not Available', ha='center', va='center', transform=ax.transAxes, color='grey', fontsize=9); ax.set_yticks([]); ax.set_xticks([]); ax.set_facecolor('#e0e0e0')

                        plot_idx += 1



                # --- 格式化 X 軸 & 高亮 (同 v1.4) ---

                # ... (X 軸格式化和高亮區間代碼與 v1.4 完全相同) ...

                if axes_list:

                    last_visible_ax = next((ax for ax in reversed(axes_list) if ax.get_visible()), None)

                    if last_visible_ax:

                        try:

                            last_ax = last_visible_ax; locator = mdates.AutoDateLocator(minticks=6, maxticks=12); formatter = mdates.ConciseDateFormatter(locator); last_ax.xaxis.set_major_locator(locator); last_ax.xaxis.set_major_formatter(formatter); last_ax.set_xlabel("Date", fontsize=9); plt.setp(last_ax.get_xticklabels(), rotation=15, ha='right', fontsize=8)

                            for ax_other in axes_list:

                                if ax_other is not last_ax and ax_other.get_visible(): plt.setp(ax_other.get_xticklabels(), visible=False); ax_other.set_xlabel("")

                        except Exception as e_xaxis: print(f"! Error formatting X-axis: {e_xaxis}")

                    plot_start = final_df.index.min(); plot_end = final_df.index.max(); highlight_dates = {'Repo Spike': ('2019-09-15', '2019-09-30'), 'COVID Crash': ('2020-03-01', '2020-04-15')}

                    if pd.notna(plot_start) and pd.notna(plot_end):

                         for ax_h in axes_list:

                              if ax_h.get_visible():

                                   try:

                                        y_min_h, y_max_h = ax_h.get_ylim()

                                        if y_min_h < y_max_h:

                                             for lbl, (s_dt, e_dt) in highlight_dates.items():

                                                  s_hl=pd.to_datetime(s_dt); e_hl=pd.to_datetime(e_dt)

                                                  if max(plot_start, s_hl) < min(plot_end, e_hl):

                                                       v_s=max(plot_start, s_hl); v_e=min(plot_end, e_hl)

                                                       if v_s < v_e:

                                                            ax_h.axvspan(v_s, v_e, color='yellow', alpha=0.15, zorder=-1); mid_d = v_s + (v_e - v_s) / 2; txt_y = y_min_h + (y_max_h - y_min_h) * 0.96; ax_xlim = ax_h.get_xlim()

                                                            if ax_xlim[0] <= mdates.date2num(mid_d) <= ax_xlim[1]: ax_h.text(mid_d, txt_y, lbl, rotation=0, ha='center', va='top', fontsize=8, bbox=dict(boxstyle='round,pad=0.2', fc='yellow', alpha=0.4), zorder=5)

                                   except Exception: pass





                # --- 顯示圖表 & 新增儲存圖片 ---

                fig.tight_layout(rect=[0, 0.03, 1, 0.96])

                # --- 主標題位置已是 y=0.95 ---

                fig.suptitle("Systemic Risk Indicators & Stress Index", fontsize=14, fontweight='bold', y=0.95)



                # *** 新增：儲存圖片 ***

                try:

                    # 確定儲存路徑 (與 PNG 導出使用相同目錄)

                    output_dir_sys = globals().get('CACHE_DIR_CELL2', ".") # 優先使用快取目錄

                    if not os.path.exists(output_dir_sys):

                        try: os.makedirs(output_dir_sys)

                        except OSError: output_dir_sys = "." # 無法創建則放當前目錄



                    # 獲取 Ticker 名稱用於檔名

                    ticker_sys = get_nested_param(config, DEFAULT_CONFIG, ['general', 'target_ticker'], 'default')

                    safe_ticker_name_sys = ticker_sys.replace('^','').replace('/','_')

                    save_filename = f"systemic_risk_plot_{safe_ticker_name_sys}.png"

                    save_filepath = os.path.join(output_dir_sys, save_filename)



                    # 儲存圖片，使用 figure 的背景色，並調整邊界

                    fig.savefig(save_filepath, bbox_inches='tight', facecolor=fig.get_facecolor(), dpi=150) #<- 增加 dpi 提高清晰度

                    print(f"    - Systemic risk plot saved to: {save_filepath}")

                except Exception as e_save:

                    print(f"    ! Warning: Failed to save systemic risk plot: {e_save}")

                # *** 結束新增儲存 ***



                plt.show() # 仍然顯示圖片



        except Exception as e_plot_main:

            print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] {function_name} Error: Failed to generate plot: {e_plot_main}")

            traceback.print_exc(limit=1)

        finally:

            if fig: # Ensure fig was created before trying to close

                 plt.close(fig) #<- 關閉圖表以釋放內存





    # --- 修改：日誌標題更新 ---

    print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] Cell 20: Matplotlib plotting function defined [v1.5 - Added Savefig].")



"""### 21. 繪圖器定義 - Gemini 儀表板 (Plotter Def - Gemini Gauge)"""



# 程式碼儲存格 21: 繪圖器定義 - Gemini 儀表板 (Plotter Def - Gemini Gauge)

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-



import pandas as pd # 雖然繪圖本身不直接用，但調用處可能傳入 Series

import numpy as np

from typing import Optional, Dict, Any, Tuple, List # 引入 Tuple, List

from datetime import datetime

import traceback



# --- 假設依賴的庫和變數已在之前的儲存格定義 ---

# 需要 Cell 0 的: LIB_STATUS (檢查 matplotlib), plt, mpatches



# 檢查 Matplotlib 是否可用

if not globals().get('LIB_STATUS', {}).get('matplotlib', False) or 'plt' not in globals() or 'mpatches' not in globals():

    print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] Cell 21 Warning: Matplotlib not available. Gemini Gauge plot cannot be generated.")

    # 定義空函數以避免 NameError

    def plot_gemini_gauge_mpl(*args, **kwargs): pass

else:

    print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] Cell 21: Plotter Definition - Gemini Gauge (Matplotlib)...")



    def plot_gemini_gauge_mpl(value: float, level_desc_en: str, color_category: str):

        """

        (繪圖工具) 使用 Matplotlib 繪製半圓形儀表板 (用於 Gemini 主觀壓力評估)。

        數值顯示在指針下方。標籤使用英文。



        :param value: 要顯示的數值 (0-100)。

        :param level_desc_en: 對應數值的英文描述 (例如 "Normal", "High Stress")。

        :param color_category: 顏色類別 ("Green", "Yellow", "Red", "Unknown") - 影響文字顏色等。

        """

        function_name = "plot_gemini_gauge_mpl"

        # print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] Executing: {function_name}...") # 執行時再打印



        try:

            # 確保值在 0-100 之間

            gauge_value = max(0, min(100, float(value) if pd.notna(value) else 0))



            # 顏色定義

            zone_colors = ["#6aa84f", "#f1c232", "#cc0000"] # Green(0-60), Yellow(60-80), Red(80-100) zones

            needle_color = '#333333'

            text_color = '#111111'

            label_text_color = 'white' # 色帶上的文字顏色

            bg_color = '#f0f0f0' # 圖表背景色



            fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(8, 5), facecolor=bg_color) # 創建圖和軸

            ax.set_aspect('equal') # 保持圓形

            ax.set_facecolor(bg_color) # 軸背景色



            # --- 繪製背景色帶 ---

            center_x, center_y = 0, 0

            radius = 1.0

            width = 0.35 # 色帶寬度



            # 角度定義 (0 度在右側水平, 180 度在左側水平)

            # 數值 0 -> 180度, 數值 100 -> 0度

            # 閾值 60 -> 180 - (60/100)*180 = 180 - 108 = 72 度

            angle_60 = 180 - (60 / 100.0) * 180.0

            # 閾值 80 -> 180 - (80/100)*180 = 180 - 144 = 36 度

            angle_80 = 180 - (80 / 100.0) * 180.0



            # 繪製色帶 (Wedge)

            # 綠色區域 (0-60): 角度從 180 到 angle_60

            green_zone = mpatches.Wedge((center_x, center_y), radius, angle_60, 180, width=width, facecolor=zone_colors[0], alpha=0.9, zorder=1)

            ax.add_patch(green_zone)

            # 黃色區域 (60-80): 角度從 angle_60 到 angle_80

            yellow_zone = mpatches.Wedge((center_x, center_y), radius, angle_80, angle_60, width=width, facecolor=zone_colors[1], alpha=0.9, zorder=1)

            ax.add_patch(yellow_zone)

            # 紅色區域 (80-100): 角度從 angle_80 到 0

            red_zone = mpatches.Wedge((center_x, center_y), radius, 0, angle_80, width=width, facecolor=zone_colors[2], alpha=0.9, zorder=1)

            ax.add_patch(red_zone)



            # --- 繪製指針 ---

            # 將數值映射到角度 (弧度)

            needle_angle_deg = 180 - (gauge_value / 100.0) * 180.0

            needle_angle_rad = np.radians(needle_angle_deg)

            needle_length = radius * 0.9 # 指針長度

            needle_x = center_x + needle_length * np.cos(needle_angle_rad)

            needle_y = center_y + needle_length * np.sin(needle_angle_rad)

            # 繪製指針線

            ax.plot([center_x, needle_x], [center_y, needle_y], color=needle_color, linewidth=3, solid_capstyle='round', zorder=3)

            # 繪製指針中心圓點

            base_circle = mpatches.Circle((center_x, center_y), radius * 0.08, facecolor=needle_color, zorder=4)

            ax.add_patch(base_circle)



            # --- 顯示文字 (數值和描述) ---

            # 顯示數值 (在中心點下方)

            ax.text(center_x, center_y - radius * 0.25, f"{gauge_value:.1f}", # 稍微向下調整

                    horizontalalignment='center', verticalalignment='top', # 垂直對齊改為 top

                    fontsize=38, fontweight='bold', zorder=5, color=text_color)

            # 顯示描述 (在數字下方)

            ax.text(center_x, center_y - radius * 0.50, level_desc_en, # 再向下調整

                    horizontalalignment='center', verticalalignment='top', # 垂直對齊改為 top

                    fontsize=16, wrap=True, zorder=5, color=text_color)



            # --- 添加色帶標籤 (Normal, High, Extreme) ---

            label_radius = radius * 0.82 # 標籤放置的半徑 (稍微向外調整)

            # 計算標籤角度 (區間的中間角度)

            label_angle_green = (180 + angle_60) / 2

            label_angle_yellow = (angle_60 + angle_80) / 2

            label_angle_red = (angle_80 + 0) / 2

            # 計算標籤位置並添加文字

            ax.text(center_x + label_radius * np.cos(np.radians(label_angle_green)),

                    center_y + label_radius * np.sin(np.radians(label_angle_green)),

                    "Normal", ha='center', va='center', fontsize=11, fontweight='bold', color=label_text_color, zorder=2)

            ax.text(center_x + label_radius * np.cos(np.radians(label_angle_yellow)),

                    center_y + label_radius * np.sin(np.radians(label_angle_yellow)),

                    "High", ha='center', va='center', fontsize=11, fontweight='bold', color=label_text_color, zorder=2)

            ax.text(center_x + label_radius * np.cos(np.radians(label_angle_red)),

                    center_y + label_radius * np.sin(np.radians(label_angle_red)),

                    "Extreme", ha='center', va='center', fontsize=11, fontweight='bold', color=label_text_color, zorder=2)



            # --- 清理圖表外觀 ---

            ax.set_xlim(-radius*1.1, radius*1.1)

            ax.set_ylim(center_y - radius*0.7, center_y + radius*1.1) # 確保底部文字空間足夠

            ax.axis('off') # 隱藏坐標軸



            plt.suptitle("Gemini Subjective Stress Gauge", fontsize=16, fontweight='bold', y=0.98) # 主標題

            plt.tight_layout(rect=[0, 0.01, 1, 0.96]) # 調整佈局

            plt.show() # 在 Notebook 中顯示圖表



        except Exception as e:

            print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] Error plotting Gemini Gauge: {e}")

            traceback.print_exc(limit=1)





print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] Cell 21: Matplotlib Gemini Gauge function defined.")



"""### 22. 繪圖器定義 - 彩色趨勢圖 (Plotter Def - Colored Trend)"""



# 程式碼儲存格 22: 繪圖器定義 - 彩色趨勢圖 (Plotter Def - Colored Trend)

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-



import pandas as pd

import numpy as np

from typing import Optional, Dict, Any, Tuple, List

from datetime import datetime

import traceback



# --- 假設依賴的庫和變數已在之前的儲存格定義 ---

# 需要 Cell 0 的: LIB_STATUS (檢查 matplotlib), plt, mdates



# 檢查 Matplotlib 是否可用

if not globals().get('LIB_STATUS', {}).get('matplotlib', False) or 'plt' not in globals() or 'mdates' not in globals():

    print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] Cell 22 Warning: Matplotlib not available. Colored trend plot cannot be generated.")

    # 定義空函數以避免 NameError

    def plot_trend_colored(*args, **kwargs): pass

else:

    print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] Cell 22: Plotter Definition - Colored Trend (Matplotlib)...")



    def plot_trend_colored(stress_series_daily: Optional[pd.Series], days_to_plot: int):

        """

        (繪圖工具) 使用 Matplotlib 繪製過去指定天數壓力指數的日趨勢圖，

        並根據壓力水平 (基於固定閾值 60 和 80) 著色線段。

        標籤使用英文。



        :param stress_series_daily: 包含日頻率壓力指數的 Pandas Series (索引需為 DatetimeIndex)。

        :param days_to_plot: 要繪製的最近天數 (必須 > 1)。

        """

        function_name = "plot_trend_colored"

        # print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] Executing: {function_name}...") # 執行時再打印



        # --- 輸入檢查 ---

        if stress_series_daily is None or not isinstance(stress_series_daily, pd.Series) or stress_series_daily.dropna().empty:

            print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] {function_name} Info: Input stress series is invalid or empty. Cannot plot.")

            return

        if not isinstance(stress_series_daily.index, pd.DatetimeIndex):

             try: # 嘗試轉換索引

                 stress_series_daily.index = pd.to_datetime(stress_series_daily.index)

             except Exception as e_idx:

                  print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] {function_name} Error: Cannot convert index to DatetimeIndex: {e_idx}. Cannot plot.")

                  return

        if not isinstance(days_to_plot, int) or days_to_plot <= 1:

             print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] {function_name} Error: 'days_to_plot' ({days_to_plot}) must be an integer greater than 1.")

             return



        # 確保有足夠的數據點

        if len(stress_series_daily.dropna()) < 2:

             print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] {function_name} Info: Less than 2 valid data points available. Cannot plot trend.")

             return



        try:

            # --- 數據選取 ---

            # 確保索引已排序

            stress_series_daily = stress_series_daily.sort_index()

            # 選取最近 N 天的數據

            latest_date_trend = stress_series_daily.index[-1]

            start_plot_date = latest_date_trend - pd.Timedelta(days=int(days_to_plot) - 1)

            # 確保開始日期不早於數據實際開始日期

            actual_start_date = max(start_plot_date, stress_series_daily.index.min())

            trend_daily = stress_series_daily.loc[actual_start_date:].dropna().copy() # 使用日數據並移除 NaN



            if len(trend_daily) < 2: # 再次檢查，因為 loc 可能選出少於 2 個點

                 print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] {function_name} Info: Less than 2 valid data points in the last {days_to_plot} days. Cannot plot trend.")

                 return



            print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] Plotting trend for last {len(trend_daily)} days (requested {days_to_plot})...")



            # --- 繪圖設定 ---

            fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(12, 4)) # 創建圖和軸

            fig.patch.set_facecolor('#f0f0f0') # 圖背景色

            ax.set_facecolor('#f8f8f8') # 軸背景色



            # 定義顏色閾值和顏色 (基於新腳本的儀表板顏色)

            thresholds = [60, 80] # Normal < 60, High 60-80, Extreme >= 80

            colors = ['#6aa84f', '#f1c232', '#cc0000'] # Green, Yellow, Red



            # --- 繪製顏色分段線條 ---

            # 遍歷數據點，根據每個線段起始點的值決定顏色

            for i in range(len(trend_daily) - 1):

                y1 = trend_daily.iloc[i]

                y2 = trend_daily.iloc[i+1]

                x1 = trend_daily.index[i]

                x2 = trend_daily.index[i+1]



                # 根據起始點 y1 的值選擇顏色

                segment_color = colors[0] # Default Green

                if y1 >= thresholds[1]: # >= 80

                    segment_color = colors[2] # Red

                elif y1 >= thresholds[0]: # >= 60 and < 80

                    segment_color = colors[1] # Yellow



                # 繪製當前線段

                ax.plot([x1, x2], [y1, y2], color=segment_color, linestyle='-', linewidth=1.5, solid_capstyle='round')



            # --- 添加水平閾值線和標籤 (英文) ---

            ax.axhline(thresholds[0], color=colors[1], linestyle='--', linewidth=0.8, alpha=0.7, zorder=0)

            ax.axhline(thresholds[1], color=colors[2], linestyle='--', linewidth=0.8, alpha=0.7, zorder=0)

            # 在圖表左側添加標籤

            plot_xlim = ax.get_xlim()

            text_x_pos = mdates.num2date(plot_xlim[0] + (plot_xlim[1]-plot_xlim[0])*0.01) # X 軸 1% 位置

            ax.text(text_x_pos, thresholds[0] + 1, f'{thresholds[0]} (High Start)', color=colors[1], va='bottom', ha='left', fontsize=9, alpha=0.8)

            ax.text(text_x_pos, thresholds[1] + 1, f'{thresholds[1]} (Extreme Start)', color=colors[2], va='bottom', ha='left', fontsize=9, alpha=0.8)



            # --- 設定圖表標題和標籤 (英文) ---

            ax.set_title(f"Stress Index - Daily Trend (Past {days_to_plot} Days, Color Coded)")

            ax.set_ylabel("Stress Index")

            ax.set_xlabel("Date")

            ax.grid(True, linestyle='--', alpha=0.6) # 啟用網格



            # --- 格式化 X 軸日期 ---

            # 使用 ConciseDateFormatter 自動選擇合適的日期格式

            locator = mdates.AutoDateLocator(minticks=5, maxticks=10)

            formatter = mdates.ConciseDateFormatter(locator)

            ax.xaxis.set_major_locator(locator)

            ax.xaxis.set_major_formatter(formatter)

            plt.setp(ax.get_xticklabels(), rotation=15, ha='right', fontsize=8) # 旋轉標籤



            # --- 設定 Y 軸範圍 ---

            min_val_plot = trend_daily.min()

            max_val_plot = trend_daily.max()

            # 計算動態 padding

            y_range = max_val_plot - min_val_plot if pd.notna(min_val_plot) and pd.notna(max_val_plot) else 100 # 預設範圍

            y_padding = max(y_range * 0.1, 5) # 至少留 5 個單位的 padding

            min_y = max(0, min_val_plot - y_padding) # 最低不小於 0

            max_y = min(110, max_val_plot + y_padding) # 最高不超過 110

            ax.set_ylim(bottom=min_y, top=max_y)



            # --- 顯示圖表 ---

            plt.tight_layout() # 自動調整佈局

            plt.show()



        except Exception as e_plot_trend:

            print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] {function_name} Error: Failed to plot colored trend: {e_plot_trend}")

            traceback.print_exc(limit=1)





print(f"[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] Cell 22: Matplotlib Colored Trend function defined.")

     if req == 'FRED_API_KEY': # 允許為 None

          if req not in globals(): is_missing = True

     elif req == 'FRED_API_KEY_MISSING': # 必須是布林值

          if req not in globals() or not isinstance(comp, bool): is_missing = True

     elif comp is None: is_missing = True

     elif req.startswith(('run_', 'generate_', 'display_', 'plot_')) and not callable(comp): is_missing = True



     if is_missing: missing_components.append(f"{req} (應在 {cell_map.get(req, '未知儲存格')} 定義/設定)")



if missing_components:

else:

# --- API Key 說明頁面函數 ---

def _display_api_key_help():

    help_text = """

## FRED API Key 設定說明



**為什麼需要 FRED API Key？**



本分析工具的部分進階功能（如選項 2 的進階分析、選項 3 的深度分析）需要從美國聖路易斯聯邦儲備銀行經濟數據庫 (FRED) 獲取重要的宏觀經濟數據（例如：美國國債利率、VIX 指數、失業率、通膨數據等）。為了能順利抓取這些數據，您需要提供一個免費申請的 FRED API Key。



**1. 如何獲取免費的 FRED API Key？**



* 請前往 FRED 官方網站的 API Key 申請頁面：

    [https://fred.stlouisfed.org/docs/api/api_key.html](https://fred.stlouisfed.org/docs/api/api_key.html)

* 您需要先在該網站註冊一個免費帳戶（如果尚未擁有）。

* 登錄後，按照網站提示請求（Request）一個 API Key。通常申請後會立即在網頁上顯示給您。

* 請**複製**並妥善保管好您的 API Key（它是一串由 32 個字母和數字組成的字符）。



**2. 如何在 Google Colab 中設定 API Key？ (推薦方式)**



為了安全且讓程式能持續使用您的 Key，強烈建議使用 **Colab Secrets** 功能：



1.  在 Colab 界面的左側邊欄，找到並點擊 **鑰匙圖標 (🔒 Secrets)**。

2.  點擊 **+ Add new secret** 按鈕。

3.  在 `Name` 欄位中，**務必精確輸入** `FRED_API_KEY` （注意大小寫完全一致）。

4.  在 `Value` 欄位中，**貼上**您剛剛從 FRED 網站複製的 32 位 API Key。

5.  **非常重要：** 請確保**勾選 (啟用)** `Notebook access` 這個選項（開關按鈕變藍色），這樣這個 Notebook 才能讀取到您設定的 Secret。

6.  點擊 **Save** 按鈕保存。



**3. 設定完成後如何操作？**



* **重要：** 在 Colab Secrets 中設定好 Key 之後，為了讓這個正在運行的 Notebook 讀取到新的設定，您**必須**執行以下**任一**操作：

    * **重新運行 Cell 1** (包含配置和讀取 Key 的儲存格)。

    * **或者** 選擇菜單欄的 **Runtime -> Restart Runtime** (執行階段 -> 重新啟動執行階段)。

* 完成上述操作後，回到缺少 Key 的提示處，按 **Enter** 鍵，程式會重新檢查 Key 是否可用。如果成功讀取，分析器將準備就緒。



---

閱讀完畢後，請按 Enter 返回之前的選項提示。

"""

    if CAN_DISPLAY_MD_HTML: display(Markdown(help_text))

    else: import re; plain_text = help_text.replace("## ", "").replace("* ", "- ").replace("`", "'").replace("->",""); plain_text = re.sub(r'\[(.*?)\]\(.*?\)', r'\1', plain_text); print(plain_text)



# --- 主要執行流程 ---

if can_proceed:

    exit_program_due_to_key = False



    # --- 啟動時檢查 API Key ---

    if IS_FRED_KEY_MISSING:

        error_md = "<font color='red'>**錯誤：缺少 FRED API Key。**</font>\n" + \

                   "<font color='red'>無法執行需要 FRED 數據的分析 (選項 2, 3, Q)。請先設定 API Key。\n</font>"

        if CAN_DISPLAY_MD_HTML: display(Markdown(error_md))

        else: print("錯誤：缺少 FRED API Key。無法執行分析。請先設定 API Key。")



        while IS_FRED_KEY_MISSING: # 保持在這個循環直到 Key 被找到或用戶退出

            retry_choice = input("\n輸入 Q 查看 API Key 設定說明 | 按 Enter 重試檢查 | 輸入 T 退出程式: ").strip().lower()



            if retry_choice == 'q':

                _display_api_key_help()

                input("按 Enter 返回檢查提示...")

                continue # 繼續 Key 缺失提示循環



            elif retry_choice == 't':

                print("\n用戶選擇退出程式。")

                exit_program_due_to_key = True

                break # 跳出 Key 缺失提示循環



            else: # 按 Enter 或其他鍵，重新檢查

                print("\n  正在重新檢查永久設定的 API Key (Secrets/環境變數)...")

                temp_key_recheck = None; api_key_source_retry = "未找到"; key_rechecked = False

                try:

                    # 重新讀取 Key

                    if IN_COLAB: temp_key_recheck = userdata.get('FRED_API_KEY')

                    if temp_key_recheck: api_key_source_retry = "Colab Secrets"

                    if not temp_key_recheck: temp_key_recheck = os.environ.get('FRED_API_KEY');

                    if temp_key_recheck: api_key_source_retry = "環境變數"

                    # 可選：如果 Cell 1 也會讀取 config，可以重新加載 config

                    # try:

                    #     globals()['ACTIVE_CONFIG'] = load_config(...) # 假設有加載配置的函數

                    #     if not temp_key_recheck:

                    #         key_from_cfg = get_nested_param(globals()['ACTIVE_CONFIG'], {}, ['general', 'fred_api_key'])

                    #         if key_from_cfg: temp_key_recheck = key_from_cfg; api_key_source_retry = "Config File"

                    # except Exception as e_reload: print(f"  ! 重新加載配置時出錯: {e_reload}")



                    key_rechecked = True

                except Exception as e_recheck: print(f"  ! 重新檢查時發生錯誤: {e_recheck}")



                if key_rechecked and temp_key_recheck:

                     print(f"  -> 成功從 {api_key_source_retry} 找到 API Key！分析器準備就緒。")

                     # 更新全局狀態

                     globals()['FRED_API_KEY'] = temp_key_recheck

                     globals()['FRED_API_KEY_MISSING'] = False

                     IS_FRED_KEY_MISSING = False # 更新循環條件

                     # 不需要 break，循環會因為 IS_FRED_KEY_MISSING 變為 False 而自然結束

                elif key_rechecked:

                     print("  -> 仍未找到設定的 API Key。請再次檢查設定並確保已重新運行 Cell 1 或重啟 Runtime。")

                     # 保持在循環中



    # --- 只有 Key 存在且用戶未退出時，才進入主分析選單循環 ---

    if not exit_program_due_to_key and not IS_FRED_KEY_MISSING:

        while True: # Level 0 - 主選單循環

            # 不需要再次檢查 IS_FRED_KEY_MISSING_CURRENT



            # --- 顯示菜單 ---

            print("\n" + "="*10 + " 分析選項選單 " + "="*10) # Level 1

            print("(提示：輸入 1-3 執行 | Q 除錯 | T 退出)") # Level 1

            print("-" * 36) # Level 1

            print("  1: 概覽 (顯示最新結果圖)") # Level 1

            print("  2: 進階分析 (Stage 1 + Stage 2)") # Level 1

            print("  3: 深度分析 (所有分析 + 圖表)") # Level 1

            print("-" * 36) # Level 1

            # 不再需要橙色警告



            try: # Level 1

                sys.stdout.flush(); choice = input("請輸入選項 (1概覽/2進階/3深度): ").strip().lower() # Level 2

            except EOFError: choice = 't' # Level 2

            if choice == 't': print("\n用戶選擇退出。"); break # Level 1



            # --- 處理選項 '1' (概覽) ---

            if choice == '1': # Level 1

                print("\n--- 正在準備概覽圖像 ---"); overview_displayed_final=False; png_generated_in_bg=False # Level 2

                try: # Level 2

                    config_temp = globals().get('ACTIVE_CONFIG', {}); ticker_temp = get_nested_param(config_temp, DEFAULT_CONFIG, ['general', 'target_ticker'], 'default'); safe_ticker_name_temp = ticker_temp.replace('^','').replace('/','_'); cache_dir_temp = globals().get('CACHE_DIR_CELL2', ".") # Level 3

                    stage2_png_filepath = os.path.join(cache_dir_temp, f"risk_dashboard_s2_{safe_ticker_name_temp}_EN.png"); systemic_png_filepath = os.path.join(cache_dir_temp, f"systemic_risk_plot_{safe_ticker_name_temp}.png") # Level 3

                    stage2_exists = os.path.exists(stage2_png_filepath); systemic_exists = os.path.exists(systemic_png_filepath) # Level 3

                    if not stage2_exists or not systemic_exists: # Level 3

                         print("\n  概覽圖像缺失，正在執行深度分析以生成最新圖像，請稍候...") # Level 4

                         config_for_overview_run = copy.deepcopy(config_temp); config_for_overview_run.setdefault('execution_control', {}) # Level 4

                         for k in ['generate_stage1_plots','generate_stage2_plots','generate_systemic_plots']: config_for_overview_run['execution_control'][k]=False # Level 4

                         config_for_overview_run.setdefault('plotting', {}).setdefault('stage2_png', {})['enable_option'] = True # Level 4

                         deep_run_func = globals().get('run_deep_analysis') # Level 4

                         if callable(deep_run_func): # Level 4

                              # 背景執行的深度分析會使用當前全局的 FRED_API_KEY (現在保證存在)

                              deep_results_overview = deep_run_func(config_for_overview_run) # Level 5

                              if isinstance(deep_results_overview, dict) and deep_results_overview.get('status') == 'Success': # Level 5

                                   print("  -> 深度分析完成，正在生成 Stage 2 PNG...") # Level 6

                                   png_export_func = globals().get('generate_combined_png_export') # Level 6

                                   if callable(png_export_func): # Level 6

                                        s2_results_for_png_overview = {}; source_dict_ov = deep_results_overview.get('advanced_analysis_results', {}) # Level 7

                                        if source_dict_ov: # Level 7

                                            for key_suffix in ['vs', 's', 'm', 'l']: # Level 8

                                                 res_key = f'{key_suffix}_results'; # Level 9

                                                 if res_key in source_dict_ov and source_dict_ov[res_key] is not None: expected_key = f'risk_{"sml_" if key_suffix in ["s", "m", "l"] else ""}{key_suffix}'; s2_results_for_png_overview[expected_key] = source_dict_ov[res_key] # Level 9

                                        if s2_results_for_png_overview: # Level 7

                                            try: png_path_overview = png_export_func(s2_results_for_png_overview, config_for_overview_run); print(f"    - Stage 2 PNG 生成成功: {png_path_overview}" if png_path_overview else "    ! Stage 2 PNG 生成失敗。"); png_generated_in_bg=bool(png_path_overview) # Level 8

                                            except Exception as e_png_ov: print(f"    ! 生成 Stage 2 PNG 時發生錯誤: {e_png_ov}") # Level 8

                                        else: print("    ! 未找到 Stage 2 結果，無法生成 PNG。") # Level 7

                                   else: print("    ! 未找到 PNG 生成函數 'generate_combined_png_export'。") # Level 6

                              else: print("  ! 深度分析在背景執行失敗，無法生成圖像。"); error_from_deep = deep_results_overview.get('error', '未知錯誤') if isinstance(deep_results_overview, dict) else '未知錯誤'; display(Markdown(f"<font color='red'>  背景分析錯誤: {error_from_deep}</font>")) # Level 5

                         else: print("  ! 錯誤：找不到深度分析函數 'run_deep_analysis'。") # Level 4

                    print("\n--- 顯示概覽圖像 ---"); # Level 3

                    print(f"\n正在查找 Stage 2 合併圖: {stage2_png_filepath}") # Level 3

                    if os.path.exists(stage2_png_filepath): # Level 3

                         if CAN_DISPLAY_IMAGE: print("  -> 找到並顯示 Stage 2 合併圖:"); display(Image(filename=stage2_png_filepath)); overview_displayed_final = True # Level 4

                         else: print("  -> 找到 Stage 2 合併圖，但環境不支持顯示。") # Level 4

                    elif png_generated_in_bg: print("  -> 警告：背景生成 PNG 後仍無法找到文件，請檢查路徑或權限。") # Level 3

                    else: print("  -> 執行後仍未找到 Stage 2 合併圖。") # Level 3

                    print(f"\n正在查找系統性風險圖: {systemic_png_filepath}") # Level 3

                    if os.path.exists(systemic_png_filepath): # Level 3

                         if CAN_DISPLAY_IMAGE: print("  -> 找到並顯示系統性風險圖:"); display(Image(filename=systemic_png_filepath)); overview_displayed_final = True # Level 4

                         else: print("  -> 找到系統性風險圖，但環境不支持顯示。") # Level 4

                    else: print("  -> 執行後仍未找到系統性風險圖。") # Level 3

                    if not overview_displayed_final: print("\n未能成功顯示任何概覽圖像。") # Level 3

                    input("\n按 Enter 鍵返回主選單...") # Level 3

                except Exception as e_overview: print(f"\n! 處理概覽選項時發生錯誤: {e_overview}"); traceback.print_exc(limit=1); input("\n發生錯誤，按 Enter 鍵返回主選單...") # Level 2

                continue # Level 2



            # --- 處理選項 2, 3, Q ---

            elif choice in analysis_map: # Level 1

                # *** Key 已在循環開始前確認存在，直接執行分析 ***

                selected_analysis = analysis_map[choice]; run_func_name = selected_analysis['func_name']; level_name_temp = selected_analysis['level_name'] # Level 2

                is_debug_run = selected_analysis.get('is_debug', False); needs_png_option = selected_analysis.get('needs_png', False) # Level 2

                run_func = globals().get(run_func_name); config_to_use = globals().get('ACTIVE_CONFIG') # Level 2

                if not callable(run_func) or config_to_use is None: display(Markdown(f"<font color='red'>**內部錯誤...**</font>")); time.sleep(1); continue # Level 2

                start_run_time = time.time(); analysis_success = False; current_run_result = None # Level 2

                try: # Level 2

                     print(f"\n--- 正在執行 '{level_name_temp}' 分析，請稍候 ---"); config_for_run = copy.deepcopy(config_to_use) # Level 3

                     if is_debug_run: print("  *** 除錯模式：禁用繪圖輸出 ***"); config_for_run.setdefault('execution_control', {}); # Level 3

                     for k in ['generate_stage1_plots','generate_stage2_plots','generate_stage2_png','generate_systemic_plots']: config_for_run['execution_control'][k]=False # Level 3

                     result_temp = run_func(config_for_run); end_run_time = time.time(); current_run_result = result_temp; last_run_result = current_run_result; last_run_level_name = level_name_temp # Level 3

                     if isinstance(current_run_result, dict): # Level 3

                         current_run_result['duration'] = end_run_time - start_run_time # Level 4

                         if is_debug_run: current_run_result['debug_mode'] = True # Level 4

                         if current_run_result.get('status') == 'Success': analysis_success = True # Level 4

                     print(f"\n[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] '{level_name_temp}' 分析執行完畢。\n(耗時: {end_run_time - start_run_time:.2f} 秒)") # Level 3

                except Exception as e_run: # Level 2

                     end_run_time = time.time(); print(f"\n[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] '{level_name_temp}' 分析執行時發生錯誤: {e_run}"); level_name_temp = f"{level_name_temp} (失敗)" # Level 3

                     current_run_result = {"status": "Failed", "error": str(e_run), "duration": end_run_time - start_run_time}; analysis_success = False # Level 3

                     if is_debug_run: current_run_result['debug_mode'] = True # Level 3

                     last_run_result = current_run_result; last_run_level_name = level_name_temp # Level 3

                print("\n--- 分析結果摘要 ---"); display_summary_func = globals().get('display_run_summary') # Level 2

                if callable(display_summary_func): # Level 2

                    try: display_summary_func(current_run_result, level_name_temp) # Level 3

                    except Exception as e_display: print(f"! 調用 display_run_summary 時發生錯誤: {e_display}"); print("備用打印:"); pprint.pprint(current_run_result, indent=2, depth=4) # Level 3

                else: print("! 錯誤: 找不到 display_run_summary 函數"); pprint.pprint(current_run_result, indent=2, depth=4) # Level 2

                print("-" * 20) # Level 2



                # --- PNG 導出選項 ---

                should_show_png_prompt = analysis_success and not is_debug_run and needs_png_option # Level 2

                if should_show_png_prompt: # Level 2

                    png_generated_or_skipped = False # Level 3

                    try: # Level 3

                        sys.stdout.flush(); action_choice = input("\n分析完成。是否合併輸出 Stage 2 圖像 (PNG)? (輸入 Y/y 合成, 按 Enter 或其他鍵返回選單): ").strip().lower() # Level 4

                        if action_choice == 'y': # Level 4

                            print("\n--- 正在嘗試合成 Stage 2 圖像 (PNG)... ---"); png_export_func = globals().get('generate_combined_png_export') # Level 5

                            if callable(png_export_func): # Level 5

                                s2_results_for_png = {}; source_dict = current_run_result # Level 6

                                if 'advanced_analysis_results' in current_run_result and isinstance(current_run_result.get('advanced_analysis_results'), dict): source_dict = current_run_result['advanced_analysis_results'] # Level 6

                                for key_suffix in ['vs', 's', 'm', 'l']: # Level 6

                                     res_key = f'{key_suffix}_results' # Level 7

                                     if res_key in source_dict and source_dict[res_key] is not None: expected_key = f'risk_{"sml_" if key_suffix in ["s", "m", "l"] else ""}{key_suffix}'; s2_results_for_png[expected_key] = source_dict[res_key] # Level 7

                                if s2_results_for_png: # Level 6

                                    try: # Level 7

                                        png_path = png_export_func(s2_results_for_png, ACTIVE_CONFIG) # Level 8

                                        if png_path: # Level 8

                                            print(f"    - PNG 圖像合成成功: {png_path}") # Level 9

                                            ImageClass = globals().get('Image') # Level 9

                                            if CAN_DISPLAY_IMAGE and ImageClass and os.path.exists(png_path): print("\n--- 合成圖像預覽 ---"); display(ImageClass(filename=png_path)) # Level 9

                                            elif not CAN_DISPLAY_IMAGE: print("! (環境不支持直接顯示圖像，請在文件路徑查看)") # Level 9

                                            elif not os.path.exists(png_path): print(f"! (警告：聲稱已保存，但在路徑 '{png_path}' 未找到文件)") # Level 9

                                        else: print("    ! PNG 圖像合成失敗 (未返回有效路徑)。") # Level 8

                                    except Exception as e_png: print(f"    ! 執行圖像合成時發生錯誤: {e_png}") # Level 7

                                else: # Level 6

                                    print("    ! 沒有找到足夠的 Stage 2 結果來合成圖像。")

                            else: # Level 5

                                display(Markdown("<font color='orange'>**警告：** PNG 合成函數 'generate_combined_png_export' 未找到。</font>"))

                            png_generated_or_skipped = True # Level 5

                        else: # Level 4

                             png_generated_or_skipped = True # Level 5



                        # *** 再次確認 input 的縮排 ***

                        if png_generated_or_skipped: # Level 3

                             input("\n按 Enter 鍵返回主選單...") # Level 4



                    except EOFError: # Level 3

                        break

                    except Exception as e_png_prompt: # Level 3

                        print(f"! 處理 PNG 選項時發生錯誤: {e_png_prompt}"); input("\n發生錯誤，按 Enter 鍵返回主選單...") # Level 4



                # --- 分析失敗或不需 PNG 提示時的處理 ---

                if not should_show_png_prompt: # Level 2

                     if not analysis_success: input("\n分析執行失敗。按 Enter 鍵返回主選單...") # Level 3

                     else: print("\n返回主選單..."); time.sleep(1.0) # Level 3

                 #<-- 結束分析邏輯 (Level 1)



            # --- 處理無效輸入 ---

            else: # Level 1

                display(Markdown(f"<font color='orange'>**輸入錯誤：** 請輸入 1, 2, 3, Q 或 T。</font>")) # Level 2

                time.sleep(1) # Level 2

                continue # Level 2



    # --- 如果因為 Key 缺失且用戶選擇退出 ---

    if exit_program_due_to_key: # Level 1

         print(f"\n[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] Cell 31: Execution halted due to missing API Key.") # Level 2



# --- 循環結束後的提示 ---

print(f"\n[{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}] Cell 31: Combined Trigger & Viewer Loop Finished.") # Level 0

# --- 全局變數 ---

if 'last_run_result' not in globals(): last_run_result = None

analysis_map = {

    '2': {'func_name': 'run_advanced_analysis', 'level_name': '進階分析', 'needs_config': True, 'needs_png': True},

    '3': {'func_name': 'run_deep_analysis', 'level_name': '深度分析', 'needs_config': True, 'needs_png': True},
